```python
"""Studio API, slice S0 (spec §7 O1–O4, O21; И10)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import time

from _studio_fixtures import LiveServer, make_client, read_sse

from h3_48gb.studio.app import create_app


def test_create_then_list_then_snapshot(tmp_path):
    client = make_client(tmp_path)
    r = client.post("/api/studio/projects",
                    json={"title": "Лес", "frame_format": "16:9", "idea_text": "девушка у озера"})
    assert r.status_code == 201
    created = r.json()
    assert created == {"id": created["id"], "revision": 1, "title": "Лес", "frame_format": "16:9",
                       "idea_text": "девушка у озера", "style_block": "",
                       "created_at": created["created_at"]}
    assert client.get("/api/studio/projects").json() == [
        {**created, "scenes_total": 0, "scenes_done": 0}]
    assert client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{created['id']}").json() == {
        "project": created, "scenes": [], "cast": [], "takes": [], "tasks": [], "last_seq": 1}


def test_projects_survive_an_app_restart(tmp_path):
    first = make_client(tmp_path)
    pid = first.post("/api/studio/projects", json={"title": "Лес", "frame_format": "9:16"}).json()["id"]
    second = make_client(tmp_path)
    assert [p["id"] for p in second.get("/api/studio/projects").json()] == [pid]


def test_validation_error_is_the_envelope_with_400(tmp_path):
    r = make_client(tmp_path).post("/api/studio/projects",
                                   json={"title": "Лес", "frame_format": "4:3"})
    assert r.status_code == 400
    body = r.json()
    assert body["error"]["code"] == "args_invalid"
    assert body["error"]["message"] == "Неверные данные запроса"
    assert [e["loc"] for e in body["error"]["detail"]["errors"]] == [["body", "frame_format"]]


def test_missing_project_is_404_before_the_body_is_read(tmp_path):
    r = make_client(tmp_path).patch("/api/studio/projects/p_nope", json={"garbage": True})
    assert r.status_code == 404
    assert r.json() == {"error": {"code": "project_not_found", "message": "Проект не найден",
                                  "detail": {"project_id": "p_nope"}}}


def test_patch_with_a_stale_revision_is_409_with_the_current_project(tmp_path):
    client = make_client(tmp_path)
    p = client.post("/api/studio/projects", json={"title": "Лес", "frame_format": "16:9"}).json()
    ok = client.patch(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}", json={"expected_revision": 1,
                                                               "title": "Лес-2"})
    assert (ok.status_code, ok.json()["revision"], ok.json()["title"]) == (200, 2, "Лес-2")
    stale = client.patch(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}", json={"expected_revision": 1,
                                                                  "title": "Лес-3"})
    assert stale.status_code == 409
    assert stale.json()["error"]["code"] == "revision_conflict"
    assert stale.json()["error"]["detail"]["current"] == ok.json()


def test_frame_format_is_locked_after_a_done_take(tmp_path):
    client = make_client(tmp_path)
    p = client.post("/api/studio/projects", json={"title": "Лес", "frame_format": "16:9"}).json()
    import sqlite3
    conn = sqlite3.connect(tmp_path / "out" / "studio" / "studio.sqlite")
    conn.execute("INSERT INTO scene (id, project_id, idx, mode, requested_s, frames, seed, "
                 "created_at) VALUES ('s', ?, 0, 'refs', 8.0, 192, 1, 'n')", (p["id"],))
    conn.execute("INSERT INTO take (id, scene_id, scene_revision, tier, executor, template, "
                 "status, created_at) VALUES ('t', 's', 1, 'sketch', 'comfy', 'h3-fl2va', 'done', "
                 "'n')")
    conn.commit()
    r = client.patch(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}", json={"expected_revision": 1,
                                                              "frame_format": "9:16"})
    assert r.status_code == 409
    assert r.json()["error"] == {"code": "format_locked",
                                 "message": "Формат кадра нельзя менять после первого готового дубля",
                                 "detail": {"done_takes": 1}}


def test_snapshot_last_seq_resumes_the_stream_exactly(tmp_path):
    """И10: subscribing with Last-Event-ID = last_seq yields the next event and nothing older."""
    app = create_app(tmp_path / "out")
    with LiveServer(app) as live:
        from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
        client = TestClient(app, base_url="http://127.0.0.1:8766")
        p = client.post("/api/studio/projects", json={"title": "Лес", "frame_format": "16:9"}).json()
        client.patch(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}", json={"expected_revision": 1, "title": "Б"})
        last = client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()["last_seq"]
        assert last == 2
        replay = read_sse(live.port, "/api/studio/events", headers={"Last-Event-ID": "1"},
                          count=1, timeout=2)
        assert [(e["id"], e["event"], e["data"]["payload"]) for e in replay] == [
            (2, "project_updated", {"title": "Б"})]
        assert read_sse(live.port, "/api/studio/events", headers={"Last-Event-ID": str(last)},
                        count=1, timeout=1) == []
        client.post("/api/studio/projects", json={"title": "В", "frame_format": "9:16"})
        nxt = read_sse(live.port, f"/api/studio/events?after={last}", count=1, timeout=2)
        assert [(e["id"], e["event"]) for e in nxt] == [(3, "project_created")]


def test_master_is_served_from_the_built_dist(tmp_path):
    dist = tmp_path / "dist"
    (dist / "assets").mkdir(parents=True)
    (dist / "index.html").write_text("<!doctype html><title>Студия</title>")
    (dist / "assets" / "app.js").write_text("console.log(1)")
    client = make_client(tmp_path, web_dist=dist)
    assert client.get("/studio/").text == "<!doctype html><title>Студия</title>"
    assert client.get("/studio/p/p_123").text == "<!doctype html><title>Студия</title>"
    assert client.get("/studio/assets/app.js").text == "console.log(1)"
    assert client.get("/studio/..%2F..%2Fetc%2Fpasswd").text == "<!doctype html><title>Студия</title>"


def test_master_not_built_is_a_named_503(tmp_path):
    r = make_client(tmp_path, web_dist=tmp_path / "nothing").get("/studio/")
    assert r.status_code == 503
    assert r.json()["error"]["code"] == "web_not_built"


def test_a_body_over_the_limit_is_413_before_anything_is_written(tmp_path):
    """Junk guard: a size limit on the request, not a cap on scenes (§4.4)."""
    client = make_client(tmp_path)
    junk = b'{"title": "' + b"x" * (2 * 1024 * 1024) + b'", "frame_format": "16:9"}'
    r = client.post("/api/studio/projects", content=junk,
                    headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    assert r.status_code == 413
    assert r.json() == {"error": {"code": "body_too_large", "message": "Запрос больше 2 МБ",
                                  "detail": {"max_bytes": 2 * 1024 * 1024}}}
    assert client.get("/api/studio/projects").json() == []


def test_a_chunked_body_over_the_limit_is_413_too(tmp_path):
    client = make_client(tmp_path)

    def chunks():
        yield b'{"title": "'
        for _ in range(3):
            yield b"x" * (1024 * 1024)
        yield b'", "frame_format": "16:9"}'

    r = client.post("/api/studio/projects", content=chunks(),
                    headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"]) == (413, "body_too_large")


def test_snapshot_and_its_last_seq_come_from_one_read(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """И10 under a concurrent write: a change committed after the project row was read must not
    make `last_seq` claim its event — the client would then never see that change."""
    from h3_48gb.studio import db, events, ops
    client = make_client(tmp_path)
    p = client.post("/api/studio/projects", json={"title": "Лес", "frame_format": "16:9"}).json()
    real_last_seq = events.last_seq

    def write_in_between(conn):
        monkeypatch.setattr(events, "last_seq", real_last_seq)
        other = db.connect(tmp_path / "out" / "studio" / "studio.sqlite")
        try:
            ops.update_project(other, p["id"], expected_revision=1, title="Б")
        finally:
            other.close()
        return real_last_seq(conn)

    monkeypatch.setattr(events, "last_seq", write_in_between)
    snap = client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()
    assert (snap["project"]["title"], snap["project"]["revision"], snap["last_seq"]) == ("Лес", 1, 1)
    snap = client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()
    assert (snap["project"]["title"], snap["project"]["revision"], snap["last_seq"]) == ("Б", 2, 2)


def test_an_sse_read_in_flight_keeps_its_connection_until_it_returns(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """С-1, deterministically: the client goes away while a journal read is running in a worker
    thread. The connection that read uses may close only after the read has returned — closing
    it under a running query is what crashed sqlite3 in the image."""
    import socket
    import threading

    from h3_48gb.studio import db as studio_db
    from h3_48gb.studio import events

    app = create_app(tmp_path / "out")
    log: list[str] = []
    started, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()
    real_connect = studio_db.connect

    class Tracked:
        def __init__(self, conn):
            self._conn = conn

        def __getattr__(self, name):
            return getattr(self._conn, name)

        def close(self):
            log.append("close")
            self._conn.close()

    def slow_read(conn, after, limit=100):
        log.append("read_start")
        started.set()
        release.wait(5)
        log.append("read_end")
        return []

    monkeypatch.setattr(studio_db, "connect", lambda path: Tracked(real_connect(path)))
    monkeypatch.setattr(events, "read_after", slow_read)
    with LiveServer(app) as live:
        sock = socket.create_connection(("127.0.0.1", live.port))
        sock.sendall(b"GET /api/studio/events HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1\r\n\r\n")
        assert started.wait(5)
        sock.close()                              # the tab goes away in the middle of the read
        time.sleep(0.5)                           # the server notices and cancels the stream
        assert log == ["read_start"]              # nothing closed under the running read
        release.set()
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 5
        while "close" not in log and time.monotonic() < deadline:
            time.sleep(0.02)
    assert log[:3] == ["read_start", "read_end", "close"]
```

Create `tests/test_studio_security.py` (маршруты S1 в нём — guard отвечает 403 до маршрутизации, поэтому тест верен уже в S0):

```python
"""The studio on the LAN without a login (ruling R26): Host allow-list against DNS rebinding,
same-origin guard on every changing request."""
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from h3_48gb.studio.app import allowed_hosts_from_env, create_app

HOSTS = ["alex-neuro", "192.168.100.50", "localhost", "127.0.0.1"]


@pytest.fixture
def app(tmp_path):
    return create_app(tmp_path / "out", allowed_hosts=HOSTS)


def _client(app, host: str) -> TestClient:
    return TestClient(app, base_url=f"http://{host}")


@pytest.mark.parametrize("host", ["alex-neuro:8766", "192.168.100.50:8766", "localhost:8766",
                                  "127.0.0.1:8766"])
def test_the_studio_answers_its_own_names(app, host):
    assert _client(app, host).get("/api/studio/health").status_code == 200


@pytest.mark.parametrize("host", ["evil.example", "evil.example:8766", "192.168.100.51:8766"])
def test_a_foreign_host_is_400_dns_rebinding(app, host):
    client = _client(app, host)
    r = client.get("/api/studio/projects")
    assert (r.status_code, r.text) == (400, "Invalid host header")
    r = client.post("/api/studio/projects", json={"title": "x", "frame_format": "16:9"})
    assert r.status_code == 400
    assert _client(app, "127.0.0.1:8766").get("/api/studio/projects").json() == []


CROSS = {"Origin": "http://evil.example"}


@pytest.mark.parametrize("method, path, kwargs", [
    ("POST", "/api/studio/projects", {"json": {"title": "x", "frame_format": "16:9"}}),
    ("PATCH", "/api/studio/projects/p_x", {"json": {"expected_revision": 1}}),
    ("PUT", "/api/studio/projects/p_x/scenes", {"json": {"expected_revision": 1, "scenes": []}}),
    ("POST", "/api/studio/assets", {"files": {"file": ("a.png", b"x")}}),
    ("DELETE", "/api/studio/projects/p_x", {}),
])
def test_a_changing_request_from_another_site_is_403(app, method, path, kwargs):
    r = _client(app, "alex-neuro:8766").request(method, path, headers=CROSS, **kwargs)
    assert r.status_code == 403
    assert r.json() == {"error": {"code": "cross_origin",
                                  "message": "Запрос с чужого сайта отклонён",
                                  "detail": {"origin": "http://evil.example",
                                             "sec_fetch_site": None}}}


def test_sec_fetch_site_cross_site_without_origin_is_403(app):
    r = _client(app, "alex-neuro:8766").post(
        "/api/studio/projects", json={"title": "x", "frame_format": "16:9"},
        headers={"Sec-Fetch-Site": "cross-site"})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"]) == (403, "cross_origin")
    assert _client(app, "alex-neuro:8766").get("/api/studio/projects").json() == []


@pytest.mark.parametrize("headers", [
    {},                                                           # curl: no Origin at all
    {"Origin": "http://alex-neuro:8766", "Sec-Fetch-Site": "same-origin"},
    {"Origin": "http://alex-neuro:8766"},
    {"Sec-Fetch-Site": "none"},
])
def test_same_origin_and_no_origin_are_allowed(app, headers):
    r = _client(app, "alex-neuro:8766").post(
        "/api/studio/projects", json={"title": "x", "frame_format": "16:9"}, headers=headers)
    assert r.status_code == 201


def test_an_origin_with_another_port_is_another_site(app):
    r = _client(app, "alex-neuro:8766").post(
        "/api/studio/projects", json={"title": "x", "frame_format": "16:9"},
        headers={"Origin": "http://alex-neuro:8765"})       # the old panel's page
    assert r.status_code == 403


def test_reading_is_not_guarded(app):
    r = _client(app, "alex-neuro:8766").get("/api/studio/projects", headers=CROSS)
    assert r.status_code == 200


def test_allowed_hosts_come_from_the_defaults_and_the_environment():
    assert allowed_hosts_from_env({}) == ["alex-neuro", "localhost", "127.0.0.1"]
    assert allowed_hosts_from_env({"STUDIO_ALLOWED_HOSTS": "192.168.100.50, alex-neuro"}) == [
        "alex-neuro", "localhost", "127.0.0.1", "192.168.100.50"]


def _post_with(app, headers):
    return _client(app, "alex-neuro:8766").post(
        "/api/studio/projects", json={"title": "x", "frame_format": "16:9"}, headers=headers)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("origin", [
    "https://alex-neuro:8766",       # same host and port, another scheme (review r3)
    "//alex-neuro:8766",             # relative: no scheme at all
    "null",                          # sandboxed iframe, file://
    "http://[",                      # malformed: used to raise ValueError -> 500
    "http://alex-neuro:99999",       # port out of range
    "http://alex-neuro:8766/path",   # not a serialized origin
    "http://user@alex-neuro:8766",   # credentials
    "ftp://alex-neuro:8766",
    "ftp://alex-neuro",              # unknown scheme without a port: no default port to look up
    "http://alex-neuro:0",           # an explicit port 0 is not the default port (review r4)
])
def test_a_bad_or_foreign_origin_is_403_never_500(app, origin):
    r = _post_with(app, {"Origin": origin})
    assert r.status_code == 403
    assert r.json()["error"] == {"code": "cross_origin", "message": "Запрос с чужого сайта отклонён",
                                 "detail": {"origin": origin, "sec_fetch_site": None}}


@pytest.mark.parametrize("fetch_site", ["same-site", "cross-site", "anything"])
def test_only_same_origin_and_none_pass_sec_fetch_site(app, fetch_site):
    r = _post_with(app, {"Sec-Fetch-Site": fetch_site, "Origin": "http://alex-neuro:8766"})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["detail"]["sec_fetch_site"]) == (403, fetch_site)


def test_the_default_port_is_part_of_the_origin(app):
    """http://localhost and http://localhost:80 are one origin; :8766 is another."""
    client = _client(app, "localhost")
    ok = client.post("/api/studio/projects", json={"title": "x", "frame_format": "16:9"},
                     headers={"Origin": "http://localhost:80"})
    no = client.post("/api/studio/projects", json={"title": "x", "frame_format": "16:9"},
                     headers={"Origin": "http://localhost:8766"})
    zero = client.post("/api/studio/projects", json={"title": "x", "frame_format": "16:9"},
                       headers={"Origin": "http://localhost:0"})      # port 0 is not port 80
    assert (ok.status_code, no.status_code, zero.status_code) == (201, 403, 403)
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/schemas.py` (версия S0):

```python
"""Request bodies of the studio API (spec §7). Responses are plain dicts built by `ops`; the
master's types come from these models through OpenAPI (`web/openapi.json`)."""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field

FrameFormat = Literal["16:9", "9:16"]


class _Body(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")


class ProjectCreate(_Body):
    title: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200)
    frame_format: FrameFormat
    idea_text: str = Field(default="", max_length=20000)


class ProjectPatch(_Body):
    expected_revision: int
    title: str | None = Field(default=None, min_length=1, max_length=200)
    frame_format: FrameFormat | None = None
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/app.py` (версия S0; задачи 9–11 дописывают маршруты перед `@app.get(f"{api}/events")`):

```python
"""The studio API (spec §4.2 `studio-api`, §7): FastAPI over `ops`, the SSE journal (O21) and the
built master under `/studio`."""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import contextlib
import json
import os
import urllib.parse
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Header, Request
from fastapi.exceptions import RequestValidationError
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse, JSONResponse, RedirectResponse, StreamingResponse
from starlette.exceptions import HTTPException as StarletteHTTPException
from starlette.middleware.trustedhost import TrustedHostMiddleware

from h3_48gb.studio import config, db, events, ops, schemas
from h3_48gb.studio.errors import StudioError

WEB_DIST = Path(__file__).parent / "web" / "dist"
#: Request body limits — the guard against junk instead of a cap on the number of scenes (a project
#: has no upper bound, §4.4). 2 MiB holds thousands of scene cards; uploads get the asset limit.
MAX_BODY_BYTES = 2 * 1024 * 1024
UPLOAD_PATH = "/api/studio/assets"
MAX_UPLOAD_BODY_BYTES = 26 * 1024 * 1024
#: The studio listens on the LAN without a login (one user, home network — plan ruling R26).
#: Host names it answers to; anything else is a DNS-rebinding attempt and gets 400. The server's
#: LAN IP comes from STUDIO_ALLOWED_HOSTS (compose sets it).
DEFAULT_ALLOWED_HOSTS = ("alex-neuro", "localhost", "127.0.0.1")
UNSAFE_METHODS = frozenset({"POST", "PUT", "PATCH", "DELETE"})


def allowed_hosts_from_env(environ=None) -> list[str]:
    raw = (os.environ if environ is None else environ).get("STUDIO_ALLOWED_HOSTS", "")
    extra = [h.strip() for h in raw.split(",") if h.strip()]
    return list(dict.fromkeys([*DEFAULT_ALLOWED_HOSTS, *extra]))


_DEFAULT_PORTS = {"http": 80, "https": 443}


def parse_origin(value: str) -> tuple[str, str, int] | None:
    """(scheme, host, port) of a serialized origin, or None when it is not one: `null`, a
    relative `//host`, a path, a query, credentials, a bad port, an unclosed `[` — anything the
    browser would never send as an Origin of a real page."""
    try:
        parts = urllib.parse.urlsplit(value)
        port = parts.port
    except ValueError:
        return None
    if parts.scheme not in _DEFAULT_PORTS or not parts.hostname:
        return None
    if parts.path or parts.query or parts.fragment or parts.username or parts.password:
        return None
    return parts.scheme, parts.hostname.lower(), port if port is not None else _DEFAULT_PORTS[parts.scheme]


def request_origin(scheme: str, host_header: str) -> tuple[str, str, int] | None:
    return parse_origin(f"{scheme}://{host_header}")


class SameOriginGuard:
    """ASGI middleware: a changing request (POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE) from another site gets 403.
    Allowed: no Origin and no Sec-Fetch-Site (curl, scripts); Sec-Fetch-Site only `same-origin`
    or `none`; an Origin whose (scheme, host, port) equals the request's own. Everything else —
    another scheme or port, `same-site`, `null`, a malformed Origin — is refused with 403, never
    a 500."""

    def __init__(self, app):
        self.app = app

    async def __call__(self, scope, receive, send):
        if scope["type"] == "http" and scope["method"] in UNSAFE_METHODS:
            headers = {k.decode("latin-1").lower(): v.decode("latin-1")
                       for k, v in scope["headers"]}
            fetch_site = headers.get("sec-fetch-site")
            origin = headers.get("origin")
            refused = fetch_site is not None and fetch_site not in ("same-origin", "none")
            if origin is not None and not refused:
                mine = request_origin(scope.get("scheme", "http"), headers.get("host", ""))
                theirs = parse_origin(origin)
                refused = theirs is None or mine is None or theirs != mine
            if refused:
                body = StudioError(403, "cross_origin", "Запрос с чужого сайта отклонён",
                                   {"origin": origin, "sec_fetch_site": fetch_site}).body()
                payload = json.dumps(body, ensure_ascii=False).encode()
                await send({"type": "http.response.start", "status": 403,
                            "headers": [(b"content-type", b"application/json"),
                                        (b"content-length", str(len(payload)).encode())]})
                await send({"type": "http.response.body", "body": payload})
                return
        await self.app(scope, receive, send)


class BodyLimit:
    """ASGI middleware: 413 `body_too_large` when the declared or the actually received body is
    over the limit for the path. The body is read (up to the limit) before the app sees it, so a
    chunked body with no length is counted too, and FastAPI's own body parsing cannot swallow
    the refusal into a 400."""

    def __init__(self, app, *, default: int, upload: int, upload_path: str):
        self.app, self.default, self.upload, self.upload_path = app, default, upload, upload_path

    async def __call__(self, scope, receive, send):
        if scope["type"] != "http":
            return await self.app(scope, receive, send)
        limit = self.upload if scope["path"] == self.upload_path else self.default
        declared = dict(scope["headers"]).get(b"content-length")
        if declared is not None and declared.isdigit() and int(declared) > limit:
            return await self._refuse(send, limit)
        chunks, total = [], 0
        while True:
            message = await receive()
            if message["type"] != "http.request":
                return                                  # the client went away
            total += len(message.get("body", b""))
            if total > limit:
                return await self._refuse(send, limit)
            chunks.append(message.get("body", b""))
            if not message.get("more_body"):
                break
        replay = {"type": "http.request", "body": b"".join(chunks), "more_body": False}
        replayed = False

        async def receive_again():
            nonlocal replayed
            if not replayed:
                replayed = True
                return replay
            return await receive()                      # later calls: disconnect (SSE)

        await self.app(scope, receive_again, send)

    @staticmethod
    async def _refuse(send, limit: int):
        body = StudioError(413, "body_too_large", f"Запрос больше {limit // (1024 * 1024)} МБ",
                           {"max_bytes": limit}).body()
        payload = json.dumps(body, ensure_ascii=False).encode()
        await send({"type": "http.response.start", "status": 413,
                    "headers": [(b"content-type", b"application/json"),
                                (b"content-length", str(len(payload)).encode())]})
        await send({"type": "http.response.body", "body": payload})


def create_app(outdir: Path, *, web_dist: Path = WEB_DIST,
               allowed_hosts: list[str] | None = None) -> FastAPI:
    home = config.studio_dir(outdir)
    home.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    db_path = home / "studio.sqlite"
    boot = db.connect(db_path)
    try:
        db.migrate(boot)
    finally:
        boot.close()

    @contextlib.asynccontextmanager
    async def lifespan(_app: FastAPI):
        async def backups():
            while True:
                await asyncio.sleep(BACKUP_EVERY_S)
                await asyncio.to_thread(db.backup_once, db_path, home / "backups",
                                        now=datetime.now())
        task = asyncio.create_task(backups())
        try:
            yield
        finally:
            task.cancel()

    app = FastAPI(title="h3 studio", version="0.1.0", lifespan=lifespan)
    # Added last = runs first: Host check, then the same-origin guard, then the body limit.
    app.add_middleware(BodyLimit, default=MAX_BODY_BYTES, upload=MAX_UPLOAD_BODY_BYTES,
                       upload_path=UPLOAD_PATH)
    app.add_middleware(SameOriginGuard)
    app.add_middleware(TrustedHostMiddleware,
                       allowed_hosts=allowed_hosts if allowed_hosts is not None
                       else allowed_hosts_from_env())
    app.state.outdir = outdir
    app.state.db_path = db_path

    @app.exception_handler(StudioError)
    async def _studio_error(_request: Request, exc: StudioError):
        return JSONResponse(exc.body(), status_code=exc.status)

    @app.exception_handler(RequestValidationError)
    async def _invalid(_request: Request, exc: RequestValidationError):
        errors = [{"loc": list(e["loc"]), "msg": e["msg"]} for e in exc.errors()]
        return JSONResponse({"error": {"code": "args_invalid", "message": "Неверные данные запроса",
                                       "detail": {"errors": errors}}}, status_code=400)

    @app.exception_handler(StarletteHTTPException)
    async def _http(_request: Request, exc: StarletteHTTPException):
        code = "not_found" if exc.status_code == 404 else "http_error"
        return JSONResponse({"error": {"code": code, "message": str(exc.detail), "detail": {}}},
                            status_code=exc.status_code)

    def get_conn():
        conn = db.connect(db_path)
        try:
            yield conn
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def project_exists(project_id: str, conn=Depends(get_conn)) -> str:
        # A dependency, so a missing project answers 404 before the body is validated (§7).
        ops.require_project(conn, project_id)
        return project_id

    api = "/api/studio"

    @app.get(f"{api}/health")
    def health(conn=Depends(get_conn)):
        return {"ok": True, "schema_version": conn.execute("PRAGMA user_version").fetchone()[0]}

    @app.get(f"{api}/projects")
    def list_projects(conn=Depends(get_conn)):
        return ops.list_projects(conn)

    @app.post(f"{api}/projects", status_code=201)
    def create_project(body: schemas.ProjectCreate, conn=Depends(get_conn)):
        return ops.create_project(conn, title=body.title, frame_format=body.frame_format,
                                  idea_text=body.idea_text)

    @app.get(f"{api}/projects/{{project_id}}")
    def get_project(project_id: str = Depends(project_exists), conn=Depends(get_conn)):
        return ops.snapshot(conn, project_id)

    @app.patch(f"{api}/projects/{{project_id}}")
    def patch_project(body: schemas.ProjectPatch, project_id: str = Depends(project_exists),
                      conn=Depends(get_conn)):
        return ops.update_project(conn, project_id, expected_revision=body.expected_revision,
                                  title=body.title, frame_format=body.frame_format)

    @app.get(f"{api}/events")
    async def stream_events(request: Request, after: int = 0,
                            last_event_id: int | None = Header(default=None)):
        start = max(after, last_event_id or 0)

        def read_batch(after_seq: int) -> list[dict]:
            # A connection per read, opened and closed inside the worker thread. A connection
            # shared across reads was closed by the generator's cleanup while a cancelled read
            # still ran in its thread — sqlite3 segfaulted (seen in the Docker image, 1 run in 8).
            conn = db.connect(db_path)
            try:
                return events.read_after(conn, after_seq)
            finally:
                conn.close()

        async def frames():
            seen = start
            idle = 0.0
            while not await request.is_disconnected():
                batch = await asyncio.to_thread(read_batch, seen)
                for event in batch:
                    seen = event["seq"]
                    yield events.sse_frame(event)
                if batch:
                    idle = 0.0
                    continue
                await asyncio.sleep(SSE_POLL_S)
                idle += SSE_POLL_S
                if idle >= SSE_HEARTBEAT_S:
                    idle = 0.0
                    yield ": ping\n\n"

        return StreamingResponse(frames(), media_type="text/event-stream",
                                 headers={"Cache-Control": "no-store", "X-Accel-Buffering": "no"})

    @app.get("/")
    def root():
        return RedirectResponse("/studio/")

    @app.get("/studio")
    @app.get("/studio/{path:path}")
    def master(path: str = ""):
        index = web_dist / "index.html"
        if not index.is_file():
            raise StudioError(503, "web_not_built",
                              "Мастер не собран: npm --prefix h3_48gb/studio/web run build")
        candidate = (web_dist / path).resolve()
        if path and candidate.is_file() and web_dist.resolve() in candidate.parents:
            return FileResponse(candidate)
        return FileResponse(index)

    return app
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/__main__.py`:

```python
"""`python -m h3_48gb.studio api|openapi` (spec §4.2)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

from h3_48gb.studio import config


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="python -m h3_48gb.studio")
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    api = sub.add_parser("api", help="FastAPI + master + SSE")
    api.add_argument("--host", default="127.0.0.1")
    api.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=8766)
    api.add_argument("--outdir", default=os.environ.get("H3_OUTDIR"))
    spec = sub.add_parser("openapi", help="write the OpenAPI document the master's types use")
    spec.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

    from h3_48gb.studio.app import create_app
    if args.command == "openapi":
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            document = create_app(Path(tmp)).openapi()
        args.out.write_text(json.dumps(document, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True)
                            + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
        return 0
    try:
        outdir = config.resolve_outdir(args.outdir)
    except config.ConfigError as err:
        print(f"studio: {err}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    import uvicorn
    uvicorn.run(create_app(outdir), host=args.host, port=args.port, log_level="info")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/test/fetchStub.ts`:

```ts
import { vi } from "vitest";

/** Replies keyed by the exact "METHOD URL" string. Anything else fails the test: a stub that
 *  matched loosely (startsWith/includes) has let mutations survive in this repo before.
 *  A route is one reply, a queue of replies (the last one repeats), or a handler that computes
 *  the reply from the request body — a tiny server with state, for tests with two clients; a
 *  handler may return a Promise to hold the answer while the test types on. */
export type Reply = { status?: number; body: unknown };
export type Handler = (body: unknown) => Reply | Promise<Reply>;
export type Call = { key: string; body: unknown };

export function stubFetch(routes: Record<string, Reply | Reply[] | Handler>) {
  const calls: Call[] = [];
  const queues = new Map(Object.entries(routes).map(([k, v]) => [k, Array.isArray(v) ? [...v] : [v]]));
  vi.stubGlobal("fetch", async (url: string, init?: RequestInit) => {
    const key = `${init?.method ?? "GET"} ${url}`;
    const raw = init?.body;
    const body = typeof raw === "string" ? JSON.parse(raw) : raw ?? null;
    calls.push({ key, body });
    const queue = queues.get(key);
    if (!queue || queue.length === 0) throw new Error(`unexpected fetch: ${key}`);
    const next = queue.length > 1 ? queue.shift()! : queue[0];
    const reply = await (typeof next === "function" ? next(body) : next);
    return new Response(JSON.stringify(reply.body), {
      status: reply.status ?? 200,
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    });
  });
  return calls;
}

/** EventSource that records its URL and lets the test push events by name. */
export class FakeEventSource {
  static last: FakeEventSource | null = null;
  url: string;
  listeners = new Map<string, ((e: MessageEvent) => void)[]>();
  closed = false;
  constructor(url: string) {
    this.url = url;
    FakeEventSource.last = this;
  }
  addEventListener(kind: string, fn: (e: MessageEvent) => void) {
    this.listeners.set(kind, [...(this.listeners.get(kind) ?? []), fn]);
  }
  emit(kind: string, data: unknown, id: number) {
    for (const fn of this.listeners.get(kind) ?? []) {
      fn(new MessageEvent(kind, { data: JSON.stringify(data), lastEventId: String(id) }));
    }
  }
  close() {
    this.closed = true;
  }
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { stubFetch } from "@/test/fetchStub";
import { api, ApiError } from "./client";

describe("api", () => {
  it("sends JSON and returns the body", async () => {
    const calls = stubFetch({ "POST /api/studio/projects": { status: 201, body: { id: "p_1" } } });
    expect(await api("POST", "/projects", { title: "Лес" })).toEqual({ id: "p_1" });
    expect(calls).toEqual([{ key: "POST /api/studio/projects", body: { title: "Лес" } }]);
  });

  it("turns the §7 envelope into ApiError with code and detail", async () => {
    stubFetch({
      "PATCH /api/studio/projects/p_1": {
        status: 409,
        body: { error: { code: "revision_conflict", message: "Данные изменились", detail: { current: { revision: 2 } } } },
      },
    });
    const err = (await api("PATCH", "/projects/p_1", {}).catch((e: unknown) => e)) as ApiError;
    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(ApiError);
    expect([err.status, err.code, err.message, err.detail]).toEqual([
      409, "revision_conflict", "Данные изменились", { current: { revision: 2 } },
    ]);
  });
});
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/theme.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { applyTheme, followSystem, readTheme } from "./theme";

function stubSystem(dark: boolean) {
  const listeners: (() => void)[] = [];
  const query = {
    matches: dark,
    addEventListener: (kind: string, fn: () => void) => { if (kind === "change") listeners.push(fn); },
    removeEventListener: (kind: string, fn: () => void) => {
      if (kind === "change") listeners.splice(listeners.indexOf(fn), 1);
    },
  };
  vi.stubGlobal("matchMedia", (q: string) => {
    if (q !== "(prefers-color-scheme: dark)") throw new Error(`unexpected media query: ${q}`);
    return query;
  });
  return { flip(next: boolean) { query.matches = next; listeners.forEach((fn) => fn()); }, listeners };
}

afterEach(() => localStorage.clear());

describe("theme", () => {
  it("defaults to light, the main theme", () => {
    expect(readTheme()).toBe("light");
  });

  it("follows the system switching while the choice is «system», and stops after", () => {
    const system = stubSystem(false);
    applyTheme("system");
    const stop = followSystem("system");
    expect(document.documentElement.dataset.theme).toBe("light");
    system.flip(true);
    expect(document.documentElement.dataset.theme).toBe("dark");
    stop();
    expect(system.listeners).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it("an explicit choice does not follow the system", () => {
    const system = stubSystem(false);
    applyTheme("light");
    followSystem("light");
    system.flip(true);
    expect(document.documentElement.dataset.theme).toBe("light");
  });
});
```

```tsx
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { stubFetch } from "@/test/fetchStub";
import { plural, ProjectsScreen } from "./ProjectsScreen";

const ITEM = {
  id: "p_1", revision: 1, title: "Лес", frame_format: "16:9", idea_text: "", style_block: "",
  created_at: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000+00:00", scenes_total: 2, scenes_done: 0,
};

describe("plural", () => {
  it("agrees сцена with the number", () => {
    expect([1, 2, 4, 5, 11, 12, 14, 21, 22, 25].map((n) => `${n} ${plural(n)}`)).toEqual([
      "1 сцена", "2 сцены", "4 сцены", "5 сцен", "11 сцен", "12 сцен", "14 сцен", "21 сцена", "22 сцены", "25 сцен",
    ]);
  });
});

describe("ProjectsScreen", () => {
  it("lists projects from O1 as cards linking to the project", async () => {
    stubFetch({ "GET /api/studio/projects": { body: [ITEM] } });
    render(<ProjectsScreen />);
    const link = await screen.findByRole("link", { name: /Лес/ });
    expect(link.getAttribute("href")).toBe("#/p/p_1");
    expect(link.querySelector("[data-testid=card-meta]")!.textContent).toBe("2 сцены · 16:9");
    expect(screen.getByText("Проекты · 1")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("creates a project with the chosen format and opens it", async () => {
    const calls = stubFetch({
      "GET /api/studio/projects": { body: [] },
      "POST /api/studio/projects": { status: 201, body: { ...ITEM, id: "p_9", frame_format: "9:16" } },
    });
    render(<ProjectsScreen />);
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Новый проект" }));
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText("Название"), "Озеро");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "9:16" }));
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText(/Идея/), "девушка у воды");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Создать проект" }));
    await screen.findByRole("form", { name: "Новый проект" });
    expect(calls[1]).toEqual({
      key: "POST /api/studio/projects",
      body: { title: "Озеро", frame_format: "9:16", idea_text: "девушка у воды" },
    });
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
    expect(location.hash).toBe("#/p/p_9");
  });

  it("shows the server's message when creation is refused", async () => {
    stubFetch({
      "GET /api/studio/projects": { body: [] },
      "POST /api/studio/projects": {
        status: 400,
        body: { error: { code: "args_invalid", message: "Неверные данные запроса", detail: {} } },
      },
    });
    render(<ProjectsScreen />);
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Новый проект" }));
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText("Название"), "X");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Создать проект" }));
    expect((await screen.findByRole("alert")).textContent).toBe("Неверные данные запроса");
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { FakeEventSource, stubFetch } from "@/test/fetchStub";
import { ProjectScreen } from "./ProjectScreen";

const project = (title: string, revision: number) => ({
  id: "p_1", revision, title, frame_format: "16:9", idea_text: "", style_block: "",
  created_at: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000+00:00",
});
const snap = (title: string, revision: number, last_seq: number) => ({
  project: project(title, revision), scenes: [], cast: [], takes: [], tasks: [], last_seq,
});

beforeEach(() => {
  FakeEventSource.last = null;
  vi.stubGlobal("EventSource", FakeEventSource);
});

describe("ProjectScreen (S0)", () => {
  it("subscribes after the snapshot's last_seq and reloads on its own project's event", async () => {
    stubFetch({ "GET /api/studio/projects/p_1": [{ body: snap("Лес", 1, 5) }, { body: snap("Озеро", 2, 6) }] });
    render(<ProjectScreen id="p_1" />);
    await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: "Лес" });
    expect(FakeEventSource.last!.url).toBe("/api/studio/events?after=5");
    FakeEventSource.last!.emit("project_updated", { project_id: "p_1" }, 6);
    await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: "Озеро" });
    expect(FakeEventSource.last!.url).toBe("/api/studio/events?after=6");
  });

  it("ignores another project's events", async () => {

    render(<ProjectScreen id="p_1" />);
    await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: "Лес" });

    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 20));
    expect(calls.map((c) => c.key)).toEqual(["GET /api/studio/projects/p_1"]);
  });
});
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/e2e/phone.spec.ts` (версия S0 — шаг 1; положение кнопки, а не классы — Н8):

```ts
import { expect, test, type Locator, type Page } from "@playwright/test";

const H = 844;
type Reply = [number, unknown];
type Route = Reply | ((body: unknown) => Reply);

/** Exact "METHOD path?query" routes (the events stream included); anything else fails the
 *  test. Every request body is logged in order. */
async function api(page: Page, routes: Record<string, Route>) {
  const log: { key: string; body: unknown }[] = [];
  await page.route("**/api/studio/**", async (route) => {
    const request = route.request();
    const url = new URL(request.url());
    const key = `${request.method()} ${url.pathname}${url.search}`;
    const raw = request.postData();
    const body = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
    log.push({ key, body });
    const hit = routes[key];
    if (!hit) throw new Error(`unexpected request: ${key}`);
    const [status, payload] = typeof hit === "function" ? hit(body) : hit;
    if (status === 204) return route.fulfill({ status });
    await route.fulfill({ status, contentType: "application/json", body: JSON.stringify(payload) });
  });
  return log;
}

/** Pinned = its bottom edge sits 12 px (the bar's padding) above the bottom of the window, and it
 *  stays there when the page is scrolled to the very end; nothing scrolls under it unreachable. */
async function expectPinned(page: Page, button: Locator) {
  await page.mouse.move(0, 0);              // a hovered primary button lifts by 1 px (AB hover)
  await expect(button).toBeVisible();
  const atTop = await button.boundingBox();
  expect(Math.round(atTop!.y + atTop!.height)).toBe(H - 12);
  await page.evaluate(() => window.scrollTo(0, document.documentElement.scrollHeight));
  const atEnd = await button.boundingBox();
  expect(Math.round(atEnd!.y + atEnd!.height)).toBe(H - 12);
  const last = await page.locator("main input, main textarea").last().boundingBox();
  const barTop = (await page.locator("form[aria-label='Новый проект'] > div.fixed").first().boundingBox())!.y;
  expect(last!.y + last!.height).toBeLessThanOrEqual(barTop);
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth)).toBe(390);
  await page.evaluate(() => window.scrollTo(0, 0));
}

test("step 1: «Создать проект» is pinned", async ({ page }) => {
  await api(page, { "GET /api/studio/projects": [200, []] });
  await page.goto("/studio/");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Новый проект" }).click();
  await expectPinned(page, page.getByRole("button", { name: "Создать проект" }));
});
```

```css
@import "tailwindcss";
@import "@fontsource/inter/cyrillic-400.css";
@import "@fontsource/inter/cyrillic-500.css";
@import "@fontsource/inter/cyrillic-600.css";
@import "@fontsource/inter/latin-400.css";
@import "@fontsource/inter/latin-500.css";
@import "@fontsource/inter/latin-600.css";
@import "@fontsource/inter-tight/cyrillic-600.css";
@import "@fontsource/inter-tight/latin-600.css";
@import "@fontsource/jetbrains-mono/cyrillic-400.css";
@import "@fontsource/jetbrains-mono/latin-400.css";

/* Design system AB (docs/design/2026-10-studio-style/AB/README.md): tokens of both themes. */
:root {
  --bg: #ffffff; --surface: #f6f7f9; --surface2: #eef0f4; --line: #e3e6ec; --line2: #cfd4de;
  --ink: #0b0d12; --ink2: #464c5a; --ink3: #6b7280;
  --cobalt: #1747e6; --cobalt-ink: #ffffff; --cobalt-soft: #eaf0ff; --cobalt-line: #b9ccff;
  --ok: #0a7a4b; --ok-soft: #e5f6ee; --warn: #a15c00; --warn-soft: #fff3dd;
  --danger: #b42318; --danger-soft: #fdecea;
  --scrim: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.88);
}
[data-theme="dark"] {
  --bg: #0a0c10; --surface: #10131a; --surface2: #171b24; --line: #222833; --line2: #333b4a;
  --ink: #f2f4f8; --ink2: #b4bbc9; --ink3: #8b93a3;
  --cobalt: #5b83ff; --cobalt-ink: #06102e; --cobalt-soft: #121d3d; --cobalt-line: #27407f;
  --ok: #4cd391; --ok-soft: #0d2a1e; --warn: #f2b254; --warn-soft: #2c2108;
  --danger: #ff8a7a; --danger-soft: #3a1410;
  --scrim: rgba(10, 12, 16, 0.9);
}

@theme inline {
  --font-sans: "Inter", ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif;
  --font-display: "Inter Tight", "Inter", sans-serif;
  --font-mono: "JetBrains Mono", ui-monospace, monospace;
  --color-bg: var(--bg);
  --color-surface: var(--surface);
  --color-surface2: var(--surface2);
  --color-line: var(--line);
  --color-line2: var(--line2);
  --color-ink: var(--ink);
  --color-ink2: var(--ink2);
  --color-ink3: var(--ink3);
  --color-cobalt: var(--cobalt);
  --color-cobalt-ink: var(--cobalt-ink);
  --color-cobalt-soft: var(--cobalt-soft);
  --color-cobalt-line: var(--cobalt-line);
  --color-ok: var(--ok);
  --color-ok-soft: var(--ok-soft);
  --color-warn: var(--warn);
  --color-warn-soft: var(--warn-soft);
  --color-danger: var(--danger);
  --color-danger-soft: var(--danger-soft);
  /* shadcn/ui variables mapped onto AB, so copied components take the AB look */
  --color-background: var(--bg);
  --color-foreground: var(--ink);
  --color-primary: var(--cobalt);
  --color-primary-foreground: var(--cobalt-ink);
  --color-secondary: var(--surface);
  --color-secondary-foreground: var(--ink);
  --color-muted: var(--surface);
  --color-muted-foreground: var(--ink3);
  --color-accent: var(--surface);
  --color-accent-foreground: var(--ink);
  --color-destructive: var(--danger);
  --color-border: var(--line);
  --color-input: var(--line2);
  --color-ring: var(--cobalt);
  --radius-sm: 4px;
  --radius-md: 6px;
  --radius-lg: 10px;
}

@layer base {
  html { background: var(--bg); color: var(--ink); font-family: var(--font-sans);
         -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased; }
  body { background: var(--bg); font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.45; }
  :focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--cobalt); outline-offset: 2px; border-radius: 4px; }
}

@layer components {
  .eyebrow { font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 11px; letter-spacing: 0.06em;
             text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--ink3); }
  .h-display { font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: -0.025em; }
  .route { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(6, 1fr); gap: 3px; }
  .route i { display: block; height: 3px; background: var(--line2); border-radius: 1px; }
  .route i.done { background: var(--ink); }
  .route i.cur { background: var(--cobalt); }
}
```

```ts
import { clsx, type ClassValue } from "clsx";
import { twMerge } from "tailwind-merge";

export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {
  return twMerge(clsx(inputs));
}
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/components/ui/button.tsx`:

```tsx
// shadcn/ui button (new-york, `npx shadcn@4.22.1 add button`), re-skinned for design system AB:
// pills (form from direction C), the primary is a cobalt gradient with a glow, colours from the
// AB tokens mapped onto shadcn's variables in styles.css. Sizes follow AB: 32 / 46 px.
import * as React from "react";
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";
import { Slot } from "radix-ui";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const buttonVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex shrink-0 cursor-pointer items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border font-medium whitespace-nowrap " +
    "transition-[filter,transform,background-color] outline-none focus-visible:ring-[3px] focus-visible:ring-ring/40 " +
    "disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:shrink-0 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default:
          "border-transparent font-semibold text-primary-foreground shadow-[0_10px_24px_-12px_var(--cobalt)] " +
          "bg-[linear-gradient(135deg,var(--cobalt),color-mix(in_srgb,var(--cobalt)_70%,#5fa8ff))] " +
          "hover:brightness-105 hover:-translate-y-px",
        outline: "border-input bg-background text-foreground hover:bg-accent",
        secondary: "border-transparent bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground hover:bg-surface2",
        ghost: "border-transparent bg-transparent text-ink2 hover:bg-accent hover:text-foreground",
        destructive: "border-transparent bg-destructive text-white hover:brightness-95",
        link: "border-transparent text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline",
      },
      size: {
        default: "h-8 px-3 text-[13px]",
        lg: "h-[46px] px-[22px] text-sm",
        icon: "size-8",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: { variant: "default", size: "default" },
  },
);

function Button({
  className,
  variant = "default",
  size = "default",
  asChild = false,
  type = "button",
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"button"> & VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants> & { asChild?: boolean }) {
  const Comp = asChild ? Slot.Root : "button";
  return (
    <Comp
      data-slot="button"
      data-variant={variant}
      data-size={size}
      type={asChild ? undefined : type}
      className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size, className }))}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

export { Button, buttonVariants };
```

```tsx
// shadcn/ui badge (new-york, shadcn@4.22.1), re-skinned as the AB chip: 22px pill, mono 11px.
// `ok` and `warn` are AB additions; `default` is the cobalt chip, `outline` the neutral one.
import * as React from "react";
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";
import { Slot } from "radix-ui";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const badgeVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex h-[22px] w-fit shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full border px-2 font-mono text-[11px] " +
    "whitespace-nowrap [&>svg]:pointer-events-none [&>svg]:size-3",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "border-cobalt-line bg-cobalt-soft text-cobalt",
        outline: "border-border bg-background text-ink2",
        secondary: "border-transparent bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground",
        destructive: "border-transparent bg-danger-soft text-danger",
        ok: "border-transparent bg-ok-soft text-ok",
        warn: "border-transparent bg-warn-soft text-warn",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: { variant: "default" },
  },
);

function Badge({
  className,
  variant = "default",
  asChild = false,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"span"> & VariantProps<typeof badgeVariants> & { asChild?: boolean }) {
  const Comp = asChild ? Slot.Root : "span";
  return <Comp data-slot="badge" data-variant={variant} className={cn(badgeVariants({ variant }), className)} {...props} />;
}

export { Badge, badgeVariants };
```

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";

/** AB field: label line (text + mono hint) above the control. The control sits inside the Radix
 *  label, so `getByLabelText` and a click on the text both reach it. */
export function Field({ text, hint, children }: { text: string; hint?: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <Label className="grid gap-1.5">
      <span className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
        {text}
        {hint && <small className="font-mono text-[10.5px] font-normal text-ink3">{hint}</small>}
      </span>
      {children}
    </Label>
  );
}
```

```ts
// Request bodies come from the server's OpenAPI (schema.d.ts, generated by `npm run gen:api`).
// Response shapes are the dicts `h3_48gb/studio/ops.py` builds; they are written out here.
import type { components } from "./schema";

export type ProjectCreate = components["schemas"]["ProjectCreate"];
export type ProjectPatch = components["schemas"]["ProjectPatch"];

export type Project = {
  id: string;
  revision: number;
  title: string;
  frame_format: "16:9" | "9:16";
  idea_text: string;
  style_block: string;
  created_at: string;
};
export type ProjectListItem = Project & { scenes_total: number; scenes_done: number };

export type Snapshot = {
  project: Project;
  scenes: unknown[];
  cast: unknown[];
  takes: unknown[];
  tasks: unknown[];
  last_seq: number;
};

export type ApiErrorBody = { error: { code: string; message: string; detail: Record<string, unknown> } };
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/api/client.ts`:

```ts
import type { ApiErrorBody } from "./types";

export const API = "/api/studio";

export class ApiError extends Error {
  status: number;
  code: string;
  detail: Record<string, unknown>;
  constructor(status: number, code: string, message: string, detail: Record<string, unknown>) {
    super(message);
    this.status = status;
    this.code = code;
    this.detail = detail;
  }
}

async function parse<T>(res: Response): Promise<T> {
  const text = await res.text();
  const body = text ? JSON.parse(text) : null;
  if (res.ok) return body as T;
  const err = (body as ApiErrorBody | null)?.error;
  if (err) throw new ApiError(res.status, err.code, err.message, err.detail ?? {});
  throw new ApiError(res.status, "http_error", `Сервер ответил ${res.status}`, {});
}

export async function api<T>(method: string, path: string, body?: unknown): Promise<T> {
  const init: RequestInit = { method };
  if (body !== undefined) {
    init.headers = { "Content-Type": "application/json" };
    init.body = JSON.stringify(body);
  }
  return parse<T>(await fetch(`${API}${path}`, init));
}

export async function upload<T>(path: string, file: File, fields: Record<string, string> = {}) {
  const form = new FormData();
  form.append("file", file);
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(fields)) form.append(k, v);
  return parse<T>(await fetch(`${API}${path}`, { method: "POST", body: form }));
}
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/theme.ts`:

```ts
export type ThemeChoice = "light" | "dark" | "system";
const KEY = "studio.theme";
const ORDER: ThemeChoice[] = ["light", "dark", "system"];
const DARK_QUERY = "(prefers-color-scheme: dark)";

/** Light is the main theme (spec §4.4), so it is also the default. */
export function readTheme(): ThemeChoice {
  try {
    const v = localStorage.getItem(KEY);
    return v === "light" || v === "dark" || v === "system" ? v : "light";
  } catch {
    return "light";
  }
}

export function applyTheme(choice: ThemeChoice) {
  const dark = choice === "dark" || (choice === "system" && matchMedia(DARK_QUERY).matches);
  document.documentElement.dataset.theme = dark ? "dark" : "light";
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(KEY, choice);
  } catch {
    /* private mode: the choice lives until reload */
  }
}

/** While the choice is "system", follow the OS switching light/dark without a reload. */
export function followSystem(choice: ThemeChoice): () => void {
  if (choice !== "system") return () => {};
  const query = matchMedia(DARK_QUERY);
  const onChange = () => applyTheme("system");
  query.addEventListener("change", onChange);
  return () => query.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
}

export function nextTheme(choice: ThemeChoice): ThemeChoice {
  return ORDER[(ORDER.indexOf(choice) + 1) % ORDER.length];
}

export const THEME_LABEL: Record<ThemeChoice, string> = {
  light: "Светлая",
  dark: "Тёмная",
  system: "Как в системе",
};
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/router.ts`:

```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export type Route = { name: "projects" } | { name: "project"; id: string };

export function parseHash(hash: string): Route {
  const m = /^#\/p\/([A-Za-z0-9_]+)$/.exec(hash);
  return m ? { name: "project", id: m[1] } : { name: "projects" };
}

export function useRoute(): Route {
  const [route, setRoute] = useState(() => parseHash(location.hash));
  useEffect(() => {
    const on = () => setRoute(parseHash(location.hash));
    addEventListener("hashchange", on);
    return () => removeEventListener("hashchange", on);
  }, []);
  return route;
}
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/useEvents.ts`:

```ts
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { API } from "./api/client";

/** O21: subscribe after `afterSeq` (И10: the snapshot's last_seq) and call `onEvent` for the
 *  listed kinds of THIS project only — the journal is shared by all projects. EventSource itself
 *  resends Last-Event-ID on reconnect. */
export function useEvents(projectId: string, afterSeq: number | null, kinds: string[],
                          onEvent: (kind: string, seq: number) => void) {
  const handler = useRef(onEvent);
  handler.current = onEvent;
  const kindsKey = kinds.join(",");
  useEffect(() => {
    if (afterSeq === null) return;
    const source = new EventSource(`${API}/events?after=${afterSeq}`);
    for (const kind of kindsKey.split(",")) {
      source.addEventListener(kind, (e) => {
        const message = e as MessageEvent;
        if (JSON.parse(message.data).project_id !== projectId) return;
        handler.current(kind, Number(message.lastEventId));
      });
    }
    return () => source.close();
  }, [projectId, afterSeq, kindsKey]);
}
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/components/AppHeader.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Clapperboard, Monitor, Moon, Sun } from "lucide-react";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { applyTheme, followSystem, nextTheme, readTheme, THEME_LABEL, type ThemeChoice } from "@/theme";

const ICON: Record<ThemeChoice, typeof Sun> = { light: Sun, dark: Moon, system: Monitor };

export function AppHeader() {
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<ThemeChoice>(readTheme);
  useEffect(() => followSystem(theme), [theme]);
  const Icon = ICON[theme];
  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 border-b border-line bg-[var(--scrim)] backdrop-blur-md">
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-14 max-w-[1360px] items-center gap-3 px-4 md:px-8">
        <a href="#/" className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-ink no-underline">
          <span className="grid h-6 w-6 place-items-center rounded-[5px] bg-ink text-bg">
            <Clapperboard className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          </span>
          <span className="h-display text-[17px]">Студия</span>
        </a>
        <div className="ml-auto">
          <Button
            variant="outline"
            aria-label={`Тема: ${THEME_LABEL[theme]}`}
            onClick={() => {
              const next = nextTheme(theme);
              applyTheme(next);
              setTheme(next);
            }}
          >
            <Icon />
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">{THEME_LABEL[theme]}</span>
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/screens/ProjectsScreen.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Film, Plus } from "lucide-react";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { api, ApiError } from "@/api/client";
import type { Project, ProjectCreate, ProjectListItem } from "@/api/types";
import { Field } from "@/components/Field";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";

export const STEPS = ["Идея", "Герой", "Сценарий", "Эскизы", "Финал", "Сборка"];

export function plural(n: number) {
  const d = n % 10, h = n % 100;
  if (d === 1 && h !== 11) return "сцена";
  if (d >= 2 && d <= 4 && (h < 12 || h > 14)) return "сцены";
  return "сцен";
}

function ProjectCard({ p, n }: { p: ProjectListItem; n: number }) {
  return (
    <a href={`#/p/${p.id}`} className="block overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-line bg-bg text-ink no-underline transition-colors hover:border-ink">
      <div className="relative aspect-video bg-surface2">
        <span className="absolute left-3 top-3 rounded-full bg-black/60 px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-white">
          № {String(n).padStart(2, "0")}
        </span>
        <span className="absolute bottom-3 left-3 flex items-center gap-1.5 font-mono text-[12px] text-ink2">
          <Film className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          <span data-testid="card-meta">{`${p.scenes_total} ${plural(p.scenes_total)} · ${p.frame_format}`}</span>
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="p-5">
        <h2 className="h-display text-[24px] leading-tight">{p.title}</h2>
        <div className="route mt-5">
          {STEPS.map((s, k) => <i key={s} className={k === 0 ? "cur" : ""} />)}
        </div>
      </div>
    </a>
  );
}

function NewProjectForm({ onCreated }: { onCreated: (p: Project) => void }) {
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [format, setFormat] = useState<"16:9" | "9:16">("16:9");
  const [idea, setIdea] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  async function submit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const body: ProjectCreate = { title: title.trim(), frame_format: format, idea_text: idea };
      onCreated(await api<Project>("POST", "/projects", body));
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Сервер не ответил");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }
  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} className="mt-6 grid gap-4 rounded-lg border border-line p-5" aria-label="Новый проект">
      <Field text="Название">
        <Input value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} required maxLength={200} />
      </Field>
      <div>
        <span className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-semibold">Формат кадра</span>
        <div role="group" aria-label="Формат кадра" className="inline-flex rounded-full border border-line2 bg-surface p-0.5">
          {(["16:9", "9:16"] as const).map((f) => (
            <button
              key={f}
              type="button"
              aria-pressed={format === f}
              onClick={() => setFormat(f)}
              className="h-[30px] cursor-pointer rounded-full px-3.5 text-[13px] font-medium text-ink2 aria-pressed:bg-bg aria-pressed:text-ink aria-pressed:shadow-[0_2px_10px_-3px_rgba(23,71,230,.28),0_0_0_1px_var(--line)]"
            >
              {f}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      <Field text="Идея" hint="можно позже">
        <Textarea value={idea} onChange={(e) => setIdea(e.target.value)} />
      </Field>
      {error && <p role="alert" className="text-[13px] text-danger">{error}</p>}
      <div className="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-20 border-t border-line bg-[var(--scrim)] p-3 backdrop-blur-md md:static md:border-0 md:bg-transparent md:p-0">
        <Button type="submit" size="lg" disabled={busy || !title.trim()} className="w-full md:w-auto">
          Создать проект
        </Button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

export function ProjectsScreen() {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<ProjectListItem[] | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    api<ProjectListItem[]>("GET", "/projects").then(setItems, (err) =>
      setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Сервер не ответил"),
    );
  }, []);
  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1360px] px-4 pb-28 pt-8 md:px-8 md:pb-16 md:pt-12">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 md:flex-row md:items-end md:justify-between">
        <div>
          <div className="eyebrow">Проекты · {items?.length ?? "…"}</div>
          <h1 className="h-display mt-2 text-[34px] leading-[1.05] md:text-[52px]">
            Что снимаем<span className="text-cobalt">.</span>
          </h1>
        </div>
        {!creating && (
          <Button size="lg" onClick={() => setCreating(true)}>
            <Plus />
            Новый проект
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>
      {creating && <NewProjectForm onCreated={(p) => (location.hash = `#/p/${p.id}`)} />}
      {error && <p role="alert" className="mt-6 text-danger">{error}</p>}
      {items && items.length === 0 && !creating && (
        <p className="mt-8 text-ink2">Проектов пока нет — начните с «Новый проект».</p>
      )}
      <div className="mt-8 grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2 md:gap-8">
        {items?.map((p, i) => <ProjectCard key={p.id} p={p} n={i + 1} />)}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/screens/ProjectScreen.tsx` (версия S0; задача 12 заменяет):

```tsx
import { ChevronLeft } from "lucide-react";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { api, ApiError } from "@/api/client";
import type { Snapshot } from "@/api/types";
import { useEvents } from "@/useEvents";

const RELOAD_ON = ["project_updated"];

export function ProjectScreen({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const [snap, setSnap] = useState<Snapshot | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const load = useCallback(() => {
    api<Snapshot>("GET", `/projects/${id}`).then(setSnap, (err) =>
      setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Сервер не ответил"),
    );
  }, [id]);
  useEffect(load, [load]);
  useEvents(id, snap?.last_seq ?? null, RELOAD_ON, load);
  if (error) return <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1360px] px-4 pt-8 md:px-8"><p role="alert" className="text-danger">{error}</p></main>;
  if (!snap) return <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1360px] px-4 pt-8 md:px-8"><p className="text-ink3">Загрузка…</p></main>;
  const p = snap.project;
  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1360px] px-4 pb-28 pt-6 md:px-8 md:pb-16 md:pt-8">
      <a href="#/" className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[13px] text-ink2 no-underline hover:text-ink">
        <ChevronLeft className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
        Проекты
      </a>
      <div className="mt-4">
        <div className="eyebrow">Проект · {p.frame_format}</div>
        <h1 className="h-display mt-2 text-[30px] leading-[1.05] md:text-[44px]">{p.title}</h1>
        {p.idea_text && <p className="mt-3 max-w-[60ch] text-[15px] text-ink2">{p.idea_text}</p>}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { StrictMode } from "react";
import { createRoot } from "react-dom/client";
import { AppHeader } from "@/components/AppHeader";
import { useRoute } from "@/router";
import { ProjectScreen } from "@/screens/ProjectScreen";
import { ProjectsScreen } from "@/screens/ProjectsScreen";
import "./styles.css";

function App() {
  const route = useRoute();
  return (
    <>
      <AppHeader />
      {route.name === "project" ? <ProjectScreen key={route.id} id={route.id} /> : <ProjectsScreen />}
    </>
  );
}

createRoot(document.getElementById("root")!).render(
  <StrictMode>
    <App />
  </StrictMode>,
);
```

**Interfaces:**

- Produces: образ `h3-studio:latest`; теги отката `h3-studio:prev` и `h3-studio:prev-<stamp>` = образ **работающего** контейнера (Н7); compose-проект `h3-studio` с сервисом `studio-api` на `network_mode: host`, порт 8766, `STUDIO_ALLOWED_HOSTS=192.168.100.50` (R26), данные в `/home/alex/Outputs/studio`;
  - `scripts/check_panel_untouched.sh` — отпечаток старой панели (ID контейнера, ID образа, SHA кода, HTTP 200 + JSON от `:8765/api/state`), любой сбой — выход 1 с причиной (Н6);
  - `scripts/studio_tag_prev.sh <stamp>` — тег отката до сборки (Н7);
  - `scripts/check_image_secrets.sh` — сборка из временной копии закоммиченного дерева с синтетическими секретами; поиск в образе: 0 — `LEAK`, выход 1; 1 — «нет секрета», выход 0; любой другой код (docker не запустился, grep не прочитал) — `check failed`, выход 2 (Б-5); `MUTATE_DOCKERIGNORE=1` — собственная проверка «увидеть красным» (Н5).

**Порядок (К-3):** файлы выкладки и тесты скриптов → гейт → **коммит координатора** → проверка секретов в образе (по коммиту) → bundle → выкладка со сверкой SHA → приёмка.

- [ ] **Step 1: Написать падающие тесты скриптов выкладки**

Create `tests/test_studio_deploy_scripts.py` (стабы `docker`/`git`/`curl` на `PATH`; настоящий Docker и старая панель не трогаются):

```python
"""Deploy helpers (plan task 5): the panel fingerprint must FAIL when a probe fails, and the
rollback tag must point at the image the running studio container uses (review r2, Н6, Н7).

docker, git and curl are replaced by stubs on PATH that answer from environment variables and
log their argv; nothing touches a real Docker daemon or the old panel."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
PANEL = ROOT / "scripts" / "check_panel_untouched.sh"
PREV = ROOT / "scripts" / "studio_tag_prev.sh"

DOCKER = r"""#!/bin/sh
printf '%s\n' "$*" >> "$STUB_LOG"
case "$1" in
  ps) [ -n "${STUB_PS:-}" ] && printf '%b\n' "$STUB_PS"; exit 0 ;;
  inspect) [ "${STUB_INSPECT_FAIL:-0}" = 1 ] && exit 1; printf '%s\n' "$STUB_IMAGE" ;;
  tag) exit 0 ;;
  *) exit 3 ;;
esac
"""
GIT = r"""#!/bin/sh
[ "${STUB_GIT_FAIL:-0}" = 1 ] && exit 128
printf '%s\n' "${STUB_SHA:-0123abc}"
"""
CURL = r"""#!/bin/sh
out=""
while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do [ "$1" = "-o" ] && { out=$2; shift; }; shift; done
[ "${STUB_CURL_EXIT:-0}" != 0 ] && exit "$STUB_CURL_EXIT"
printf '%s' "${STUB_BODY:-{\"ok\": true}}" > "$out"
printf '%s' "${STUB_HTTP:-200}"
"""


@pytest.fixture
def run(tmp_path):
    stubs = tmp_path / "bin"
    stubs.mkdir()
    for name, text in (("docker", DOCKER), ("git", GIT), ("curl", CURL)):
        (stubs / name).write_text(text)
        (stubs / name).chmod(0o755)
    log = tmp_path / "docker.log"

    def _run(script: Path, *args: str, **env: str):
        full = {"PATH": f"{stubs}:/usr/bin:/bin", "STUB_LOG": str(log),
                "STUB_IMAGE": "sha256:aaa", **env}
        done = subprocess.run(["bash", str(script), *args], capture_output=True, text=True,
                              env=full)
        calls = log.read_text().splitlines() if log.exists() else []
        return done, calls
    return _run


def test_a_healthy_panel_prints_its_fingerprint(run):
    done, _ = run(PANEL, STUB_PS="cid1")
    assert (done.returncode, done.stdout, done.stderr) == (
        0, "container cid1\nimage sha256:aaa\ncode 0123abc\nhttp 200\n", "")


@pytest.mark.parametrize("env, message", [
    ({"STUB_PS": "cid1", "STUB_HTTP": "500"},
     "panel check failed: GET http://127.0.0.1:8765/api/state answered 500\n"),
    ({"STUB_PS": "cid1", "STUB_BODY": "<html>"},
     "panel check failed: http://127.0.0.1:8765/api/state did not answer JSON\n"),
    ({"STUB_PS": "cid1", "STUB_CURL_EXIT": "7"},
     "panel check failed: curl http://127.0.0.1:8765/api/state failed\n"),
    ({"STUB_PS": ""}, "panel check failed: expected exactly one running container "
                      "h3-panel-h3-panel-1, found 0\n"),
    ({"STUB_PS": "cid1\\ncid2"}, "panel check failed: expected exactly one running container "
                                 "h3-panel-h3-panel-1, found 2\n"),
])
def test_a_failing_probe_exits_non_zero_with_the_reason(run, env, message):
    done, _ = run(PANEL, **env)
    assert (done.returncode, done.stdout, done.stderr) == (1, "", message)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("env", [{"STUB_PS": "cid1", "STUB_INSPECT_FAIL": "1"},
                                 {"STUB_PS": "cid1", "STUB_GIT_FAIL": "1"}])
def test_a_failing_docker_or_git_call_is_not_swallowed(run, env):
    """`set -e` does not stop on a failure inside `echo "$(…)"` — each value is taken alone."""
    done, _ = run(PANEL, **env)
    assert done.returncode != 0
    assert done.stdout == ""


# --- a Docker with state, for the rollback tag and the image secret check -----------------------

STATEFUL_DOCKER = r"""#!/usr/bin/env python3
import json, os, sys
path = os.environ["DOCKER_STATE"]
st = json.load(open(path))
args = sys.argv[1:]
st.setdefault("calls", []).append(" ".join(args))
code = 0
if args[0] == "ps":                                  # ps -q --no-trunc --filter name=^NAME$
    name = args[-1].split("=^", 1)[1].rstrip("$")
    if name in st["containers"]:
        print(name)
elif args[0] == "inspect":                           # inspect -f {{.Image}} CONTAINER
    print(st["containers"][args[-1]])
elif args[:2] == ["image", "inspect"]:               # image inspect -f {{.Id}} REF
    print(st["tags"][args[-1]])
elif args[0] == "tag":
    st["tags"][args[2]] = st["tags"].get(args[1], args[1])
elif args[0] == "build":
    code = st.get("build_rc", 0)
elif args[0] == "run":
    code = st.get("run_rc", 1)
json.dump(st, open(path, "w"))
sys.exit(code)
"""


@pytest.fixture
def docker_state(tmp_path):
    stubs = tmp_path / "sbin"
    stubs.mkdir()
    (stubs / "docker").write_text(STATEFUL_DOCKER)
    (stubs / "docker").chmod(0o755)
    path = tmp_path / "docker.json"

    def run(argv: list[str], state: dict | None = None, cwd: Path | None = None):
        if state is not None:
            path.write_text(json.dumps(state))
        done = subprocess.run(argv, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=cwd,
                              env={"PATH": f"{stubs}:/usr/bin:/bin", "DOCKER_STATE": str(path),
                                   "HOME": str(tmp_path)})
        return done, json.loads(path.read_text())
    return run


A, B = "sha256:" + "a" * 64, "sha256:" + "b" * 64
STUDIO = "h3-studio-studio-api-1"


def test_rollback_returns_to_the_image_that_was_running_after_a_failed_build_and_a_retry(
        docker_state):
    """Review r3: images, not argv. A runs; `latest` already points at the new B (build passed,
    tests in the image failed, no `up`); the retry tags again; rollback must restore A."""
    _, st = docker_state(["bash", str(PREV), "20261021-1200"],
                         {"containers": {STUDIO: A}, "tags": {"h3-studio:latest": A}})
    st["tags"]["h3-studio:latest"] = B                      # compose build of the new image
    docker_state(["true"], st)
    done, st = docker_state(["bash", str(PREV), "20261021-1230"])
    assert (done.returncode, done.stdout) == (
        0, f"prev {A} -> h3-studio:prev h3-studio:prev-20261021-1230\n")
    _, st = docker_state(["docker", "tag", "h3-studio:prev", "h3-studio:latest"])   # DEPLOY: Откат
    assert st["tags"] == {"h3-studio:latest": A, "h3-studio:prev": A,
                          "h3-studio:prev-20261021-1200": A, "h3-studio:prev-20261021-1230": A}


def test_prev_on_the_first_deploy_tags_nothing(docker_state):
    done, st = docker_state(["bash", str(PREV), "20261021-1200"], {"containers": {}, "tags": {}})
    assert (done.returncode, done.stdout, st["tags"]) == (
        0, "no running studio container: first deploy, nothing to keep\n", {})


SECRETS = ROOT / "scripts" / "check_image_secrets.sh"


@pytest.fixture
def tree(tmp_path):
    """A committed tree with what the script needs: Dockerfile, .dockerignore, a built dist."""
    repo = tmp_path / "repo"
    (repo / "h3_48gb" / "studio" / "web" / "dist").mkdir(parents=True)
    (repo / "Dockerfile.studio").write_text("FROM scratch\n")
    (repo / ".dockerignore").write_text(".env\n**/.env\n")
    (repo / ".gitignore").write_text("dist/\n")
    (repo / "h3_48gb" / "studio" / "web" / "dist" / "index.html").write_text("<!doctype html>")
    git = ["git", "-C", str(repo), "-c", "user.email=t@t", "-c", "user.name=t"]
    subprocess.run([*git[:3], "init", "-q"], check=True)
    subprocess.run([*git, "add", "-A"], check=True)
    subprocess.run([*git, "commit", "-qm", "tree"], check=True)
    return repo


@pytest.mark.parametrize("run_rc, code, out, err", [
    (1, 0, "no planted secret in the image\n", ""),
    (0, 1, "", "LEAK: a planted secret is inside the image\n"),
    (125, 2, "", "check failed: the search in the image exited 125\n"),   # docker could not run
    (2, 2, "", "check failed: the search in the image exited 2\n"),       # grep could not read
])
def test_only_not_found_is_a_pass(docker_state, tree, run_rc, code, out, err):
    before = sorted(p.name for p in tree.iterdir())
    done, st = docker_state(["bash", str(SECRETS)], {"containers": {}, "tags": {}, "run_rc": run_rc},
                            cwd=tree)
    assert (done.returncode, done.stdout, done.stderr) == (code, out, err)
    assert sorted(p.name for p in tree.iterdir()) == before      # the working tree is untouched
    assert not (tree / ".env").exists()


def test_a_failed_build_is_a_failed_check(docker_state, tree):
    done, _ = docker_state(["bash", str(SECRETS)], {"containers": {}, "tags": {}, "build_rc": 1},
                           cwd=tree)
    assert done.returncode != 0
    assert "no planted secret" not in done.stdout
```
