Create `scripts/check_panel_untouched.sh`:

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Fingerprint of the old panel on alex-neuro (studio spec §4.3: the studio must not touch it).
# Run before and after a studio deploy; the two outputs must be byte-identical. Every probe is
# checked on its own and any failure exits non-zero — a diagnostic that cannot fail proves nothing.
set -euo pipefail

PANEL_NAME=${PANEL_NAME:-h3-panel-h3-panel-1}
PANEL_REPO=${PANEL_REPO:-/home/alex/Projects/h3-panel}
PANEL_URL=${PANEL_URL:-http://127.0.0.1:8765/api/state}

fail() { printf 'panel check failed: %s\n' "$1" >&2; exit 1; }

ids=$(docker ps -q --no-trunc --filter "name=^${PANEL_NAME}\$")
count=$(printf '%s\n' "$ids" | grep -c . || true)
[ "$count" = 1 ] || fail "expected exactly one running container ${PANEL_NAME}, found ${count}"
cid=$ids
image=$(docker inspect -f '{{.Image}}' "$cid")
[ -n "$image" ] || fail "docker inspect gave no image id"
code=$(git -C "$PANEL_REPO" rev-parse HEAD)
body=$(mktemp)
trap 'rm -f "$body"' EXIT
http=$(curl -s -o "$body" -w '%{http_code}' "$PANEL_URL") || fail "curl ${PANEL_URL} failed"
[ "$http" = 200 ] || fail "GET ${PANEL_URL} answered ${http}"
python3 -c 'import json, sys; json.load(open(sys.argv[1]))' "$body" 2>/dev/null \
  || fail "${PANEL_URL} did not answer JSON"
printf 'container %s\nimage %s\ncode %s\nhttp %s\n' "$cid" "$image" "$code" "$http"
```

Create `scripts/studio_tag_prev.sh`:

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Before a studio deploy: tag the image the RUNNING studio container uses as h3-studio:prev (and a
# unique h3-studio:prev-<stamp>). Not `latest`: after a failed build `latest` may already be the
# new image while the container still runs the old one — a retry would then lose the way back.
set -euo pipefail

NAME=${STUDIO_CONTAINER:-h3-studio-studio-api-1}
stamp=${1:?usage: studio_tag_prev.sh <deploy-stamp>}

ids=$(docker ps -q --no-trunc --filter "name=^${NAME}\$")
if [ -z "$ids" ]; then
  printf 'no running studio container: first deploy, nothing to keep\n'
  exit 0
fi
count=$(printf '%s\n' "$ids" | grep -c .)
[ "$count" = 1 ] || { printf 'expected one studio container, found %s\n' "$count" >&2; exit 1; }
image=$(docker inspect -f '{{.Image}}' "$ids")
[ -n "$image" ] || { printf 'docker inspect gave no image id\n' >&2; exit 1; }
docker tag "$image" "h3-studio:prev-${stamp}"
docker tag "$image" h3-studio:prev
printf 'prev %s -> h3-studio:prev h3-studio:prev-%s\n' "$image" "$stamp"
```

Create `scripts/check_image_secrets.sh`:

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Build the studio image from a TEMPORARY copy of the committed tree (+ the built web/dist) with
# synthetic secrets planted in it, and fail if any of them reached the image. The working tree —
# and a real .env in it, if any — is never read or written.
# MUTATE_DOCKERIGNORE=1 strips every .env / providers.json rule from the COPY's .dockerignore: the
# check must then fail; that run is its own "seen red".
set -euo pipefail

repo=$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)
dist="$repo/h3_48gb/studio/web/dist"
[ -f "$dist/index.html" ] || { printf 'build the master first\n' >&2; exit 2; }
tmp=$(mktemp -d)
tag="h3-studio:secret-check-$$"
trap 'docker rmi -f "$tag" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true; rm -rf "$tmp"' EXIT

git -C "$repo" archive HEAD | tar -x -C "$tmp"
mkdir -p "$tmp/h3_48gb/studio/web/dist"
cp -R "$dist/." "$tmp/h3_48gb/studio/web/dist/"
marker="sk-SYNTHETIC-$$-$(date +%s)-CHECK"
printf 'CAILA_API_KEY=%s\n' "$marker" > "$tmp/.env"
mkdir -p "$tmp/conf"
printf 'KEY=%s\n' "$marker" > "$tmp/conf/.env"
printf '{"key": "%s"}\n' "$marker" > "$tmp/conf/providers.json"
if [ "${MUTATE_DOCKERIGNORE:-0}" = 1 ]; then
  grep -v -E '^(\.env|\*\*/\.env|\*\*/\.env\.\*|\*\*/providers\.json)$' "$tmp/.dockerignore" \
    > "$tmp/.dockerignore.mutated" || true
  mv "$tmp/.dockerignore.mutated" "$tmp/.dockerignore"
fi

docker build -q -f "$tmp/Dockerfile.studio" -t "$tag" "$tmp" >/dev/null
# grep: 0 = found, 1 = not found, 2 = could not read. docker run: 125-127 = could not run at all.
# Only an explicit 1 is a pass; anything unexpected is a failed check, never a silent success.
set +e
docker run --rm --entrypoint sh "$tag" -c "grep -rqsF -- '$marker' /app"
rc=$?
set -e
case "$rc" in
  0) printf 'LEAK: a planted secret is inside the image\n' >&2; exit 1 ;;
  1) printf 'no planted secret in the image\n' ;;
  *) printf 'check failed: the search in the image exited %s\n' "$rc" >&2; exit 2 ;;
esac
```

```bash
chmod +x scripts/check_panel_untouched.sh scripts/studio_tag_prev.sh scripts/check_image_secrets.sh
```

Create `Dockerfile.studio`:

```dockerfile
FROM python:3.12-slim
# Studio API (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-10-studio-part1-core-design.md §4.2). The master is
# built on the Mac (`npm --prefix h3_48gb/studio/web run build`) and arrives as web/dist: npm
# registry access from alex-neuro is unverified, PyPI is (the panel image installs from it).
# Secrets never enter the image: .dockerignore drops .env / providers.json / keys / databases,
# and the live ones sit in /home/alex/Outputs/studio, mounted at run time.
RUN apt-get update \
    && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends tzdata \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
WORKDIR /app
COPY requirements-panel.txt requirements-studio.txt /app/
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r requirements-panel.txt -r requirements-studio.txt
COPY --chown=1000:1000 . /app
RUN test -f /app/h3_48gb/studio/web/dist/index.html \
    || (echo "build the master first: npm --prefix h3_48gb/studio/web run build" >&2; exit 1)
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir --no-deps -e .
ENV PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1
USER 1000:1000
ENTRYPOINT ["python", "-m", "h3_48gb.studio"]
CMD ["api", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", "8766"]
```

Create `compose.studio.yaml`:

```yaml
# Studio API (spec 2026-10-10 §4.2–4.3). The old panel's compose.yaml is not touched; this file is
# its own compose project: docker compose -f compose.studio.yaml up -d
name: h3-studio
services:
  studio-api:
    build:
      context: .
      dockerfile: Dockerfile.studio
    image: h3-studio:latest
    network_mode: host
    user: "1000:1000"
    restart: unless-stopped
    init: true
    stop_grace_period: 10s
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD", "python", "-c", "import urllib.request; urllib.request.urlopen('http://127.0.0.1:8766/api/studio/health', timeout=5)"]
      interval: 30s
      timeout: 10s
      start_period: 20s
      retries: 3
    environment:
      TZ: Europe/Moscow
      HOME: /tmp
      H3_OUTDIR: /home/alex/Outputs
      # Host names the studio answers to besides alex-neuro/localhost/127.0.0.1 (R26): the LAN IP
      STUDIO_ALLOWED_HOSTS: "192.168.100.50"
    volumes:
      - /home/alex/Outputs/studio:/home/alex/Outputs/studio
```

Modify `.dockerignore` — добавить в конец:

```text
**/node_modules
# secrets and state never go into an image (К-13); the live ones are mounted at run time
.env
**/.env
**/.env.*
**/providers.json
**/*.pem
**/*.key
**/*.sqlite
**/*.sqlite-*
```

````markdown
# Студия на alex-neuro: выкладка, обновление, откат

Спека: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-10-studio-part1-core-design.md` §4.2–4.3. Старая панель
(`/home/alex/Projects/h3-panel`, контейнер `h3-panel-h3-panel-1`, :8765) этой процедурой не
затрагивается — это доказывает отпечаток до и после (`scripts/check_panel_untouched.sh`, любой
сбой проверки — ненулевой код).

| Что | Где |
|---|---|
| Код (клон ветки) | `/home/alex/Projects/h3-studio` |
| Данные (`H3_OUTDIR=/home/alex/Outputs`) | `/home/alex/Outputs/studio/` — `studio.sqlite`, `backups/`, `assets/`, `providers.json`, `.env` |
| Страница | `http://alex-neuro:8766/studio` или `http://192.168.100.50:8766/studio` (с Мака); другие имена — 400 (R26) |
| Контейнер | `h3-studio-studio-api-1`, `network_mode: host` |

Пароля нет (R26): студия отвечает только своим именам и отклоняет изменяющие запросы с чужих
сайтов. Если LAN-IP сервера сменился — поправить `STUDIO_ALLOWED_HOSTS` в `compose.studio.yaml`.

      && sleep 25 && docker compose -f compose.studio.yaml ps'
    ssh alex-neuro 'curl -s 127.0.0.1:8766/api/studio/health; echo; \
      curl -s -o /dev/null -w "foreign host %{http_code}\n" -H "Host: evil.example" 127.0.0.1:8766/api/studio/health'

Ожидание: `prev … -> h3-studio:prev h3-studio:prev-<stamp>` (или «first deploy»); `no planted
secret in the image`; тесты зелёные; `(healthy)`; `{"ok":true,"schema_version":1}` и `foreign host 400`.

## 4. Отпечаток старой панели — после

    ssh alex-neuro 'bash /tmp/check_panel_untouched.sh' > "$TMPDIR/panel-after.txt"
    diff "$TMPDIR/panel-before.txt" "$TMPDIR/panel-after.txt" && echo "panel untouched"

## Откат

`h3-studio:prev` — образ, на котором студия работала перед этой выкладкой (`studio_tag_prev.sh`
берёт его у работающего контейнера, а не у `latest`, поэтому неудачная сборка и повтор его не
затирают; каждая выкладка оставляет ещё и `prev-<stamp>`). Миграции S0–S1 только добавляют.

    ssh alex-neuro 'cd /home/alex/Projects/h3-studio && docker tag h3-studio:prev h3-studio:latest \
      && docker compose -f compose.studio.yaml up -d --no-build'

Выключить студию: `docker compose -f compose.studio.yaml down` в `/home/alex/Projects/h3-studio`;
данные в `/home/alex/Outputs/studio` остаются.
````

### Task 6: Длительность сцены (§6.2)

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `FPS`, `FRAMES_PER_CHUNK`, `FRAME_REMAINDER` из `h3_48gb/engines/sglang_args.py:17-19` (только константы, §3.1; `parse()` не берётся).
- Produces: `duration.snap_frames(requested_s: float) -> int`, `duration.seconds(frames: int) -> float`, константы `MIN_FRAMES=73`, `MAX_FRAMES=345`, `MIN_SECONDS=3.0`, `MAX_SECONDS=15.0`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Написать падающий тест**

Create `tests/test_studio_duration.py`:

```python
"""§6.2: nearest 17n+5 grid point at 24 fps, ties up, clamped to [73, 345]."""
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from h3_48gb.studio.duration import snap_frames


@pytest.mark.parametrize("requested_s, frames", [
    (8.0, 192),      # already on the grid (Codex-13: not 197)
    (3.0, 73),       # 72 -> 73
    (10.0, 243),     # 240: 226 is 14 away, 243 is 3 away
    (4.8125, 124),   # 115.5 frames: exactly between 107 and 124 -> up
    (14.375, 345),   # the upper bound itself
    (15.0, 345),     # 360 -> 362 is above the bound -> clamped
    (0.5, 73),       # below the bound -> clamped
    (60.0, 345),
])
def test_snap(requested_s, frames):
    assert snap_frames(requested_s) == frames
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Увидеть красным**

Run: `env -u NODE_OPTIONS $PY -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider tests/test_studio_duration.py`
Expected: `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'h3_48gb.studio.duration'`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Реализация**

Create `h3_48gb/studio/duration.py`:

```python
"""Scene length (spec §6.2): `requested_s` is kept as typed; `frames` is the nearest point of the
17n+5 grid at 24 fps (ties go up), clamped to [73, 345] = 3.042–14.375 s."""
from __future__ import annotations

from fractions import Fraction

from h3_48gb.engines.sglang_args import FPS, FRAME_REMAINDER, FRAMES_PER_CHUNK

MIN_FRAMES = 73
MAX_FRAMES = 345
MIN_SECONDS = 3.0
MAX_SECONDS = 15.0


def snap_frames(requested_s: float) -> int:
    # Fraction: 4.8125 s is exactly 115.5 frames, halfway between 107 and 124; float arithmetic
    # must not decide that tie.
    target = Fraction(requested_s) * FPS
    low = FRAME_REMAINDER + FRAMES_PER_CHUNK * int((target - FRAME_REMAINDER) // FRAMES_PER_CHUNK)
    high = low + FRAMES_PER_CHUNK
    frames = low if target - low < high - target else high
    return min(max(frames, MIN_FRAMES), MAX_FRAMES)


def seconds(frames: int) -> float:
    return frames / FPS
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Зелёный** — тот же Run, `8 passed`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Мутации — увидеть красными, вернуть**

1. `if target - low < high - target` → `if target - low <= high - target` (равенство вниз). Expected: `FAILED tests/test_studio_duration.py::test_snap[4.8125-124] - assert 107 == 124`.
2. `return min(max(frames, MIN_FRAMES), MAX_FRAMES)` → `return max(frames, MIN_FRAMES)`. Expected: `test_snap[15.0-345]` — `assert 362 == 345` (и `60.0`).

### Task 7: Линтер промпта — правила S1 (§10)

**Files:**
- Create: `h3_48gb/studio/lint.py`
- Test: `tests/test_studio_lint.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `duration.MIN_SECONDS`, `duration.MAX_SECONDS` (задача 6).
- Produces:
  - `lint.LintScene` (frozen dataclass: `id, idx, mode, action_text, performance_text, sound_text, music_text, state_in_text, state_out_text, requested_s, frames, source_frame_kind, source_scene_id, cast_tags: tuple[str, ...] = ()` — кто в кадре, из SCENE_REF), `lint.LintProject(scenes: list[LintScene], portrait_phrases: dict[str, str])` с `.scene(id)`;
  - находка — `{"rule": "L1".."L12", "severity": "error"|"warning"|"info", "scene_id", "field": str | None, "message": str}`;
  - `lint.lint_scene(scene, project, limits=None) -> list[dict]` (правила в порядке `RULES`) — **никогда не бросает**: сбой правила → находка `{"rule": "lint_internal", "severity": "info", "field": None, "message": "Правило <имя> не смогло проверить сцену (<тип>)"}` (Б-1); `lint.has_errors(findings) -> bool`, `lint.TEXT_FIELDS`;
  - цепочка для L6 — все сцены, связанные `prev_last_frame` в любую сторону (разветвления и петли допустимы данными);
  - каждое правило — `(scene, project, limits) -> list[dict]` (§10), `limits` в S1 не используется (L7b/L13 — S3).

- [ ] **Step 1: Написать падающие тесты**

Create `tests/test_studio_lint.py`:

```python
"""§10: every S1 rule fires on its case, stays silent on a clean scene, and holds its bounds."""
from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import itertools

import pytest

from h3_48gb.studio import lint
from h3_48gb.studio.lint import LintProject, LintScene, lint_scene

#: Written out here, not imported: a field dropped from the linter's own list must fail a test
#: instead of silently shrinking the parametrization (review r2, Н2).
ENGLISH_FIELDS = ["action_text", "performance_text", "sound_text", "music_text", "state_in_text",
                  "state_out_text"]

PHRASE = "a woman with a loose dark braid"
ACTION = (f"{PHRASE}. At dawn she walks out from the birches onto a forest path, stops at the "
          "edge of the water and looks across the lake toward the far shore while the morning "
          "mist lifts slowly above the reeds.")
PERF = ("Her gaze searches the gaps between the trees, then her shoulders drop and her fingers "
        "loosen around the strap of her bag.")
CLEAN = LintScene(id="s1", idx=0, mode="from_frame", action_text=ACTION, performance_text=PERF,
                  sound_text="Soft footsteps on damp grass, linen rustles.", music_text="none",
                  state_in_text="", state_out_text="", requested_s=8.0, frames=192,
                  source_frame_kind="none", source_scene_id=None)
L1_MSG = ("Игра героя не прописана: нужно хотя бы два из — взгляд, мимика, руки, смена "
          "состояния")
L2_MSG = "Звук описан только отрицанием: назовите, что звучит (материал и контакт)"


def _lint(scene, others=(), phrases=None):
    project = LintProject(scenes=[scene, *others], portrait_phrases=phrases or {})
    return [(f["rule"], f["severity"], f["field"], f["message"]) for f in lint_scene(scene, project)]


def _words(n: int) -> str:
    """`n` words for action+performance together; PERF keeps L1 quiet (gaze, then, fingers)."""
    filler = n - len(PERF.split())
    return " ".join(["birch"] * filler)


def test_a_clean_scene_has_no_findings():
    assert _lint(CLEAN) == []


@pytest.mark.parametrize("over, expected", [
    ({"performance_text": ""}, [("L1", "warning", "performance_text", L1_MSG)]),
    ({"performance_text": "Her gaze is steady and calm throughout the whole long scene here."},
     [("L1", "warning", "performance_text", L1_MSG)]),
    ({"sound_text": "Silence. No music."}, [("L2", "warning", "sound_text", L2_MSG)]),
    ({"sound_text": "No music. Nothing is audible."}, [("L2", "warning", "sound_text", L2_MSG)]),
    ({"sound_text": "Nothing is audible, without voices."},
     [("L2", "warning", "sound_text", L2_MSG)]),
    ({"sound_text": "No background music."}, [("L2", "warning", "sound_text", L2_MSG)]),
    ({"sound_text": "No speech is heard."}, [("L2", "warning", "sound_text", L2_MSG)]),
    ({"sound_text": "No music. This scene is indoors."},          # review r3: a place is no sound
     [("L2", "warning", "sound_text", L2_MSG)]),
    # statements made only of fillers and hearing words name no source
    ({"sound_text": "No music. The sound is audible there."},
     [("L2", "warning", "sound_text", L2_MSG)]),
    ({"sound_text": "No voices. Any noise here is heard."},
     [("L2", "warning", "sound_text", L2_MSG)]),
    ({"sound_text": "Soft footsteps, linen rustles."},
     [("L3", "info", "sound_text", "Шаги без материала: по чему идут (трава, гравий, доски)?")]),
    ({"action_text": ACTION + " @anna smiles."},
     [("L5", "error", "action_text", "@тег работает только в режиме «Референсы»")]),
    ({"requested_s": 16.0, "frames": 345},
     [("L7a", "error", "requested_s", "Длительность 16 с вне 3–15 с")]),
    ({"requested_s": 3.0, "frames": 73},
     [("L7a", "info", "requested_s", "73 кадров = 3.042 с")]),
    ({"performance_text": PERF + " A calm neutral expression stays."},
     [("L8", "warning", "performance_text",
       "«Измерительная» формула даёт лицо-маску: опишите состояние и его смену")]),
    ({"action_text": ACTION + " Five birds rise."},
     [("L9", "warning", "action_text",
       "H3 не умеет считать больше трёх: замените число на «несколько» или уберите")]),
    ({"sound_text": "Soft footsteps on damp grass.\nmode: refs"},
     [("L10", "error", "sound_text", "Строка «mode:» в тексте: режим задаёт поле сцены")]),
    ({"source_frame_kind": "prev_last_frame", "source_scene_id": None},
     [("L12", "error", "source_frame",
       "Продолжение без предыдущей сцены: выберите другой источник кадра")]),
])
def test_rule_fires(over, expected):
    assert _lint(dataclasses.replace(CLEAN, **over)) == expected


@pytest.mark.parametrize("sound", [
    "No music and wind rustles through reeds.",        # a negation next to a real source
    "No speech, only the creak of oars in rowlocks.",
    "Nothing is audible but wind rustles through reeds.",
    "Wind rustles through reeds without music.",                   # review r3
    "Gravel under her boots, no music.",                           # an L3 material is a source
    "No music, only wind.",                                        # «wind» is the only source word
])
def test_l2_is_quiet_when_a_positive_source_is_named(sound):
    assert _lint(dataclasses.replace(CLEAN, sound_text=sound)) == []


@pytest.mark.parametrize("field_name", ENGLISH_FIELDS)
def test_l4_checks_every_english_field(field_name):
    scene = dataclasses.replace(CLEAN, **{field_name: getattr(CLEAN, field_name) + " Тишина"})
    assert [f for f in _lint(scene) if f[0] == "L4"] == [
        ("L4", "error", field_name, "Кириллица в поле промпта: H3 ждёт английский")]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("words, fires", [(39, True), (40, False), (500, False), (501, True)])
def test_l11_bounds(words, fires):
    scene = dataclasses.replace(CLEAN, action_text=_words(words))
    got = [f for f in _lint(scene) if f[0] == "L11"]
    assert got == ([("L11", "warning", "action_text",
                     f"Действие и игра — {words} слов; H3 лучше держит 40–500")] if fires else [])


def test_tag_is_fine_in_refs_mode():
    assert _lint(dataclasses.replace(CLEAN, mode="refs", action_text=ACTION + " @anna nods.")) == []


@pytest.mark.parametrize("source_idx", [1, 0])
def test_l12_source_that_is_not_earlier(source_idx):
    """A later scene and a scene at the same index are both not «earlier» (§10 L12 `>=`)."""
    other = dataclasses.replace(CLEAN, id="s2", idx=source_idx)
    scene = dataclasses.replace(CLEAN, source_frame_kind="prev_last_frame", source_scene_id="s2")
    assert _lint(scene, [other]) == [
        ("L12", "error", "source_frame",
         f"Кадр берётся из сцены {source_idx + 1}, а она идёт не раньше этой")]


def _chain(first_action=ACTION, second_action=ACTION, second_tags=("anna",), linked=True):
    first = dataclasses.replace(CLEAN, action_text=first_action, cast_tags=("anna",))
    second = dataclasses.replace(
        CLEAN, id="s2", idx=1, action_text=second_action, cast_tags=second_tags,
        source_frame_kind="prev_last_frame" if linked else "none",
        source_scene_id="s1" if linked else None)
    return first, second


L6 = ("L6", "warning", "action_text",
      f"Описание @anna расходится с другими сценами цепочки: вставьте дословно «{PHRASE}»")
DRIFTED = ACTION.replace("a loose dark braid", "a dark plait")
DRIFTED_OTHER = ACTION.replace("a loose dark braid", "short grey hair")


def _both(first, second):
    return _lint(first, [second], {"anna": PHRASE}), _lint(second, [first], {"anna": PHRASE})


def test_l6_flags_the_drifted_scene_at_either_end_of_the_chain():
    assert _both(*_chain(second_action=DRIFTED)) == ([], [L6])
    assert _both(*_chain(first_action=DRIFTED)) == ([L6], [])   # review r1, finding 8


def test_l6_the_same_description_in_both_scenes_is_not_a_difference():
    """Review r2, Н4: both scenes carry the same edited phrase — nothing differs between them."""
    assert _both(*_chain(first_action=DRIFTED, second_action=DRIFTED)) == ([], [])


def test_l6_two_different_edits_flag_the_one_unlike_the_first_scene():
    assert _both(*_chain(first_action=DRIFTED, second_action=DRIFTED_OTHER)) == ([], [L6])


def test_l6_is_quiet_when_the_hero_is_not_in_the_other_scene_or_the_chain_is_broken():
    first, second = _chain(first_action=DRIFTED, second_tags=())
    assert _lint(first, [second], {"anna": PHRASE}) == []
    first, second = _chain(first_action=DRIFTED, linked=False)
    assert _lint(first, [second], {"anna": PHRASE}) == []


def test_l6_the_same_portrait_with_a_different_action_is_not_a_finding():
    """Review r3: «… braid walks.» and «… braid sits.» describe the hero the same way."""
    first = dataclasses.replace(CLEAN, action_text=f"{PHRASE} walks to the water.",
                                cast_tags=("anna",))
    second = dataclasses.replace(CLEAN, id="s2", idx=1, action_text=f"{PHRASE} sits on the planks.",
                                 cast_tags=("anna",), source_frame_kind="prev_last_frame",
                                 source_scene_id="s1")
    assert [f for f in _both(first, second)[1] if f[0] == "L6"] == []


def _scene_with(sid, idx, kind="none", source=None, action=ACTION, tags=("anna",)):
    return dataclasses.replace(CLEAN, id=sid, idx=idx, action_text=action, cast_tags=tags,
                               source_frame_kind=kind, source_scene_id=source)


def test_l6_on_a_branch_flags_the_drifted_branch_only():
    """Review r3 blocker: s2 and s3 both continue s1 — the linter used to raise KeyError."""
    s1, s2 = _scene_with("s1", 0), _scene_with("s2", 1, "prev_last_frame", "s1")
    s3 = _scene_with("s3", 2, "prev_last_frame", "s1", action=DRIFTED)
    project = LintProject(scenes=[s1, s2, s3], portrait_phrases={"anna": PHRASE})
    got = {s.id: [f["rule"] for f in lint_scene(s, project) if f["rule"] in ("L6", "lint_internal")]
           for s in (s1, s2, s3)}
    assert got == {"s1": [], "s2": [], "s3": ["L6"]}


def test_l6_on_a_loop_does_not_hang_or_raise():
    s1 = _scene_with("s1", 0, "prev_last_frame", "s2")
    s2 = _scene_with("s2", 1, "prev_last_frame", "s1", action=DRIFTED)
    project = LintProject(scenes=[s1, s2], portrait_phrases={"anna": PHRASE})
    assert [f["rule"] for f in lint_scene(s2, project) if f["rule"] in ("L6", "lint_internal")] \
        == ["L6"]


def _graphs(n: int):
    """Every scene graph of n scenes the data model allows: each scene's frame source is none, an
    asset, `prev_last_frame` without a scene (first scene) or `prev_last_frame` to ANY other scene
    — later ones and loops included (L12 reports those, they are still storable)."""
    ids = [f"s{i}" for i in range(n)]
    choices = [[("none", None), ("asset", None), ("prev_last_frame", None),
                *[("prev_last_frame", other) for other in ids if other != me]] for me in ids]
    for sources in itertools.product(*choices):
        for pattern in range(4):          # who is on screen and whose portrait drifted
            yield [_scene_with(sid, i, kind, src,
                               action=DRIFTED if (pattern & 1 and i % 2) else ACTION,
                               tags=("anna",) if (pattern & 2 == 0 or i % 2 == 0) else ())
                   for i, (sid, (kind, src)) in enumerate(zip(ids, sources))]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("n", [1, 2, 3, 4])
def test_the_linter_never_breaks_on_an_allowed_scene_graph(n):
    """Coordinator r4: a pure function — no exception and no lint_internal on any graph of up to
    four scenes. Each rule is called directly, so lint_scene's own safety net hides nothing."""
    checked = 0
    for scenes in _graphs(n):
        project = LintProject(scenes=scenes, portrait_phrases={"anna": PHRASE})
        for scene in scenes:
            for rule in lint.RULES:
                rule(scene, project, None)
            checked += 1
    assert checked == {1: 12, 2: 2 * 4 * 16, 3: 3 * 4 * 125, 4: 4 * 4 * 1296}[n]


def test_a_rule_that_breaks_becomes_a_lint_internal_finding(monkeypatch):
    def broken(scene, project, limits):
        raise KeyError("s3")
    broken.__name__ = "l6_portrait_drift"
    monkeypatch.setattr(lint, "RULES", (lint.l1_performance, broken))
    assert lint_scene(CLEAN, LintProject([CLEAN])) == [
        {"rule": "lint_internal", "severity": "info", "scene_id": "s1", "field": None,
         "message": "Правило l6_portrait_drift не смогло проверить сцену (KeyError)"}]
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Реализация**

Create `h3_48gb/studio/lint.py`:

```python
"""Prompt linter (spec §10). Slice S1 carries L1–L6, L7a, L8–L12; L7b/L7d/L13/L14 need templates,
keyframes and takes and arrive with them. Each rule is `(scene, project, limits) -> findings`."""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from h3_48gb.studio.duration import MAX_SECONDS, MIN_SECONDS

TEXT_FIELDS = ("action_text", "performance_text", "sound_text", "music_text", "state_in_text",
               "state_out_text")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LintScene:
    id: str
    idx: int
    mode: str
    action_text: str
    performance_text: str
    sound_text: str
    music_text: str
    state_in_text: str
    state_out_text: str
    requested_s: float
    frames: int
    source_frame_kind: str
    source_scene_id: str | None
    cast_tags: tuple[str, ...] = ()        # who is on screen (SCENE_REF), in ref order


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LintProject:
    scenes: list[LintScene]
    portrait_phrases: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)   # tag -> phrase

    def scene(self, scene_id: str | None) -> LintScene | None:
        return next((s for s in self.scenes if s.id == scene_id), None)


def finding(rule: str, severity: str, scene: LintScene, field_name: str | None,
            message: str) -> dict:
    return {"rule": rule, "severity": severity, "scene_id": scene.id, "field": field_name,
            "message": message}


_GAZE = re.compile(r"\b(gaze|eyes|looks?|looking|glanc\w*|stares?|blinks?)\b", re.I)
_FACE = re.compile(r"\b(face|facial|smil\w*|frown\w*|brows?|lips|jaw|cheeks?|mouth)\b", re.I)
_HANDS = re.compile(r"\b(hands?|fingers?|fists?|palms?|grip\w*|clutch\w*)\b", re.I)
_CHANGE = re.compile(r"\b(then|until|gradually|shifts?|gives way|softens?|hardens?|becomes?)\b",
                     re.I)


def l1_performance(scene, project, limits):
    text = scene.performance_text
    hits = sum(bool(p.search(text)) for p in (_GAZE, _FACE, _HANDS, _CHANGE))
    if not text.strip() or hits < 2:
        return [finding("L1", "warning", scene, "performance_text",
                        "Игра героя не прописана: нужно хотя бы два из — взгляд, мимика, руки, "
                        "смена состояния")]
    return []


#: §10 L2, narrowed (ruling R21a): «the sound is not described physically». It fires only when the
#: sound text negates something ("no …", "without …", "nothing …", "silence", "not … heard") AND
#: names no sound at all — no word from the sound vocabulary below and no material from L3. A
#: negation next to a described source ("Wind rustles through reeds without music.") is fine.
#: The vocabulary is deliberately the reliable part: a source outside it can produce a spurious
#: warning, never an error; the list grows when the scenarist's real texts show a miss.
_NEGATION = re.compile(r"\b(?:no|without|nothing|none|silence|silent|mute|muted|soundless)\b|"
                       r"\bnot\b[^.;,]*\b(?:heard|audible)\b", re.I)
_SOUND_WORDS = re.compile(
    r"\b(?:wind|rain|water|waves?|stream|river|splash\w*|drip\w*|rustl\w*|creak\w*|crunch\w*|"
    r"crackl\w*|thud\w*|tap\w*|knock\w*|click\w*|clatter\w*|clink\w*|ring\w*|hum\w*|buzz\w*|"
    r"whistl\w*|hiss\w*|roar\w*|rumbl\w*|patter\w*|swish\w*|squeak\w*|scrap\w*|thump\w*|"
    r"footsteps?|footfalls?|steps?|heels?|breath\w*|sigh\w*|laugh\w*|voices?|whisper\w*|"
    r"murmur\w*|chirp\w*|birds?|birdsong|bells?|engines?|traffic|thunder|fire|flames?|leaves|"
    r"oars?|rowlocks?|bark\w*|song|sing\w*|hums?)\b", re.I)


#: "no background music", "without voices": the negated words are not a described source.
_NEGATED_PHRASE = re.compile(r"\b(?:no|without)\s+(?:[a-z-]+\s+)?[a-z-]+", re.I)


def l2_mute_sound(scene, project, limits):
    text = scene.sound_text
    if not _NEGATION.search(text):
        return []
    rest = _NEGATED_PHRASE.sub(" ", text)
    if _SOUND_WORDS.search(rest) or _MATERIAL.search(rest):
        return []
    return [finding("L2", "warning", scene, "sound_text",
                    "Звук описан только отрицанием: назовите, что звучит (материал и контакт)")]


_STEPS = re.compile(r"\b(steps?|footsteps?|footfalls?|walk\w*)\b", re.I)
_MATERIAL = re.compile(r"\b(grass|gravel|wood\w*|stone\w*|concrete|sand|snow|mud|leaves|floor\w*|"
                       r"tiles?|asphalt|carpet|water|metal|dirt|earth|moss|planks?|boards?)\b",
                       re.I)


def l3_material(scene, project, limits):
    if _STEPS.search(scene.sound_text) and not _MATERIAL.search(scene.sound_text):
        return [finding("L3", "info", scene, "sound_text",
                        "Шаги без материала: по чему идут (трава, гравий, доски)?")]
    return []


_CYRILLIC = re.compile(r"[А-Яа-яЁё]")


def l4_cyrillic(scene, project, limits):
    return [finding("L4", "error", scene, name, "Кириллица в поле промпта: H3 ждёт английский")
            for name in TEXT_FIELDS if _CYRILLIC.search(getattr(scene, name))]


_TAG = re.compile(r"@[\w-]+")


def l5_tag_mode(scene, project, limits):
    if scene.mode not in ("text", "from_frame"):
        return []
    return [finding("L5", "error", scene, name,
                    "@тег работает только в режиме «Референсы»")
            for name in TEXT_FIELDS if _TAG.search(getattr(scene, name))]


def _chain(project: LintProject, scene: LintScene) -> list[LintScene]:
    """Every scene connected to `scene` through `prev_last_frame` links, in either direction —
    a branch (two scenes continuing the same one) and a loop are both allowed by the data and
    must not break the linter. Always contains `scene`; ordered by `idx`."""
    by_id = {s.id: s for s in project.scenes}
    neighbours: dict[str, set[str]] = {s.id: set() for s in project.scenes}
    for s in project.scenes:
        if s.source_frame_kind == "prev_last_frame" and s.source_scene_id in by_id:
            neighbours[s.id].add(s.source_scene_id)
            neighbours[s.source_scene_id].add(s.id)
    seen, todo = {scene.id}, [scene.id]
    neighbours.setdefault(scene.id, set())
    while todo:
        for nxt in neighbours.get(todo.pop(), ()):
            if nxt not in seen:
                seen.add(nxt)
                todo.append(nxt)
    return sorted((by_id.get(i, scene) for i in seen), key=lambda s: (s.idx, s.id))


def _content(text: str) -> set[str]:
    return {w for w in re.findall(r"[a-z]+", text.lower()) if len(w) > 2}


def _portrait(scene: LintScene, phrase: str) -> frozenset[str]:
    """What this scene says of the hero's looks, narrowed to what can be told reliably (ruling
    R21): the words of the card's phrase that the scene's text still carries. The action around
    the portrait («walks» / «sits») never enters it."""
    return frozenset(_content(phrase) & _content(scene.action_text))


def l6_portrait_drift(scene, project, limits):
    """§10 L6, narrowed (R21): within the scenes of one `prev_last_frame` chain that have the hero
    on screen (SCENE_REF), a scene whose portrait words differ from another's is flagged — the
    reference is the card's full phrase if some scene keeps all its words, else the first scene
    of the chain. The same portrait with a different action is not a finding. Drift that only
    ADDS words absent from the card («… and a red scarf») is not detected."""
    chain = _chain(project, scene)
    out = []
    for tag in scene.cast_tags:
        phrase = project.portrait_phrases.get(tag, "")
        present = [s for s in chain if tag in s.cast_tags]
        if not phrase or len(present) < 2:
            continue
        portraits = {s.id: _portrait(s, phrase) for s in present}
        full = frozenset(_content(phrase))
        reference = full if full in portraits.values() else portraits[present[0].id]
        if portraits[scene.id] != reference:
            out.append(finding("L6", "warning", scene, "action_text",
                               f"Описание @{tag} расходится с другими сценами цепочки: вставьте "
                               f"дословно «{phrase}»"))
    return out


def l7a_duration(scene, project, limits):
    if not (MIN_SECONDS <= scene.requested_s <= MAX_SECONDS):
        return [finding("L7a", "error", scene, "requested_s",
                        f"Длительность {scene.requested_s:g} с вне 3–15 с")]
    if scene.frames / 24 != scene.requested_s:
        return [finding("L7a", "info", scene, "requested_s",
                        f"{scene.frames} кадров = {scene.frames / 24:.3f} с")]
    return []


_MEASURED = re.compile(r"calm neutral expression|neutral expression|expressionless|"
                       r"lips stay closed|mouth stays closed", re.I)


def l8_measured(scene, project, limits):
    return [finding("L8", "warning", scene, name,
                    "«Измерительная» формула даёт лицо-маску: опишите состояние и его смену")
            for name in ("action_text", "performance_text")
            if _MEASURED.search(getattr(scene, name))]


_BIG_NUMBER = re.compile(r"\b([4-9]|\d{2,}|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|"
                         r"dozens?|hundreds?)\b", re.I)


def l9_counting(scene, project, limits):
    if _BIG_NUMBER.search(scene.action_text):
        return [finding("L9", "warning", scene, "action_text",
                        "H3 не умеет считать больше трёх: замените число на «несколько» или "
                        "уберите")]
    return []


_MODE_LINE = re.compile(r"^\s*mode\s*:", re.I | re.M)


def l10_mode_line(scene, project, limits):
    return [finding("L10", "error", scene, name, "Строка «mode:» в тексте: режим задаёт поле сцены")
            for name in TEXT_FIELDS if _MODE_LINE.search(getattr(scene, name))]


def l11_length(scene, project, limits):
    words = len(f"{scene.action_text} {scene.performance_text}".split())
    if words < 40 or words > 500:
        return [finding("L11", "warning", scene, "action_text",
                        f"Действие и игра — {words} слов; H3 лучше держит 40–500")]
    return []


def l12_chain(scene, project, limits):
    if scene.source_frame_kind != "prev_last_frame":
        return []
    source = project.scene(scene.source_scene_id)
    if source is None:
        return [finding("L12", "error", scene, "source_frame",
                        "Продолжение без предыдущей сцены: выберите другой источник кадра")]
    if source.idx >= scene.idx:
        return [finding("L12", "error", scene, "source_frame",
                        f"Кадр берётся из сцены {source.idx + 1}, а она идёт не раньше этой")]
    return []


RULES = (l1_performance, l2_mute_sound, l3_material, l4_cyrillic, l5_tag_mode, l6_portrait_drift,
         l7a_duration, l8_measured, l9_counting, l10_mode_line, l11_length, l12_chain)


def lint_scene(scene: LintScene, project: LintProject, limits: dict | None = None) -> list[dict]:
    """Never raises: the linter runs after an operation has committed, so a rule that breaks on
    some scene graph must become a finding, not a 500 for a change that was saved."""
    out: list[dict] = []
    for rule in RULES:
        try:
            out.extend(rule(scene, project, limits))
        except Exception as err:                 # a bug in a rule, reported where it is seen
            out.append(finding("lint_internal", "info", scene, None,
                               f"Правило {rule.__name__} не смогло проверить сцену "
                               f"({type(err).__name__})"))
    return out


def has_errors(findings: list[dict]) -> bool:
    return any(f["severity"] == "error" for f in findings)
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Мутация на каждое правило (гейт S1) — увидеть красной, вернуть**

Каждая строка — отдельный прогон `tests/test_studio_lint.py`; все проверены красными 21.10 (у L3 — 15 failed: чистая сцена тоже со «steps»). Строки с пометкой К-8/К-9 — мутации, которые ревью назвало непойманными в редакции 1.

| Правило | Мутация в `lint.py` | Красный тест |
|---|---|---|
| L1 | `or hits < 2` → `or hits < 1` | `test_rule_fires` (одна категория игры) |
| L2 (Б-3) | `rest = _NEGATED_PHRASE.sub(" ", text)` → `rest = text` | `test_rule_fires` («Nothing is audible, without voices.», «No voices. Any noise here is heard.» — отрицаемые «voices» приняты за источник) |
| L2 (Б-3) | `if _SOUND_WORDS.search(rest) or _MATERIAL.search(rest):` → `if _SOUND_WORDS.search(rest):` | `test_l2_is_quiet_when_a_positive_source_is_named[Gravel under her boots, no music.]` |
| L2 (Б-3, r4) | в `_SOUND_WORDS` убрать `wind\|` | `test_l2_is_quiet_when_a_positive_source_is_named[No music, only wind.]` — `wind` там единственное слово-источник |
| L3 | удалить `and not _MATERIAL.search(scene.sound_text)` | `test_a_clean_scene_has_no_findings` и др. |
| L4 (Н2) | из `TEXT_FIELDS` убрать `"state_in_text",` | `test_l4_checks_every_english_field[state_in_text]` — список полей в тесте литеральный |
| L4 (К-9) | в `l4_cyrillic` `for name in TEXT_FIELDS` → `for name in ("music_text",)` | `test_l4_checks_every_english_field` — 5 полей |
| L5 | `("text", "from_frame")` → `("text",)` | `test_rule_fires` (`@anna` в `from_frame`) |
| L6 (Б-1) | в `_chain` удалить строку `neighbours[s.source_scene_id].add(s.id)` (связь только «вверх») | `test_l6_flags_the_drifted_scene_at_either_end_of_the_chain` (проверено r4; генератор и тест ветви при этой мутации проходят — они ловят исключения, а не потерю ребра) |
| L6 (Б-3) | `return frozenset(_content(phrase) & _content(scene.action_text))` → `return frozenset(_content(scene.action_text))` | `test_l6_the_same_portrait_with_a_different_action_is_not_a_finding` и др. |
| L6 (Н4) | `reference = full if full in portraits.values() else portraits[present[0].id]` → `reference = full` | `test_l6_the_same_description_in_both_scenes_is_not_a_difference` и `…two_different_edits…` |
| lint_internal (Б-1) | в `lint_scene` `except Exception as err:` → `except ZeroDivisionError as err:` | `test_a_rule_that_breaks_becomes_a_lint_internal_finding` (и в задаче 10 — API-тест) |
| L6 | `present = [s for s in chain if tag in s.cast_tags]` → `present = list(chain)` | `test_l6_is_quiet_when_the_hero_is_not_in_the_other_scene…` |
| L7a | `MIN_SECONDS <= scene.requested_s` → `MIN_SECONDS < scene.requested_s` | `test_rule_fires` (3 с) |
| L8 | `("action_text", "performance_text")` в `l8_measured` → `("action_text",)` | `test_rule_fires` (L8) |
| L9 | `four\|five\|six` → `four\|six` | `test_rule_fires` (L9) |
| L10 | `re.I \| re.M)` → `re.I)` | `test_rule_fires` (L10) |
| L11 (К-9) | `if words < 40 or words > 500:` → `if words < 40:` | `test_l11_bounds[501-True]` |
| L11 (К-9) | `words < 40` → `words < 39` | `test_l11_bounds[39-True]` |
| L12 (К-9) | `if source.idx >= scene.idx:` → `if source.idx > scene.idx:` | `test_l12_source_that_is_not_earlier[0]` |
| L12 | `if source is None:` → `if False:` | `test_rule_fires` (L12, `AttributeError`) |

### Task 8: Дверь к LLM — `provider.chat_with_schema`, `ProviderLlm`, GPU-flock

**Files:**
- Modify: `h3_48gb/provider.py` (дописать в конец файла, после `test_provider`)
- Create: `h3_48gb/studio/llm.py`
- Test: `tests/test_studio_llm.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `provider._chat_turn`, `provider.load_providers(root)`, `provider.load_env(root)`, `provider.shares_gpu(cfg)` (`provider.py:283`, `:269`, `:309`, `:673`), `provider.ProviderError`; `tests/_fake_llama._FakeLlama`.
- Produces:
  - `provider.chat_with_schema(cfg: dict, env: dict, messages: list[dict], schema: dict, retry_reminder: str) -> dict` (R2);
  - `llm.Llm` (Protocol: `label: str`, `json_turn(messages, schema, retry_reminder) -> dict`);
  - `llm.ProviderLlm(home: Path, lock_path: Path, *, provider_name: str | None = None, lock_timeout_s: float = 5.0)` — провайдер из `<home>/providers.json` (`active` или `provider_name`), ключи из `<home>/.env`; нет провайдера / нет токена → `StudioError(503, "llm_not_configured")`; `ProviderError` → `StudioError(502, "llm_failed", message=SAFE_PROVIDER_MESSAGES[code], detail={"provider", "provider_code"})` **без текста провайдера и без `__cause__`** (R25, К-1); провайдер с `shares_gpu` → `gpu_lock` на `lock_path`, занят дольше `lock_timeout_s` → `StudioError(409, "gpu_busy")`;
  - `llm.SAFE_PROVIDER_MESSAGES: dict[str, str]`; `llm.validate_reply(reply, schema)` — jsonschema-проверка ответа модели по схеме студии независимо от режима провайдера, нарушение → `StudioError(502, "llm_failed", "Модель ответила не по схеме", {"schema", "path", "rule"})` (К-6; используют задачи 9 и 11);
  - `llm.gpu_lock(path, *, enabled: bool, timeout_s: float)` — контекст.

- [ ] **Step 1: Написать падающие тесты**

Create `tests/test_studio_llm.py`:

```python
"""The studio's LLM door: the wire carries the caller's schema; config and GPU-lock refusals."""
from __future__ import annotations

import fcntl
import json
import os

import pytest
from _fake_llama import _FakeLlama

from h3_48gb import provider
from h3_48gb.studio.errors import StudioError
from h3_48gb.studio.llm import ProviderLlm, gpu_lock

SCHEMA = {"name": "studio_probe", "schema": {
    "type": "object", "properties": {"x": {"type": "string"}}, "required": ["x"],
    "additionalProperties": False}}


def _reply(obj):
    return {"choices": [{"message": {"content": json.dumps(obj)}, "finish_reason": "stop"}]}


def test_chat_with_schema_sends_the_callers_schema(tmp_path):
    fake = _FakeLlama(chat_payload=_reply({"x": "ok"}))
    try:
        cfg = {"type": "openai", "base_url": f"http://127.0.0.1:{fake.port}", "model": "m"}
        got = provider.chat_with_schema(cfg, {}, [{"role": "user", "content": "hi"}], SCHEMA,
                                        "only JSON")
    finally:
        fake.close()
    assert got == {"x": "ok"}
    (req,) = fake.requests
    assert req["body"]["response_format"] == {"type": "json_schema", "json_schema": SCHEMA}


def _home_with(tmp_path, providers: dict):
    home = tmp_path / "studio"
    home.mkdir()
    (home / "providers.json").write_text(json.dumps(providers))
    return home


def test_provider_llm_talks_to_the_active_provider(tmp_path):
    fake = _FakeLlama(chat_payload=_reply({"x": "да"}))
    try:
        home = _home_with(tmp_path, {"active": "f", "providers": {"f": {
            "type": "openai", "base_url": f"http://127.0.0.1:{fake.port}", "model": "m",
            "shares_gpu": False}}})
        llm = ProviderLlm(home, tmp_path / "gpu-mac.lock")
        assert llm.json_turn([{"role": "user", "content": "q"}], SCHEMA, "r") == {"x": "да"}
        assert llm.label == "f:m"
    finally:
        fake.close()


def test_no_provider_is_a_named_503(tmp_path):
    llm = ProviderLlm(tmp_path / "empty", tmp_path / "gpu-mac.lock")
    with pytest.raises(StudioError) as err:
        llm.json_turn([], SCHEMA, "r")
    assert (err.value.status, err.value.code) == (503, "llm_not_configured")


def test_provider_failure_is_a_named_502(tmp_path):
    home = _home_with(tmp_path, {"active": "f", "providers": {"f": {
        "type": "openai", "base_url": "http://127.0.0.1:9", "model": "m", "shares_gpu": False}}})
    with pytest.raises(StudioError) as err:
        ProviderLlm(home, tmp_path / "gpu-mac.lock").json_turn([], SCHEMA, "r")
    assert (err.value.status, err.value.code, err.value.message, err.value.detail) == \
        (502, "llm_failed", "Сценарист недоступен: провайдер не ответил",
         {"provider": "f", "provider_code": "chat_unreachable"})


def test_gpu_lock_held_elsewhere_is_409_gpu_busy(tmp_path):
    lock = tmp_path / "gpu-mac.lock"
    fd = os.open(lock, os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT)
    fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
    try:
        with pytest.raises(StudioError) as err:
            with gpu_lock(lock, enabled=True, timeout_s=0.3):
                pass
        assert (err.value.status, err.value.code) == (409, "gpu_busy")
        with gpu_lock(lock, enabled=False, timeout_s=0.3):
            pass                        # a provider off the GPU never waits for the render
    finally:
        os.close(fd)


def test_a_gpu_sharing_provider_waits_for_the_render_lock(tmp_path):
    """§8.4 local-mac: the request never reaches a provider that shares the GPU with the render."""
    fake = _FakeLlama(chat_payload=_reply({"x": "ok"}))
    lock = tmp_path / "gpu-mac.lock"
    fd = os.open(lock, os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT)
    fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
    try:
        home = _home_with(tmp_path, {"active": "f", "providers": {"f": {
            "type": "openai", "base_url": f"http://127.0.0.1:{fake.port}", "model": "m",
            "shares_gpu": True}}})
        with pytest.raises(StudioError) as err:
            ProviderLlm(home, lock, lock_timeout_s=0.3).json_turn([], SCHEMA, "r")
        assert (err.value.status, err.value.code, fake.requests) == (409, "gpu_busy", [])
    finally:
        os.close(fd)
        fake.close()


def test_a_provider_without_its_token_is_a_named_503(tmp_path):
    home = _home_with(tmp_path, {"active": "caila", "providers": {"caila": {
        "type": "openai", "base_url": "http://127.0.0.1:9", "model": "m",
        "api_key_env": "CAILA_API_KEY"}}})

SECRET = "sk-SYNTHETIC-SECRET-0042"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("payload, code", [
    # the model's own text is not JSON and quotes the key: provider's bad_model_json echoes it
    ({"choices": [{"message": {"content": f"not json, key {SECRET}"}}]}, "bad_model_json"),
    # a 200 envelope that is not a completion, echoing the key with and without "Bearer"
    ({"error": {"message": f"Authorization: Bearer {SECRET}; key={SECRET}"}}, "bad_provider_reply"),
])
def test_provider_text_never_leaves_the_studio(tmp_path, caplog, payload, code):
    """No provider message, raw model reply or token reaches the error body or the log."""
    fake = _FakeLlama(chat_payload=payload)
    try:
        home = _home_with(tmp_path, {"active": "f", "providers": {"f": {
            "type": "openai", "base_url": f"http://127.0.0.1:{fake.port}", "model": "m",
            "api_key_env": "KEY", "shares_gpu": False}}})
        (home / ".env").write_text(f"KEY={SECRET}\n")
        with pytest.raises(StudioError) as err:
            ProviderLlm(home, tmp_path / "gpu-mac.lock").json_turn([], SCHEMA, "r")
    finally:
        fake.close()
    assert err.value.body() == {"error": {
        "code": "llm_failed", "message": SAFE_MESSAGES[code],
        "detail": {"provider": "f", "provider_code": code}}}
    assert err.value.__cause__ is None
    assert SECRET not in caplog.text

                 "bad_provider_reply": "Сценарист недоступен: провайдер вернул ошибку"}
```

Append to `h3_48gb/provider.py` (в самый конец файла):

```python


def chat_with_schema(cfg: dict, env: dict, messages: list[dict], schema: dict,
                     retry_reminder: str) -> dict:
    """One turn shaped by the caller's own `schema` (`{"name", "schema"}`, the `PROMPT_SCHEMA`
    form) -- the studio's scenarist and cast card (studio spec §9.1). Same wire, retry and four
    named failures as `chat`/`chat_scenario`; only the schema and the reminder are the caller's."""
    return _chat_turn(cfg, env, messages, schema, retry_reminder)
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/llm.py`:

```python
"""The studio's door to an LLM (spec §9.1, §8.4). `Llm` is what ops and the director depend on;
`ProviderLlm` is the real one over `provider.chat_with_schema`, configured by
`<outdir>/studio/providers.json` + `.env` (the same roster format as the old panel)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import fcntl
import os
import time
from contextlib import contextmanager
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Protocol

import jsonschema

from h3_48gb import provider
from h3_48gb.studio.errors import StudioError


class Llm(Protocol):
    label: str

    def json_turn(self, messages: list[dict], schema: dict, retry_reminder: str) -> dict: ...


def validate_reply(reply, schema: dict) -> None:
    """Check a model's reply against the studio's own schema, whatever the provider's mode.

    `provider._chat_turn` checks the schema itself only for `json_object` routes and trusts the
    server for `json_schema` (tests/test_provider.py pins that), so `{}` can arrive here. The
    refusal names the place, never the value: the reply is the model's text."""
    error = jsonschema.exceptions.best_match(
        jsonschema.Draft202012Validator(schema["schema"]).iter_errors(reply))
    if error is not None:
        path = "/" + "/".join(str(p) for p in error.absolute_path)
        raise StudioError(502, "llm_failed", "Модель ответила не по схеме",
                          {"schema": schema["name"], "path": path, "rule": error.validator})


@contextmanager
def gpu_lock(path: Path, *, enabled: bool, timeout_s: float):
    """§8.4 `local-mac`: an LLM that shares the GPU waits for the same flock the worker holds
    while rendering — at most `timeout_s`, then 409 `gpu_busy` instead of hanging the request."""
    if not enabled:
        yield
        return
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    fd = os.open(path, os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT, 0o644)
    try:
        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout_s
        while True:
            try:
                fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
                break
            except BlockingIOError:
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    raise StudioError(409, "gpu_busy",
                                      "Сценарист ждёт видеокарту: её занял рендер",
                                      {"lock": str(path)}) from None
                time.sleep(0.1)
        try:
            yield
        finally:
            fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
    finally:
        os.close(fd)


#: What the owner reads when a provider call fails. Only these fixed texts leave the studio: the
#: provider's own message can carry the model's raw reply (`bad_model_json` quotes 400 characters
#: of it unredacted, provider.py) or an echoed token, and it goes into API bodies and reports.
SAFE_PROVIDER_MESSAGES = {
    "chat_unreachable": "Сценарист недоступен: провайдер не ответил",
    "bad_provider_reply": "Сценарист недоступен: провайдер вернул ошибку",
    "chat_truncated": "Ответ сценариста обрезан лимитом вывода провайдера (max_tokens)",
    "bad_model_json": "Модель ответила не по схеме",
    "bad_provider_config": "Неверная настройка провайдера в studio/providers.json",
}


class ProviderLlm:
    def __init__(self, home: Path, lock_path: Path, *, provider_name: str | None = None,
                 lock_timeout_s: float = 5.0):
        self.home = home
        self.lock_path = lock_path
        self.provider_name = provider_name
        self.lock_timeout_s = lock_timeout_s

    def _config(self) -> tuple[str, dict]:
        roster = provider.load_providers(self.home)
        name = self.provider_name or roster["active"]
        cfg = roster["providers"].get(name) if name else None
        if cfg is None:
            raise StudioError(503, "llm_not_configured",
                              "Сценарист не настроен: нет провайдера в studio/providers.json",
                              {"provider": name})
        if not cfg["available"]:
            raise StudioError(503, "llm_not_configured",
                              f"Сценарист не настроен: {cfg['reason']}", {"provider": name})
        return name, cfg

    @property
    def label(self) -> str:
        try:
            name, cfg = self._config()
        except StudioError:
            return "none"
        return f"{name}:{cfg.get('model', cfg.get('preset', ''))}"

    def json_turn(self, messages: list[dict], schema: dict, retry_reminder: str) -> dict:
        name, cfg = self._config()
        with gpu_lock(self.lock_path, enabled=provider.shares_gpu(cfg) is True,
                      timeout_s=self.lock_timeout_s):
            try:
                return provider.chat_with_schema(cfg, provider.load_env(self.home), messages,
                                                 schema, retry_reminder)
            except provider.ProviderError as err:
                message = SAFE_PROVIDER_MESSAGES.get(err.code, "Сценарист не ответил")
                # `from None`: the provider's text must not ride along as __cause__ either.
                raise StudioError(502, "llm_failed", message,
                                  {"provider": name, "provider_code": err.code}) from None
```

Append to `tests/_studio_fixtures.py`:

```python


#: A real 2x2 PNG (Pillow decodes it) — upload tests must not pass on a signature alone.
TINY_PNG = bytes.fromhex(
    "89504e470d0a1a0a0000000d4948445200000002000000020802000000fdd49a73000000124944415478"
    "9c6314777fc6c0c0c0c40006000e8b01487dba26d80000000049454e44ae426082")


class ScriptedLlm:
    """An `llm.Llm` that answers from a list. Records each call's messages and the whole schema
    dict; a call past the end of the script fails the test instead of inventing an answer."""

    label = "scripted"

    def __init__(self, replies: list):
        self.replies = list(replies)
        self.calls: list[dict] = []

    def json_turn(self, messages: list[dict], schema: dict, retry_reminder: str) -> dict:
        self.calls.append({"messages": messages, "schema": schema})
        if not self.replies:
            raise AssertionError("unexpected LLM call")
        reply = self.replies.pop(0)
        if isinstance(reply, BaseException):
            raise reply
        return reply
```

Create `tests/test_studio_cast.py`:

```python
"""Slice S1 step 2: upload a hero picture (O6, И7) and add the hero to the cast (O5)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import io
import json

from _studio_fixtures import TINY_PNG, ScriptedLlm, make_client

from h3_48gb.studio.errors import StudioError

# Literals, not imports from the code under test (review r2, Н2).
CAST_PROMPT = (
    'You write the English card of one character for the MiniMax H3 video model.\n'
    '\n'
    'Input: a JSON object with `tag` (a short latin name) and `description_ru` (how the owner\n'
    'describes the character, in Russian).\n'
    '\n'
    'Answer with one JSON object:\n'
    '- `description_en` — the same character in English, 1–3 sentences: age, build, face, hair,\n'
    '  clothing, one or two distinctive details. Concrete and visual. No names of real people, no\n'
    '  mood words, no Cyrillic.\n'
    '- `portrait_phrase` — one sentence of at most 30 words that identifies the character on screen\n'
    '  (for example "a woman in her thirties with a loose dark braid and a long grey linen dress").\n'
    '  It will be pasted verbatim into every scene where the character appears, so it must read as\n'
    '  a noun phrase that can start a sentence.\n'
)
CAST_SCHEMA = {"name": "studio_cast_card", "schema": {
    "type": "object",
    "properties": {"description_en": {"type": "string"}, "portrait_phrase": {"type": "string"}},
    "required": ["description_en", "portrait_phrase"],
    "additionalProperties": False}}

PNG_SHA256 = "d2c8571330616ad74a60d54379918d803dca8083d62e9fac6c41e619c0ce1d4b"


def _project(client):
    return client.post("/api/studio/projects", json={"title": "Лес", "frame_format": "16:9"}).json()


def _upload(client, data=TINY_PNG, name="hero.png", kind=None):
    form = {"kind": kind} if kind else {}
    return client.post("/api/studio/assets", files={"file": (name, io.BytesIO(data))}, data=form)


def test_upload_stores_the_bytes_once_and_serves_them(tmp_path):
    client = make_client(tmp_path, llm=ScriptedLlm([]))
    r = _upload(client)
    assert r.status_code == 201
    asset = r.json()
    assert asset == {"id": asset["id"], "kind": "image", "sha256": PNG_SHA256, "bytes": 75,
                     "mime": "image/png", "url": f"/api/studio/assets/{asset['id']}/file"}
    served = client.get(asset["url"])
    assert (served.content, served.headers["content-type"]) == (TINY_PNG, "image/png")
    second = _upload(client).json()
    assert second["id"] != asset["id"]
    assert client.get(asset["url"]).content == TINY_PNG


def test_upload_refuses_what_is_not_a_picture(tmp_path):
    client = make_client(tmp_path, llm=ScriptedLlm([]))
    r = _upload(client, data=b"%PDF-1.7 ...", name="x.pdf")
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"]) == (400, "asset_type_invalid")
    r = _upload(client, kind="audio")
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["detail"]) == (400, {"sniffed": "image",
                                                                  "kind": "audio"})


def test_a_right_signature_on_broken_content_is_refused(tmp_path):
    """Review finding 7: signature + zeros, and a real PNG cut in half, are not pictures."""
    client = make_client(tmp_path, llm=ScriptedLlm([]))
    for data in (b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n" + b"\x00" * 64, TINY_PNG[:50]):
        r = _upload(client, data=data)
        assert r.json() == {"error": {"code": "asset_corrupt",
                                      "message": "Картинка повреждена или не читается",
                                      "detail": {}}}
        assert r.status_code == 400
    assert list((tmp_path / "out" / "studio" / "assets").glob("*")) == []


def test_upload_over_25_mb_is_413(tmp_path):
    client = make_client(tmp_path, llm=ScriptedLlm([]))
    r = _upload(client, data=TINY_PNG + b"\x00" * (25 * 1024 * 1024))
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"]) == (413, "asset_too_large")


def test_an_iphone_heic_photo_is_refused_with_the_formats_named(tmp_path):
    """Review Focus 1: the owner's phone shoots HEIC."""
    client = make_client(tmp_path, llm=ScriptedLlm([]))
    heic = b"\x00\x00\x00\x18ftypheic\x00\x00\x00\x00mif1heic" + b"\x00" * 32
    r = _upload(client, data=heic, name="IMG_0001.HEIC")
    assert r.status_code == 400
    assert r.json()["error"] == {"code": "asset_type_invalid",
                                 "message": "Нужна картинка PNG, JPEG или WebP", "detail": {}}


def test_cast_card_from_the_llm(tmp_path):
    llm = ScriptedLlm([{"description_en": "A woman in her thirties, dark braid, grey linen dress.",
                        "portrait_phrase": "a woman in her thirties with a loose dark braid."}])
    client = make_client(tmp_path, llm=llm)
    p = _project(client)
    asset = _upload(client).json()
    r = client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/cast", json={
        "expected_revision": 1, "tag": "anna", "description_ru": "женщина лет тридцати, коса",
        "assets": [{"asset_id": asset["id"], "role": "image"}]})
    assert r.status_code == 201
    body = r.json()
    assert body["project"]["revision"] == 2
    assert body["cast_member"] == {
        "id": body["cast_member"]["id"], "tag": "anna",
        "description_ru": "женщина лет тридцати, коса",
        "description_en": "A woman in her thirties, dark braid, grey linen dress.",
        "portrait_phrase": "a woman in her thirties with a loose dark braid",
        "assets": [{"asset_id": asset["id"], "role": "image", "ord": 1, "url": asset["url"]}]}
    (call,) = llm.calls
    assert call["schema"] == CAST_SCHEMA
    assert call["messages"] == [
        {"role": "system", "content": CAST_PROMPT},
        {"role": "user", "content": json.dumps({"tag": "anna",
                                                "description_ru": "женщина лет тридцати, коса"},
                                               ensure_ascii=False)}]
    assert client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()["cast"] == [body["cast_member"]]


def test_a_cast_reply_off_the_schema_is_502_not_500(tmp_path):
    """Review finding 6: a json_schema route may hand back anything; the studio checks it."""
    for reply, path in (({}, "/"), ({"description_en": "A woman.", "portrait_phrase": 7},
                                    "/portrait_phrase")):
        client = make_client(tmp_path / path.strip("/").replace("_", ""), llm=ScriptedLlm([reply]))
        p = _project(client)
        r = client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/cast", json={
            "expected_revision": 1, "tag": "anna", "description_ru": "ж"})
        assert r.status_code == 502
        assert r.json()["error"]["code"] == "llm_failed"
        assert r.json()["error"]["message"] == "Модель ответила не по схеме"
        assert r.json()["error"]["detail"]["path"] == path


def test_manual_card_skips_the_llm(tmp_path):
    llm = ScriptedLlm([])
    client = make_client(tmp_path, llm=llm)
    p = _project(client)
    r = client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/cast", json={
        "expected_revision": 1, "tag": "anna", "description_ru": "женщина",
        "description_en": "A woman.", "portrait_phrase": "a woman with a braid"})
    assert r.status_code == 201
    assert (r.json()["cast_member"]["description_en"], llm.calls) == ("A woman.", [])


def test_cyrillic_in_a_manual_card_is_refused(tmp_path):
    client = make_client(tmp_path, llm=ScriptedLlm([]))
    p = _project(client)
    r = client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/cast", json={
        "expected_revision": 1, "tag": "anna", "description_ru": "женщина",
        "description_en": "Женщина.", "portrait_phrase": "a woman"})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"], r.json()["error"]["detail"]) == \
        (400, "cast_card_invalid", {"field": "description_en"})


def test_llm_failure_is_502_and_nothing_is_written(tmp_path):
    client = make_client(tmp_path, llm=ScriptedLlm([StudioError(502, "llm_failed", "down")]))
    p = _project(client)
    r = client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/cast", json={
        "expected_revision": 1, "tag": "anna", "description_ru": "женщина"})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"]) == (502, "llm_failed")
    snap = client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()
    assert (snap["cast"], snap["project"]["revision"], snap["last_seq"]) == ([], 1, 1)


def test_same_tag_twice_is_409_and_stale_revision_is_409(tmp_path):
    client = make_client(tmp_path, llm=ScriptedLlm([]))
    p = _project(client)
    card = {"tag": "anna", "description_ru": "ж", "description_en": "A woman.",
            "portrait_phrase": "a woman"}
    assert client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/cast",
                       json={"expected_revision": 1, **card}).status_code == 201
    stale = client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/cast", json={"expected_revision": 1, **card})
    assert stale.json()["error"]["code"] == "revision_conflict"
    taken = client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/cast", json={"expected_revision": 2, **card})
    assert (taken.status_code, taken.json()["error"]["code"]) == (409, "cast_tag_taken")


def test_an_unknown_asset_cannot_be_the_hero_picture(tmp_path):
    client = make_client(tmp_path, llm=ScriptedLlm([]))
    p = _project(client)
    r = client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/cast", json={
        "expected_revision": 1, "tag": "anna", "description_ru": "ж", "description_en": "A woman.",
        "portrait_phrase": "a woman", "assets": [{"asset_id": "a_nope", "role": "image"}]})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"]) == (400, "asset_unknown")
    assert client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()["cast"] == []


def test_the_provider_text_does_not_reach_the_api_body_or_the_log(tmp_path, caplog):
    """Review finding 1 end to end: a real ProviderLlm over _FakeLlama, a synthetic key."""
    from _fake_llama import _FakeLlama
    secret = "sk-SYNTHETIC-SECRET-0042"
    fake = _FakeLlama(chat_payload={"choices": [{"message": {"content": f"oops {secret}"}}]})
    try:
        home = tmp_path / "out" / "studio"
        home.mkdir(parents=True)
        (home / "providers.json").write_text(json.dumps({"active": "f", "providers": {"f": {
            "type": "openai", "base_url": f"http://127.0.0.1:{fake.port}", "model": "m",
            "api_key_env": "KEY", "shares_gpu": False}}}))
        (home / ".env").write_text(f"KEY={secret}\n")
        client = make_client(tmp_path)
        p = _project(client)
        r = client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/cast", json={
            "expected_revision": 1, "tag": "anna", "description_ru": "ж"})
    finally:
        fake.close()
    assert r.status_code == 502
    assert secret not in r.text
    assert secret not in caplog.text
    events = client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()["last_seq"]
    assert events == 1                          # nothing was journaled for the failed call
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/assets.py`:

```python
"""Uploaded files (spec §7 O6, И7): stored once under `<outdir>/studio/assets/<id>.<ext>`, never
overwritten; the row is immutable (trigger in migration 001)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import io
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path

from PIL import Image

from h3_48gb.studio import db, events, ids
from h3_48gb.studio.errors import StudioError

MAX_ASSET_BYTES = 25 * 1024 * 1024

#: Slice S1 takes pictures only (the hero's photo); voice, video and tracks arrive later (§2).
_PIL_FORMATS = {"png": "PNG", "jpg": "JPEG", "webp": "WEBP"}


def sniff(data: bytes) -> tuple[str, str, str] | None:
    """(family, ext, mime) by leading bytes."""
    if data.startswith(b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n"):
        return "image", "png", "image/png"
    if data.startswith(b"\xff\xd8\xff"):
        return "image", "jpg", "image/jpeg"
    if data[:4] == b"RIFF" and data[8:12] == b"WEBP":
        return "image", "webp", "image/webp"
    return None


def check_decodes(data: bytes, ext: str) -> None:
    """A right signature on broken content is still a broken picture: decode it fully."""
    try:
        with Image.open(io.BytesIO(data)) as image:
            image.load()
            decoded_as = image.format
    except Exception:                       # Pillow raises half a dozen types for bad input
        raise StudioError(400, "asset_corrupt", "Картинка повреждена или не читается") from None
    if decoded_as != _PIL_FORMATS[ext]:
        raise StudioError(400, "asset_corrupt", "Картинка повреждена или не читается",
                          {"decoded_as": decoded_as})


def asset_out(row: sqlite3.Row) -> dict:
    return {"id": row["id"], "kind": row["kind"], "sha256": row["sha256"], "bytes": row["bytes"],
            "mime": row["mime"], "url": f"/api/studio/assets/{row['id']}/file"}


def import_asset(conn: sqlite3.Connection, home: Path, *, data: bytes,
                 kind: str | None = None) -> dict:
    if len(data) > MAX_ASSET_BYTES:
        raise StudioError(413, "asset_too_large", "Файл больше 25 МБ",
                          {"bytes": len(data), "max": MAX_ASSET_BYTES})
    sniffed = sniff(data)
    if sniffed is None:
        raise StudioError(400, "asset_type_invalid", "Нужна картинка PNG, JPEG или WebP")
    family, ext, mime = sniffed
    if kind is not None and kind != family:
        raise StudioError(400, "asset_type_invalid", f"Файл — {family}, а ожидался {kind}",
                          {"sniffed": family, "kind": kind})
    check_decodes(data, ext)
    asset_id = ids.new_id("a")
    folder = home / "assets"
    folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path = folder / f"{asset_id}.{ext}"
    with open(path, "xb") as fh:          # "x": never overwrite an existing asset file (И7)
        fh.write(data)
    try:
        with db.tx(conn):
            conn.execute(
                "INSERT INTO asset (id, kind, path, sha256, bytes, mime, origin_kind, created_at) "
                "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'upload', ?)",
                (asset_id, family, str(path), hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), len(data), mime,
                 ids.now_iso()))
            events.emit(conn, "asset_imported", payload={"asset_id": asset_id, "kind": family})
            return asset_out(conn.execute("SELECT * FROM asset WHERE id = ?",
                                          (asset_id,)).fetchone())
    except BaseException:
        path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise


def asset_file(conn: sqlite3.Connection, asset_id: str) -> tuple[Path, str]:
    row = conn.execute("SELECT path, mime FROM asset WHERE id = ?", (asset_id,)).fetchone()
    if row is None or not Path(row["path"]).is_file():
        raise StudioError(404, "asset_not_found", "Файл не найден", {"asset_id": asset_id})
    return Path(row["path"]), row["mime"]
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/director/prompts/cast.md`:

```markdown
You write the English card of one character for the MiniMax H3 video model.

Input: a JSON object with `tag` (a short latin name) and `description_ru` (how the owner
describes the character, in Russian).

Answer with one JSON object:
- `description_en` — the same character in English, 1–3 sentences: age, build, face, hair,
  clothing, one or two distinctive details. Concrete and visual. No names of real people, no
  mood words, no Cyrillic.
- `portrait_phrase` — one sentence of at most 30 words that identifies the character on screen
  (for example "a woman in her thirties with a loose dark braid and a long grey linen dress").
  It will be pasted verbatim into every scene where the character appears, so it must read as
  a noun phrase that can start a sentence.
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/director/cast.py`:

```python
"""Cast card: Russian description -> English description + portrait phrase (spec §7 O5, §9.1)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
from pathlib import Path

from h3_48gb.studio.errors import StudioError
from h3_48gb.studio.llm import Llm, validate_reply

PROMPT = Path(__file__).parent / "prompts" / "cast.md"
_CYRILLIC = re.compile(r"[А-Яа-яЁё]")
MAX_PORTRAIT_WORDS = 30

CAST_SCHEMA = {
    "name": "studio_cast_card",
    "schema": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {"description_en": {"type": "string"},
                       "portrait_phrase": {"type": "string"}},
        "required": ["description_en", "portrait_phrase"],
        "additionalProperties": False,
    },
}


def check_card(description_en: str, portrait_phrase: str) -> None:
    """The same checks for a model's card and for one typed by hand."""
    for name, text in (("description_en", description_en), ("portrait_phrase", portrait_phrase)):
        if not text.strip():
            raise StudioError(400, "cast_card_invalid", f"Пустое поле {name}", {"field": name})
        if _CYRILLIC.search(text):
            raise StudioError(400, "cast_card_invalid", f"Кириллица в {name}: нужен английский",
                              {"field": name})
    if len(portrait_phrase.split()) > MAX_PORTRAIT_WORDS:
        raise StudioError(400, "cast_card_invalid",
                          f"Портретная фраза длиннее {MAX_PORTRAIT_WORDS} слов",
                          {"field": "portrait_phrase"})


def describe(llm: Llm, *, tag: str, description_ru: str) -> dict:
    messages = [{"role": "system", "content": PROMPT.read_text(encoding="utf-8")},
                {"role": "user", "content": json.dumps({"tag": tag,
                                                        "description_ru": description_ru},
                                                       ensure_ascii=False)}]
    card = llm.json_turn(messages, CAST_SCHEMA,
                         "Ответ строго одним JSON-объектом {description_en, portrait_phrase}.")
    validate_reply(card, CAST_SCHEMA)
    description_en = card["description_en"].strip()
    portrait_phrase = card["portrait_phrase"].strip().rstrip(".")
    try:
        check_card(description_en, portrait_phrase)
    except StudioError as err:
        raise StudioError(502, "llm_failed", f"Карточка героя от модели не годится: {err.message}",
                          err.detail) from err
    return {"description_en": description_en, "portrait_phrase": portrait_phrase}
```

```python
import sqlite3

from h3_48gb.studio import db, events, ids
```

Modify `h3_48gb/studio/ops.py` — заменить функцию `snapshot` целиком на:

```python
def snapshot(conn: sqlite3.Connection, project_id: str) -> dict:
    """O3: the project with everything the master draws, and the `last_seq` it is consistent
    with (И10) — read in one snapshot so an event committed in between cannot be lost. Scenes
    arrive with task 10; takes and tasks in S2–S3."""
    with db.read(conn):
        project = project_out(_project_row(conn, project_id))
        return {"project": project, "scenes": [],
                "cast": [cast_out(conn, r) for r in _cast_rows(conn, project_id)],
                "takes": [], "tasks": [], "last_seq": events.last_seq(conn)}
```

Append to `h3_48gb/studio/ops.py`:

```python


# --- slice S1: cast (task 9) ------------------------------------------------------------------


def _cast_rows(conn: sqlite3.Connection, project_id: str) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:
    return conn.execute("SELECT * FROM cast_member WHERE project_id = ? ORDER BY created_at, id",
                        (project_id,)).fetchall()


def cast_out(conn: sqlite3.Connection, row: sqlite3.Row) -> dict:
    assets = conn.execute("SELECT asset_id, role, ord FROM cast_asset WHERE cast_id = ? "
                          "ORDER BY ord", (row["id"],)).fetchall()
    return {"id": row["id"], "tag": row["tag"], "description_ru": row["description_ru"],
            "description_en": row["description_en"], "portrait_phrase": row["portrait_phrase"],
            "assets": [{"asset_id": a["asset_id"], "role": a["role"], "ord": a["ord"],
                        "url": f"/api/studio/assets/{a['asset_id']}/file"} for a in assets]}


def add_cast(conn: sqlite3.Connection, project_id: str, *, expected_revision: int, tag: str,
             description_ru: str, assets: list[dict], description_en: str | None,
             portrait_phrase: str | None, llm: Llm) -> dict:
    """O5. The English card comes from the owner (both fields) or from the LLM — outside the
    write transaction, so a slow model never holds the database."""
    row = _project_row(conn, project_id)
    _check_revision(row, expected_revision, project_out(row))
    if description_en is not None and portrait_phrase is not None:
        cast_card.check_card(description_en, portrait_phrase)
        card = {"description_en": description_en.strip(),
                "portrait_phrase": portrait_phrase.strip().rstrip(".")}
    else:
        card = cast_card.describe(llm, tag=tag, description_ru=description_ru)
    cast_id = ids.new_id("c")
    with db.tx(conn):
        row = _project_row(conn, project_id)
        _check_revision(row, expected_revision, project_out(row))
        if conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM cast_member WHERE project_id = ? AND tag = ?",
                        (project_id, tag)).fetchone():
            raise StudioError(409, "cast_tag_taken", f"@{tag} уже есть в кастинге", {"tag": tag})
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO cast_member (id, project_id, tag, description_ru, description_en, "
            "portrait_phrase, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
            (cast_id, project_id, tag, description_ru, card["description_en"],
             card["portrait_phrase"], ids.now_iso()))
        for ord_, item in enumerate(assets, start=1):
            asset = conn.execute("SELECT kind FROM asset WHERE id = ?",
                                 (item["asset_id"],)).fetchone()
            if asset is None or asset["kind"] != "image":
                raise StudioError(400, "asset_unknown",
                                  f"Файл {item['asset_id']} не подходит для роли {item['role']}",
                                  {"asset_id": item["asset_id"], "role": item["role"]})
            conn.execute("INSERT INTO cast_asset (cast_id, asset_id, role, ord) "
                         "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)", (cast_id, item["asset_id"], item["role"], ord_))
        conn.execute("UPDATE project SET revision = revision + 1 WHERE id = ?", (project_id,))
        events.emit(conn, "cast_added", project_id=project_id, payload={"tag": tag})
        member = cast_out(conn, conn.execute("SELECT * FROM cast_member WHERE id = ?",
                                             (cast_id,)).fetchone())
        return {"cast_member": member, "project": project_out(_project_row(conn, project_id))}
```

```python
# --- slice S1 ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Tag = Field(pattern=r"^[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{0,31}$")


class CastAssetIn(_Body):
    asset_id: str
    role: Literal["image"]        # voice references arrive with audio uploads (after S1)


class CastCreate(_Body):
    expected_revision: int
    tag: str = Tag
    description_ru: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=4000)
    assets: list[CastAssetIn] = Field(default_factory=list, max_length=8)
    description_en: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=4000)
    portrait_phrase: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=400)
```

```python
    @app.post(f"{api}/assets", status_code=201)
    async def import_asset(file: UploadFile = File(...), kind: str | None = Form(default=None),
                           conn=Depends(get_conn)):
```
