### Task 10: Сцены — O7, O8, O9, И1, конфликт ревизий

**Files:**
- Modify: `h3_48gb/studio/ops.py` (импорты, `snapshot`, дописать блок), `h3_48gb/studio/schemas.py` (дописать), `h3_48gb/studio/app.py` (маршруты), `h3_48gb/studio/web/openapi.json` (перегенерировать)
- Test: `tests/test_studio_scenes.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `duration.snap_frames`, `duration.seconds` (6), `lint.*` (7), `ops._cast_rows`, `ops.cast_out` (9), `ops._project_row`, `ops._check_revision`, `ops.project_out` (2).
- Produces:
  - O7 `PUT /api/studio/projects/{id}/scenes` `{expected_revision, scenes: [SceneIn]}` → `{"project", "scenes", "diff": {"added", "unchanged", "changed", "moved", "deleted"}, "lint": {scene_id: [finding]}}`; ошибки `revision_conflict` (409), `scene_unknown`, `cast_unknown`, `source_scene_unknown`, `asset_unknown`, `args_invalid` (400); событие `scenes_replaced {diff}`;
  - O8 `PATCH /api/studio/scenes/{id}` `{expected_revision, <поля>?}` → `{"scene", "cascade": [], "lint": [finding]}`; ошибки `revision_conflict` (409, `detail.current` — сцена), `scene_not_found` (404); событие `scene_updated {revision, fields}` (только если что-то изменилось);
  - O9 `POST /api/studio/projects/{id}/lint` `{scene_ids?}` → `{"findings": {scene_id: [finding]}}`;
  - `ScenesReplace.scenes` — **без верхнего предела** (§4.4, К-4; от мусора защищает лимит тела, R23);
  - `SceneIn {scene_id?, mode, action_text, performance_text, sound_text, music_text, state_in_text, state_out_text, requested_s (0 < x ≤ 60), seed? (1..2³¹−1), source_frame {kind: none|prev_last_frame|asset, scene_id?, asset_id?}, refs: [tag]}` — `refs` = кто в кадре (R6);
  - сцена в ответах — `{"id", "idx", "revision", "state_version", "mode", <6 текстов>, "requested_s", "frames", "duration_s", "seed", "source_frame": {"kind", "scene_id", "asset_id"}, "refs": [tag], "review_state", "approved_sketch_take_id", "approved_final_take_id"}`;
  - `ops.replace_scenes(conn, project_id, *, expected_revision, scenes, event_kind="scenes_replaced", event_payload=None, in_tx_hook=None)` — `event_kind`/`event_payload`/`in_tx_hook` нужны задаче 11 (O26 идёт тем же путём);
  - `ops.update_scene(conn, scene_id, *, expected_revision, changes: dict)`, `ops.lint_project(conn, project_id, scene_ids=None)` (передаёт линтеру `cast_tags` сцен из SCENE_REF), `ops.scene_out`, `ops._new_seed() -> int` (тесты подменяют), `ops.FINGERPRINT_FIELDS`;
  - снимок O3: `scenes` заполнен (живые, по `idx`).
  - Правило источника кадра: `prev_last_frame` без `scene_id` — предыдущая сцена массива (O7) или предыдущая живая по `idx` (O8); для первой сцены — `None` (L12 error, R5).

- [ ] **Step 1: Написать падающие тесты**

Create `tests/test_studio_scenes.py`:

```python
"""Slice S1 scenes: O7 matching by scene_id, O8 edits, И1 revisions, 409 revision_conflict."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest
from _studio_fixtures import ScriptedLlm, make_client

from h3_48gb.studio import ops

ACTION = ("A woman walks out from the birches onto a forest path, stops at the edge of the water "
          "and looks across the lake toward the far shore while the morning mist lifts slowly "
          "above the reeds.")
PERF = ("Her gaze searches the gaps between the trees, then her shoulders drop and her fingers "
        "loosen around the strap of her bag.")


def _scene(**over):
    base = {"mode": "from_frame", "action_text": ACTION, "performance_text": PERF,
            "sound_text": "Soft footsteps on damp grass.", "music_text": "none",
            "requested_s": 8.0, "source_frame": {"kind": "none"}, "refs": []}
    return {**base, **over}


@pytest.fixture
def client(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    seeds = iter(range(101, 200))
    monkeypatch.setattr(ops, "_new_seed", lambda: next(seeds))
    return make_client(tmp_path, llm=ScriptedLlm([]))


def _project(client):
    return client.post("/api/studio/projects", json={"title": "Лес", "frame_format": "16:9"}).json()


def _put(client, pid, rev, scenes):
    return client.put(f"/api/studio/projects/{pid}/scenes",
                      json={"expected_revision": rev, "scenes": scenes})


def _with_cast(client, pid, rev=1, tag="anna"):
    client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{pid}/cast", json={
        "expected_revision": rev, "tag": tag, "description_ru": "ж",
        "description_en": "A woman.", "portrait_phrase": "a woman with a braid"})


def test_put_creates_scenes_in_array_order_with_derived_sources(client):
    p = _project(client)
    r = _put(client, p["id"], 1, [_scene(), _scene(source_frame={"kind": "prev_last_frame"},
                                               requested_s=4.8125)])
    assert r.status_code == 200
    body = r.json()
    one, two = body["scenes"]
    assert body["project"]["revision"] == 2
    assert body["diff"] == {"added": [one["id"], two["id"]], "unchanged": [], "changed": [],
                            "moved": [], "deleted": []}
    assert [(s["idx"], s["revision"], s["frames"], s["duration_s"], s["seed"]) for s in (one, two)] \
        == [(0, 1, 192, 8.0, 101), (1, 1, 124, 5.167, 102)]
    assert two["source_frame"] == {"kind": "prev_last_frame", "scene_id": one["id"],
                                   "asset_id": None}
    assert client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()["scenes"] == body["scenes"]


def test_put_matches_by_id_moves_changes_and_soft_deletes(client):
    p = _project(client)
    a, b, c = _put(client, p["id"], 1, [_scene(), _scene(action_text=ACTION + " B"),
                                        _scene(action_text=ACTION + " C")]).json()["scenes"]
    # new order: C, A(edited), new; B deleted
    r = _put(client, p["id"], 2, [
        {**_scene(action_text=ACTION + " C"), "scene_id": c["id"]},
        {**_scene(action_text=ACTION + " A2"), "scene_id": a["id"]},
        _scene(action_text=ACTION + " D")]).json()
    new_id = r["scenes"][2]["id"]
    assert r["diff"] == {"added": [new_id], "unchanged": [], "changed": [a["id"]],
                         "moved": [c["id"]], "deleted": [b["id"]]}
    assert [(s["id"], s["idx"], s["revision"], s["seed"]) for s in r["scenes"]] == [
        (c["id"], 0, 1, c["seed"]), (a["id"], 1, 2, a["seed"]), (new_id, 2, 1, 104)]


def test_reorder_that_changes_the_frame_source_is_a_content_change(client):
    p = _project(client)
    a, b, c = _put(client, p["id"], 1, [
        _scene(), _scene(source_frame={"kind": "prev_last_frame"}, action_text=ACTION + " B"),
        _scene(source_frame={"kind": "prev_last_frame"}, action_text=ACTION + " C")]).json()["scenes"]
    r = _put(client, p["id"], 2, [
        {**_scene(), "scene_id": a["id"]},
        {**_scene(source_frame={"kind": "prev_last_frame"}, action_text=ACTION + " C"),
         "scene_id": c["id"]},
        {**_scene(source_frame={"kind": "prev_last_frame"}, action_text=ACTION + " B"),
         "scene_id": b["id"]}]).json()
    assert r["diff"]["changed"] == [c["id"], b["id"]]
    assert r["diff"]["unchanged"] == [a["id"]]
    assert [(s["id"], s["revision"], s["source_frame"]["scene_id"]) for s in r["scenes"]] == [
        (a["id"], 1, None), (c["id"], 2, a["id"]), (b["id"], 2, c["id"])]


def test_put_with_an_unknown_scene_id_writes_nothing(client):
    p = _project(client)
    r = _put(client, p["id"], 1, [{**_scene(), "scene_id": "s_nope"}])
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"]) == (400, "scene_unknown")
    snap = client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()
    assert (snap["scenes"], snap["project"]["revision"], snap["last_seq"]) == ([], 1, 1)


def test_put_with_a_stale_project_revision_is_409(client):
    p = _project(client)
    _put(client, p["id"], 1, [_scene()])
    r = _put(client, p["id"], 1, [_scene()])
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"]) == (409, "revision_conflict")
    assert r.json()["error"]["detail"]["current"]["revision"] == 2


@pytest.mark.parametrize("field, value, stored", [
    ("mode", "refs", {"mode": "refs"}),
    ("action_text", ACTION + " Then she smiles.", {"action_text": ACTION + " Then she smiles."}),
    ("performance_text", PERF + " Then she laughs.", {"performance_text": PERF + " Then she laughs."}),
    ("sound_text", "Wind in the reeds.", {"sound_text": "Wind in the reeds."}),
    ("music_text", "A single cello.", {"music_text": "A single cello."}),
    ("requested_s", 10.0, {"requested_s": 10.0, "frames": 243, "duration_s": 10.125}),
    ("seed", 7, {"seed": 7}),
    ("source_frame", {"kind": "prev_last_frame"},
     {"source_frame": {"kind": "prev_last_frame", "scene_id": "<first>", "asset_id": None}}),
    ("refs", ["anna"], {"refs": ["anna"]}),
])
def test_each_fingerprint_field_bumps_the_revision_and_is_stored(client, field, value, stored):
    """И1: +1 revision, the new value in the answer AND on a fresh read, project untouched."""
    p = _project(client)
    _with_cast(client, p["id"])
    first, second = _put(client, p["id"], 2, [_scene(), _scene()]).json()["scenes"]
    expected = {**second, **json.loads(json.dumps(stored).replace("<first>", first["id"])),
                "revision": 2}
    r = client.patch(f"/api/studio/scenes/{second['id']}",
                     json={"expected_revision": 1, field: value})
    assert r.status_code == 200
    assert r.json()["scene"] == expected
    snap = client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()
    assert snap["scenes"][1] == expected
    assert snap["project"]["revision"] == 3


@pytest.mark.parametrize("field, value", [
    ("state_in_text", "She stands at the birches."), ("state_out_text", "She faces the lake."),
    ("requested_s", 8.1),        # same 192 frames: requested_s alone is not in the fingerprint
])

def test_patch_with_a_stale_scene_revision_is_409_with_the_current_scene(client):
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Реализация**

Modify `h3_48gb/studio/ops.py` — блок импортов стал:

```python
import secrets
import sqlite3

from h3_48gb.studio import db, duration, events, ids, lint
from h3_48gb.studio.director import cast as cast_card
from h3_48gb.studio.errors import StudioError
from h3_48gb.studio.llm import Llm
```

Modify `h3_48gb/studio/ops.py` — заменить функцию `snapshot` целиком на:

```python
def snapshot(conn: sqlite3.Connection, project_id: str) -> dict:
    """O3: the project with everything the master draws, and the `last_seq` it is consistent
    with (И10) — read in one snapshot so an event committed in between cannot be lost."""
    with db.read(conn):
        project = project_out(_project_row(conn, project_id))
        return {"project": project,
                "scenes": [scene_out(conn, r) for r in _live_scenes(conn, project_id)],
                "cast": [cast_out(conn, r) for r in _cast_rows(conn, project_id)],
                "takes": [], "tasks": [], "last_seq": events.last_seq(conn)}
```

Append to `h3_48gb/studio/ops.py`:

```python


# --- slice S1: scenes and lint (task 10) ------------------------------------------------------

TEXT_FIELDS = lint.TEXT_FIELDS
#: И1: a change to any of these (or to the scene's refs) is a new content revision.
FINGERPRINT_FIELDS = ("mode", "action_text", "performance_text", "sound_text", "music_text",
                      "frames", "seed", "source_frame_kind", "source_scene_id",
                      "source_frame_asset_id")
STORED_FIELDS = ("mode", *TEXT_FIELDS, "requested_s", "frames", "seed", "source_frame_kind",
                 "source_scene_id", "source_frame_asset_id")


def _new_seed() -> int:
    return secrets.randbelow(2**31 - 1) + 1


def _refs(conn: sqlite3.Connection, scene_id: str) -> list[tuple[str, int, str]]:
    return [tuple(r) for r in conn.execute(
        "SELECT cast_id, ord, role FROM scene_ref WHERE scene_id = ? ORDER BY ord", (scene_id,))]


def _ref_tags(conn: sqlite3.Connection, scene_id: str) -> list[str]:
    return [r[0] for r in conn.execute(
        "SELECT c.tag FROM scene_ref r JOIN cast_member c ON c.id = r.cast_id "
        "WHERE r.scene_id = ? ORDER BY r.ord", (scene_id,))]


def scene_out(conn: sqlite3.Connection, row: sqlite3.Row) -> dict:
    return {"id": row["id"], "idx": row["idx"], "revision": row["revision"],
            "state_version": row["state_version"], "mode": row["mode"],
            **{f: row[f] for f in TEXT_FIELDS},
            "requested_s": row["requested_s"], "frames": row["frames"],
            "duration_s": round(duration.seconds(row["frames"]), 3), "seed": row["seed"],
            "source_frame": {"kind": row["source_frame_kind"], "scene_id": row["source_scene_id"],
                             "asset_id": row["source_frame_asset_id"]},
            "refs": _ref_tags(conn, row["id"]), "review_state": row["review_state"],
            "approved_sketch_take_id": row["approved_sketch_take_id"],
            "approved_final_take_id": row["approved_final_take_id"]}


def _live_scenes(conn: sqlite3.Connection, project_id: str) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:
    return conn.execute("SELECT * FROM scene WHERE project_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL "
                        "ORDER BY idx", (project_id,)).fetchall()


def _scene_row(conn: sqlite3.Connection, scene_id: str) -> sqlite3.Row:
    row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM scene WHERE id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL",
                       (scene_id,)).fetchone()
    if row is None:
        raise StudioError(404, "scene_not_found", "Сцена не найдена", {"scene_id": scene_id})
    return row


def _fingerprint(fields, refs: list[tuple[str, int, str]]) -> tuple:
    return (*(fields[name] for name in FINGERPRINT_FIELDS), tuple(refs))


def _resolve_source(conn: sqlite3.Connection, source: dict, *, prev_id: str | None,
                    allowed_scene_ids: set[str]) -> tuple[str, str | None, str | None]:
    kind = source["kind"]
    if kind == "none":
        return "none", None, None
    if kind == "prev_last_frame":
        scene_id = source.get("scene_id") or prev_id
        if scene_id is not None and scene_id not in allowed_scene_ids:
            raise StudioError(400, "source_scene_unknown", "Источник кадра — не сцена этого проекта",
                              {"scene_id": scene_id})
        return "prev_last_frame", scene_id, None
    asset_id = source.get("asset_id")
    row = conn.execute("SELECT kind FROM asset WHERE id = ?", (asset_id,)).fetchone()
    if row is None or row["kind"] not in ("image", "frame"):
        raise StudioError(400, "asset_unknown", "Кадр для сцены не найден",
                          {"asset_id": asset_id})
    return "asset", None, asset_id


def _refs_from_tags(cast_by_tag: dict, tags: list[str]) -> list[tuple[str, int, str]]:
    if len(tags) != len(set(tags)):
        raise StudioError(400, "args_invalid", "Тег повторяется в референсах сцены",
                          {"refs": tags})
    out = []
    for ord_, tag in enumerate(tags, start=1):
        member = cast_by_tag.get(tag)
        if member is None:
            raise StudioError(400, "cast_unknown", f"В кастинге нет @{tag}", {"tag": tag})
        out.append((member["id"], ord_, "person"))
    return out


def _write_refs(conn: sqlite3.Connection, scene_id: str, refs: list[tuple[str, int, str]]):
    conn.execute("DELETE FROM scene_ref WHERE scene_id = ?", (scene_id,))
    conn.executemany("INSERT INTO scene_ref (scene_id, cast_id, ord, role) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                     [(scene_id, *r) for r in refs])


def lint_project(conn: sqlite3.Connection, project_id: str,
                 scene_ids: list[str] | None = None) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
    """O9. Findings for every live scene (or the listed ones), keyed by scene id."""
    rows = _live_scenes(conn, project_id)
    project = lint.LintProject(
        scenes=[lint.LintScene(**{k: r[k] for k in (
            "id", "idx", "mode", *TEXT_FIELDS, "requested_s", "frames", "source_frame_kind",
            "source_scene_id")}, cast_tags=tuple(_ref_tags(conn, r["id"]))) for r in rows],
        portrait_phrases={c["tag"]: c["portrait_phrase"] for c in _cast_rows(conn, project_id)})
    wanted = set(scene_ids) if scene_ids is not None else {r["id"] for r in rows}
    unknown = wanted - {r["id"] for r in rows}
    if unknown:
        raise StudioError(404, "scene_not_found", "Сцена не найдена",
                          {"scene_ids": sorted(unknown)})
    return {s.id: lint.lint_scene(s, project) for s in project.scenes if s.id in wanted}


def update_scene(conn: sqlite3.Connection, scene_id: str, *, expected_revision: int,
                 changes: dict) -> dict:
    """O8. Content edits bump `scene.revision` only when the fingerprint changes (И1); the
    project revision is not touched, so editing one scene never stales another's plan (§7)."""
    with db.tx(conn):
        row = _scene_row(conn, scene_id)
        if row["revision"] != expected_revision:
            raise StudioError(409, "revision_conflict",
                              "Сцена изменилась в другом окне — обновите и повторите",
                              {"current": scene_out(conn, row)})
        fields = {name: row[name] for name in STORED_FIELDS}
        for name in ("mode", *TEXT_FIELDS, "seed"):
            if changes.get(name) is not None:
                fields[name] = changes[name]
        if changes.get("requested_s") is not None:
            fields["requested_s"] = changes["requested_s"]
            fields["frames"] = duration.snap_frames(changes["requested_s"])
        live = _live_scenes(conn, row["project_id"])
        if changes.get("source_frame") is not None:
            earlier = [r["id"] for r in live if r["idx"] < row["idx"]]
            kind, src_scene, src_asset = _resolve_source(
                conn, changes["source_frame"], prev_id=earlier[-1] if earlier else None,
                allowed_scene_ids={r["id"] for r in live} - {scene_id})
            fields.update(source_frame_kind=kind, source_scene_id=src_scene,
                          source_frame_asset_id=src_asset)
        old_refs = _refs(conn, scene_id)
        refs = old_refs
        if changes.get("refs") is not None:
            cast_by_tag = {c["tag"]: c for c in _cast_rows(conn, row["project_id"])}
            refs = _refs_from_tags(cast_by_tag, changes["refs"])
        changed = sorted(n for n in STORED_FIELDS if fields[n] != row[n])
        if refs != old_refs:
            changed.append("refs")
        if changed:
            bump = 0 if _fingerprint(fields, refs) == _fingerprint(row, old_refs) else 1
            sets = ", ".join(f"{name} = ?" for name in STORED_FIELDS)
            conn.execute(f"UPDATE scene SET {sets}, revision = revision + ? WHERE id = ?",
                         (*(fields[n] for n in STORED_FIELDS), bump, scene_id))
            if refs != old_refs:
                _write_refs(conn, scene_id, refs)
            events.emit(conn, "scene_updated", project_id=row["project_id"], scene_id=scene_id,
                        payload={"revision": row["revision"] + bump, "fields": changed})
        out = scene_out(conn, _scene_row(conn, scene_id))
    findings = lint_project(conn, row["project_id"], [scene_id])[scene_id]
    # `cascade` (И11) is the list of scenes whose approval this edit revoked; approvals exist
    # from slice S3 on, so in S1 it is always empty.
    return {"scene": out, "cascade": [], "lint": findings}


def replace_scenes(conn: sqlite3.Connection, project_id: str, *, expected_revision: int,
                   scenes: list[dict], event_kind: str = "scenes_replaced",
                   event_payload: dict | None = None,
                   in_tx_hook=None) -> dict:
    """O7 (and O26 through it). Identity is `scene_id`; array order is `idx`; a scene missing
    from the array is soft-deleted; a matched scene keeps its revision unless its fingerprint
    changed (И1). A `prev_last_frame` source without an explicit scene is the previous scene of
    the array, so a reorder that changes it is a content change (§7 «Сопоставление сцен»)."""
    with db.tx(conn):
        prow = _project_row(conn, project_id)
        _check_revision(prow, expected_revision, project_out(prow))
        live = {r["id"]: r for r in _live_scenes(conn, project_id)}
        given = [s.get("scene_id") for s in scenes]
        known = [sid for sid in given if sid is not None]
        if len(known) != len(set(known)):
            raise StudioError(400, "args_invalid", "scene_id повторяется", {"scene_ids": known})
        for sid in known:
            if sid not in live:
                raise StudioError(400, "scene_unknown", "Такой сцены в проекте нет",
                                  {"scene_id": sid})
        final_ids = [sid or ids.new_id("s") for sid in given]
        cast_by_tag = {c["tag"]: c for c in _cast_rows(conn, project_id)}
        diff: dict[str, list[str]] = {"added": [], "unchanged": [], "changed": [], "moved": [],
                                      "deleted": []}
        now = ids.now_iso()
        # Free the live (project_id, idx) slots before writing the new order.
        conn.execute("UPDATE scene SET idx = -1 - idx WHERE project_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL",
                     (project_id,))
        for idx, (sid, item) in enumerate(zip(final_ids, scenes)):
            kind, src_scene, src_asset = _resolve_source(
                conn, item["source_frame"], prev_id=final_ids[idx - 1] if idx else None,
                allowed_scene_ids=set(final_ids) - {sid})
            refs = _refs_from_tags(cast_by_tag, item["refs"])
            old = live.get(sid)
            fields = {"mode": item["mode"], **{f: item[f] for f in TEXT_FIELDS},
                      "requested_s": item["requested_s"],
                      "frames": duration.snap_frames(item["requested_s"]),
                      "seed": item["seed"] or (old["seed"] if old else _new_seed()),
                      "source_frame_kind": kind, "source_scene_id": src_scene,
                      "source_frame_asset_id": src_asset}
            names = ", ".join(STORED_FIELDS)
            marks = ", ".join("?" for _ in STORED_FIELDS)
            if old is None:
                conn.execute(f"INSERT INTO scene (id, project_id, idx, created_at, {names}) "
                             f"VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, {marks})",
                             (sid, project_id, idx, now, *(fields[n] for n in STORED_FIELDS)))
                _write_refs(conn, sid, refs)
                diff["added"].append(sid)
                continue
            old_refs = _refs(conn, sid)
            same = _fingerprint(fields, refs) == _fingerprint(old, old_refs)
            sets = ", ".join(f"{n} = ?" for n in STORED_FIELDS)
            conn.execute(f"UPDATE scene SET idx = ?, {sets}, revision = revision + ? WHERE id = ?",
                         (idx, *(fields[n] for n in STORED_FIELDS), 0 if same else 1, sid))
            if refs != old_refs:
                _write_refs(conn, sid, refs)
            if not same:
                diff["changed"].append(sid)
            elif old["idx"] != idx:
                diff["moved"].append(sid)
            else:
                diff["unchanged"].append(sid)
        for sid in live:
            if sid not in final_ids:
                conn.execute("UPDATE scene SET deleted_at = ? WHERE id = ?", (now, sid))
                diff["deleted"].append(sid)
        conn.execute("UPDATE project SET revision = revision + 1 WHERE id = ?", (project_id,))
        if in_tx_hook is not None:
            in_tx_hook(conn)
        events.emit(conn, event_kind, project_id=project_id,
                    payload={"diff": diff, **(event_payload or {})})
        out = [scene_out(conn, r) for r in _live_scenes(conn, project_id)]
        project = project_out(_project_row(conn, project_id))
    return {"project": project, "scenes": out, "diff": diff,
            "lint": lint_project(conn, project_id)}
```

Append to `h3_48gb/studio/schemas.py`:

```python


Mode = Literal["from_frame", "refs", "text"]
_TEXT = Field(default="", max_length=8000)


class SourceFrame(_Body):
    kind: Literal["none", "prev_last_frame", "asset"] = "none"
    scene_id: str | None = None
    asset_id: str | None = None


class SceneIn(_Body):
    scene_id: str | None = None
    mode: Mode
    action_text: str = _TEXT
    performance_text: str = _TEXT
    sound_text: str = _TEXT
    music_text: str = _TEXT
    state_in_text: str = _TEXT
    state_out_text: str = _TEXT
    requested_s: float = Field(gt=0, le=60)
    seed: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1, le=2**31 - 1)
    source_frame: SourceFrame = Field(default_factory=SourceFrame)
    refs: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, max_length=12)


class ScenesReplace(_Body):
    expected_revision: int
    scenes: list[SceneIn]          # no upper bound: a project may grow without limit (§4.4)


class ScenePatch(_Body):
    expected_revision: int
    mode: Mode | None = None
    action_text: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=8000)
    performance_text: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=8000)
    sound_text: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=8000)
    music_text: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=8000)
    state_in_text: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=8000)
    state_out_text: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=8000)
    requested_s: float | None = Field(default=None, gt=0, le=60)
    seed: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1, le=2**31 - 1)
    source_frame: SourceFrame | None = None
    refs: list[str] | None = Field(default=None, max_length=12)


class LintRequest(_Body):
    scene_ids: list[str] | None = None
```

Modify `h3_48gb/studio/app.py` — перед строкой `    @app.get(f"{api}/events")` вставить:

```python
    @app.put(f"{api}/projects/{{project_id}}/scenes")
    def replace_scenes(body: schemas.ScenesReplace, project_id: str = Depends(project_exists),
                       conn=Depends(get_conn)):
        return ops.replace_scenes(conn, project_id, expected_revision=body.expected_revision,
                                  scenes=[s.model_dump() for s in body.scenes])

    @app.patch(f"{api}/scenes/{{scene_id}}")
    def patch_scene(scene_id: str, body: schemas.ScenePatch, conn=Depends(get_conn)):
        changes = body.model_dump(exclude={"expected_revision"}, exclude_none=True)
        return ops.update_scene(conn, scene_id, expected_revision=body.expected_revision,
                                changes=changes)

    @app.post(f"{api}/projects/{{project_id}}/lint")
    def lint_project(body: schemas.LintRequest, project_id: str = Depends(project_exists),
                     conn=Depends(get_conn)):
        return {"findings": ops.lint_project(conn, project_id, body.scene_ids)}
```

```python
"""Slice S1 step 3: the scenarist proposes (O25), the owner applies (O26)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import io
import json
import sqlite3

import pytest
from _studio_fixtures import TINY_PNG, ScriptedLlm, make_client

# Expected values are literals here, never imported from the code under test (review r2, Н2):
# a change to the prompt or the schema must be made twice — in the code and here, on purpose.
SCENARIST_PROMPT = (
    'You are the scenarist of a short video made of consecutive scenes, each scene rendered by the\n'
    'MiniMax H3 video model as its own 3–15 second clip. Scene N+1 starts from the last frame of\n'
    'scene N, so the end of one scene is the beginning of the next.\n'
    '\n'
    'Input: a JSON object with\n'
    '- `idea` — what the owner wants, usually in Russian;\n'
    '- `scenes_count` — exactly how many scenes to write (1 to 12 in one answer);\n'
    '- `frame_format` — "16:9" or "9:16";\n'
    '- `style_block` — visual style to respect (may be empty);\n'
    '- `cast` — characters: `tag`, `description_en`, `portrait_phrase`.\n'
    '\n'
    'Answer with one JSON object `{"scenes": [...]}` holding exactly `scenes_count` scenes, in play\n'
    'order. Every field is English (no Cyrillic). For each scene:\n'
    '- `action` — what happens in the frame: who stands or sits where and on what, what they do,\n'
    "  where the camera is. Do not repeat the character's portrait: the code pastes it in. Never\n"
    '  name an absent character, even in a negation. Never write "@tag" or "mode:".\n'
    '- `performance` — the acting: the inner state at the start, how and when it changes, the face,\n'
    '  the gaze, the hands, small movements. A scene without a change of state reads as a dead face.\n'
    '- `sound` — what sounds, described physically: the material and the contact ("bare heels on\n'
    '  wet planks", "linen brushing against reeds") and the loudness that the gait gives. Say what\n'
    '  does not sound only after what does.\n'
    '- `music` — non-diegetic music: instruments, tempo, dynamics, or "none".\n'
    '- `state_in` — one sentence: the frame at the first moment of the scene.\n'
    '- `state_out` — one sentence: the frame at the last moment; the next scene starts from it.\n'
    '- `cast_tags` — tags from `cast` who are on screen in this scene.\n'
    '- `requested_s` — length in seconds, 3 to 15; 8 is a good default.\n'
    '\n'
    'Do not count above three in the action: say "several" instead of a number.\n'
)
SCENARIST_PROMPT_VERSION = "bdfb247d800c"     # sha256(SCENARIST_PROMPT)[:12]

}

def _llm_scene(i, tags=("anna",), seconds=8.0):
    return {"action": f"She walks to the lake, shot {i}.",
            "performance": f"Her gaze searches the trees, then her shoulders drop ({i}).",
            "sound": f"Bare heels on wet planks ({i}).", "music": f"A low cello ({i}).",
            "state_in": f"in {i}", "state_out": f"out {i}", "cast_tags": list(tags),

                                                  "idea_text": "девушка идёт к озеру"}).json()

        assets = [{"asset_id": a["id"], "role": "image"}]

        "expected_revision": 1, "tag": "anna", "description_ru": "ж", "assets": assets,
        "description_en": "A woman in her thirties.", "portrait_phrase": PHRASE})
    return p["id"]


def _proposed(i, *, mode, source, seconds):
    return {"scene_id": None, "mode": mode,
            "action_text": f"{PHRASE}. She walks to the lake, shot {i}.",
            "performance_text": f"Her gaze searches the trees, then her shoulders drop ({i}).",
            "sound_text": f"Bare heels on wet planks ({i}).", "music_text": f"A low cello ({i}).",
            "state_in_text": f"in {i}", "state_out_text": f"out {i}", "requested_s": seconds,
            "seed": None, "source_frame": {"kind": source, "scene_id": None, "asset_id": None},
            "refs": ["anna"]}


def test_the_expected_prompt_version_is_the_hash_of_the_expected_prompt():
    import hashlib
    assert hashlib.sha256(SCENARIST_PROMPT.encode()).hexdigest()[:12] == SCENARIST_PROMPT_VERSION


def test_propose_then_apply_gives_two_chained_scenes(tmp_path, seeds):
    raw = {"scenes": [_llm_scene(1), _llm_scene(2, tags=("anna", "@anna"), seconds=4.8125)]}
    llm = ScriptedLlm([copy.deepcopy(raw)])
    client = make_client(tmp_path, llm=llm)
    pid = _project_with_hero(client)
    r = client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{pid}/director/propose",
                    json={"idea_text": "девушка идёт к озеру", "scenes_count": 2})
    assert r.status_code == 200
    proposal = r.json()
    (call,) = llm.calls
    assert call["schema"] == SCENES_SCHEMA
    assert call["messages"] == [
        {"role": "system", "content": SCENARIST_PROMPT},
        {"role": "user", "content": (
            '{"idea": "девушка идёт к озеру", "scenes_count": 2, "frame_format": "16:9", '
            '"style_block": "", "cast": [{"tag": "anna", "description_en": '
            '"A woman in her thirties.", "portrait_phrase": "' + PHRASE + '"}]}')}]
    assert proposal == {
        "proposal_id": proposal["proposal_id"],
        "scenes": [_proposed(1, mode="refs", source="none", seconds=8.0),
                   _proposed(2, mode="from_frame", source="prev_last_frame", seconds=4.8125)],
        "durations": [{"frames": 192, "duration_s": 8.0}, {"frames": 124, "duration_s": 5.167}],
        "dropped_tags": []}
    row = sqlite3.connect(tmp_path / "out" / "studio" / "studio.sqlite").execute(
        "SELECT llm_json, applied_at FROM proposal").fetchone()
    assert (json.loads(row[0]), row[1]) == (
        {"provider": "scripted", "prompt_version": SCENARIST_PROMPT_VERSION, "raw": raw}, None)
    assert client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{pid}").json()["scenes"] == []

    applied = client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{pid}/director/apply",
                          json={"proposal_id": proposal["proposal_id"], "expected_revision": 2})
    assert applied.status_code == 200
    s1, s2 = applied.json()["scenes"]
    assert [(s["idx"], s["mode"], s["frames"], s["seed"], s["refs"], s["sound_text"],
             s["music_text"]) for s in (s1, s2)] == [
        (0, "refs", 192, 500, ["anna"], "Bare heels on wet planks (1).", "A low cello (1)."),
        (1, "from_frame", 124, 501, ["anna"], "Bare heels on wet planks (2).", "A low cello (2).")]
    assert s2["action_text"] == f"{PHRASE}. She walks to the lake, shot 2."
    assert s2["source_frame"] == {"kind": "prev_last_frame", "scene_id": s1["id"], "asset_id": None}


def test_first_scene_without_a_hero_picture_waits_for_a_keyframe(tmp_path, seeds):
    client = make_client(tmp_path, llm=ScriptedLlm([{"scenes": [_llm_scene(1)]}]))
    pid = _project_with_hero(client, with_image=False)
    (scene,) = client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{pid}/director/propose",
                           json={"idea_text": "x", "scenes_count": 1}).json()["scenes"]
    assert (scene["mode"], scene["refs"], scene["source_frame"]["kind"]) == \
        ("from_frame", ["anna"], "none")


def test_wrong_number_of_scenes_is_502_and_no_proposal(tmp_path, seeds):
    client = make_client(tmp_path, llm=ScriptedLlm([{"scenes": [_llm_scene(1)]}]))
    pid = _project_with_hero(client)
    r = client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{pid}/director/propose",
                    json={"idea_text": "x", "scenes_count": 2})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"], r.json()["error"]["detail"]) == \
        (502, "llm_failed", {"got": 1, "wanted": 2})


@pytest.mark.parametrize("reply, path", [
    ({}, "/"),
    ({"scenes": [{k: v for k, v in _llm_scene(1).items() if k != "sound"}]}, "/scenes/0"),
    ({"scenes": [{**_llm_scene(1), "requested_s": "8"}]}, "/scenes/0/requested_s"),
    ({"scenes": [{**_llm_scene(1), "cast_tags": "anna"}]}, "/scenes/0/cast_tags"),
])
def test_a_reply_off_the_schema_is_a_named_502_not_a_500(tmp_path, seeds, reply, path):
    """Review finding 6: json_schema routes are not checked by the provider layer."""
    client = make_client(tmp_path, llm=ScriptedLlm([reply]))
    pid = _project_with_hero(client)
    r = client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{pid}/director/propose",
                    json={"idea_text": "x", "scenes_count": 1})
    assert r.status_code == 502
    assert (r.json()["error"]["message"], r.json()["error"]["detail"]["path"]) == \
        ("Модель ответила не по схеме", path)


def test_unknown_tags_are_dropped_and_reported(tmp_path, seeds):
    client = make_client(tmp_path, llm=ScriptedLlm([{"scenes": [_llm_scene(1, ("ghost", "anna"))]}]))
    pid = _project_with_hero(client)
    body = client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{pid}/director/propose",
                       json={"idea_text": "x", "scenes_count": 1}).json()
    assert (body["scenes"][0]["refs"], body["dropped_tags"]) == (["anna"], ["ghost"])


def test_more_than_twelve_scenes_is_the_scenarists_limit_not_the_projects(tmp_path, seeds):
    llm = ScriptedLlm([])
    client = make_client(tmp_path, llm=llm)
    pid = _project_with_hero(client)
    r = client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{pid}/director/propose",
                    json={"idea_text": "x", "scenes_count": 13})
    assert r.status_code == 400
    assert r.json()["error"] == {
        "code": "too_many_scenes_for_scenarist",
        "message": "Сценарист пишет не больше 12 сцен за раз; остальные добавьте «+ Сцена»",
        "detail": {"max": 12, "wanted": 13}}
    assert llm.calls == []


def test_applying_twice_is_409_and_the_proposal_survives_a_restart(tmp_path, seeds):
    client = make_client(tmp_path, llm=ScriptedLlm([{"scenes": [_llm_scene(1)]}]))
    pid = _project_with_hero(client)
    proposal_id = client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{pid}/director/propose",
                              json={"idea_text": "x", "scenes_count": 1}).json()["proposal_id"]
    restarted = make_client(tmp_path, llm=ScriptedLlm([]))
    ok = restarted.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{pid}/director/apply",
                        json={"proposal_id": proposal_id, "expected_revision": 2})
    assert ok.status_code == 200
    again = restarted.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{pid}/director/apply",
                           json={"proposal_id": proposal_id, "expected_revision": 3})
    assert (again.status_code, again.json()["error"]["code"]) == (409, "proposal_already_applied")


def test_a_proposal_survives_a_revision_conflict_and_applies_with_the_fresh_revision(tmp_path, seeds):
    """Review Focus 3: the owner added a hero after proposing; the stale apply must not consume it."""
    client = make_client(tmp_path, llm=ScriptedLlm([{"scenes": [_llm_scene(1)]}]))
    pid = _project_with_hero(client)
    proposal_id = client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{pid}/director/propose",
                              json={"idea_text": "x", "scenes_count": 1}).json()["proposal_id"]
    client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{pid}/cast", json={
        "expected_revision": 2, "tag": "boris", "description_ru": "м",
        "description_en": "A man.", "portrait_phrase": "a tall man"})
    stale = client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{pid}/director/apply",
                        json={"proposal_id": proposal_id, "expected_revision": 2})
    assert (stale.status_code, stale.json()["error"]["code"]) == (409, "revision_conflict")
    fresh = client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{pid}/director/apply",
                        json={"proposal_id": proposal_id, "expected_revision": 3})
    assert fresh.status_code == 200


def test_prompt_version_follows_the_prompt_file(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """The version written into proposal.llm_json must change when the prompt changes."""
    from h3_48gb.studio.director import scenarist
    other = tmp_path / "scenarist.md"
    other.write_text("another prompt\n", encoding="utf-8")
    monkeypatch.setattr(scenarist, "PROMPT", other)
    assert scenarist.prompt_version() == "8fc954fcad67"


def test_the_saved_proposal_carries_the_version_of_the_prompt_actually_used(tmp_path, seeds,
                                                                            monkeypatch):
    """Review r3: not the hash function alone — O25 must WRITE the version of the prompt file it
    sent. With another prompt file the stored version is that file's hash (literal)."""
    from h3_48gb.studio.director import scenarist
    other = tmp_path / "scenarist.md"
    other.write_text("another prompt\n", encoding="utf-8")
    monkeypatch.setattr(scenarist, "PROMPT", other)
    llm = ScriptedLlm([{"scenes": [_llm_scene(1)]}])
    client = make_client(tmp_path, llm=llm)
    pid = _project_with_hero(client)
    client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{pid}/director/propose",
                json={"idea_text": "x", "scenes_count": 1})
    stored = json.loads(sqlite3.connect(tmp_path / "out" / "studio" / "studio.sqlite").execute(
        "SELECT llm_json FROM proposal").fetchone()[0])
    assert (stored["prompt_version"], llm.calls[0]["messages"][0]["content"]) == (
        "8fc954fcad67", "another prompt\n")
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Реализация**

Create `h3_48gb/studio/director/prompts/scenarist.md`:

```markdown
You are the scenarist of a short video made of consecutive scenes, each scene rendered by the
MiniMax H3 video model as its own 3–15 second clip. Scene N+1 starts from the last frame of
scene N, so the end of one scene is the beginning of the next.

Input: a JSON object with
- `idea` — what the owner wants, usually in Russian;
- `scenes_count` — exactly how many scenes to write (1 to 12 in one answer);
- `frame_format` — "16:9" or "9:16";
- `style_block` — visual style to respect (may be empty);
- `cast` — characters: `tag`, `description_en`, `portrait_phrase`.

Answer with one JSON object `{"scenes": [...]}` holding exactly `scenes_count` scenes, in play
order. Every field is English (no Cyrillic). For each scene:
- `action` — what happens in the frame: who stands or sits where and on what, what they do,
  where the camera is. Do not repeat the character's portrait: the code pastes it in. Never
  name an absent character, even in a negation. Never write "@tag" or "mode:".
- `performance` — the acting: the inner state at the start, how and when it changes, the face,
  the gaze, the hands, small movements. A scene without a change of state reads as a dead face.
- `sound` — what sounds, described physically: the material and the contact ("bare heels on
  wet planks", "linen brushing against reeds") and the loudness that the gait gives. Say what
  does not sound only after what does.
- `music` — non-diegetic music: instruments, tempo, dynamics, or "none".
- `state_in` — one sentence: the frame at the first moment of the scene.
- `state_out` — one sentence: the frame at the last moment; the next scene starts from it.
- `cast_tags` — tags from `cast` who are on screen in this scene.
- `requested_s` — length in seconds, 3 to 15; 8 is a good default.

Do not count above three in the action: say "several" instead of a number.
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/director/scenarist.py`:

```python
"""«Сценарист» (spec §9.1): idea -> scenes. The model writes the words; the code does the
arithmetic — number of scenes, lengths, modes and sources, the verbatim portrait phrase."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path

from h3_48gb.studio.errors import StudioError
from h3_48gb.studio import duration
from h3_48gb.studio.llm import Llm, validate_reply

PROMPT = Path(__file__).parent / "prompts" / "scenarist.md"
#: One LLM call writes at most this many scenes (its output budget, see the prompt). This is the
#: scenarist's limit, not the project's: a project has no upper bound on scenes (§4.4, owner 21.10).
MAX_SCENES_PER_PROPOSAL = 12

_STR = {"type": "string"}
STUDIO_SCENES_SCHEMA = {
    "name": "studio_scenes",
    "schema": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "scenes": {
                "type": "array",
                "items": {
                    "type": "object",
                    "properties": {
                        "action": _STR, "performance": _STR, "sound": _STR, "music": _STR,
                        "state_in": _STR, "state_out": _STR,
                        "cast_tags": {"type": "array", "items": _STR},
                        "requested_s": {"type": "number", "minimum": duration.MIN_SECONDS,
                                        "maximum": duration.MAX_SECONDS},
                    },
                    "required": ["action", "performance", "sound", "music", "state_in",
                                 "state_out", "cast_tags", "requested_s"],
                    "additionalProperties": False,
                },
            },
        },
        "required": ["scenes"],
        "additionalProperties": False,
    },
}
RETRY_REMINDER = ("Ответ строго одним JSON-объектом {\"scenes\": [...]} по схеме, без другого "
                  "текста.")


def prompt_version() -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(PROMPT.read_bytes()).hexdigest()[:12]


def _with_portraits(action: str, phrases: list[str]) -> str:
    missing = [p for p in phrases if p and p not in action]
    if not missing:
        return action
    return ". ".join(missing) + ". " + action


def propose(llm: Llm, *, idea_text: str, scenes_count: int, frame_format: str,
            style_block: str, cast: list[dict]) -> tuple[list[dict], dict, list[str]]:
    """Return (scenes in the O7 input shape, llm_json for the proposal row, dropped tags).

    `cast` items: `{"tag", "description_en", "portrait_phrase", "has_image"}`. A scene's `refs`
    are the cast members on screen (SCENE_REF); pictures of them condition the render only in
    `refs` mode (S3)."""
    if scenes_count > MAX_SCENES_PER_PROPOSAL:
        raise StudioError(400, "too_many_scenes_for_scenarist",
                          f"Сценарист пишет не больше {MAX_SCENES_PER_PROPOSAL} сцен за раз; "
                          f"остальные добавьте «+ Сцена»",
                          {"max": MAX_SCENES_PER_PROPOSAL, "wanted": scenes_count})
    user = {"idea": idea_text, "scenes_count": scenes_count, "frame_format": frame_format,
            "style_block": style_block,
            "cast": [{k: c[k] for k in ("tag", "description_en", "portrait_phrase")}
                     for c in cast]}
    messages = [{"role": "system", "content": PROMPT.read_text(encoding="utf-8")},
                {"role": "user", "content": json.dumps(user, ensure_ascii=False)}]
    raw = llm.json_turn(messages, STUDIO_SCENES_SCHEMA, RETRY_REMINDER)
    validate_reply(raw, STUDIO_SCENES_SCHEMA)
    got = raw["scenes"]
    if len(got) != scenes_count:
        raise StudioError(502, "llm_failed",
                          f"Сценарист вернул {len(got)} сцен вместо {scenes_count}",
                          {"got": len(got), "wanted": scenes_count})
    by_tag = {c["tag"]: c for c in cast}
    scenes, dropped = [], []
    for i, s in enumerate(got):
        tags: list[str] = []
        for raw_tag in s["cast_tags"]:
            tag = raw_tag.lstrip("@")
            if tag not in by_tag:
                if tag not in dropped:
                    dropped.append(tag)
            elif tag not in tags:
                tags.append(tag)
        with_image = [t for t in tags if by_tag[t]["has_image"]]
        if i == 0:
            mode = "refs" if with_image else "from_frame"
            source = {"kind": "none", "scene_id": None, "asset_id": None}
        else:
            mode = "from_frame"
            source = {"kind": "prev_last_frame", "scene_id": None, "asset_id": None}
        requested = min(max(float(s["requested_s"]), duration.MIN_SECONDS), duration.MAX_SECONDS)
        scenes.append({
            "scene_id": None, "mode": mode,
            "action_text": _with_portraits(s["action"].strip(),
                                           [by_tag[t]["portrait_phrase"] for t in tags]),
            "performance_text": s["performance"].strip(), "sound_text": s["sound"].strip(),
            "music_text": s["music"].strip(), "state_in_text": s["state_in"].strip(),
            "state_out_text": s["state_out"].strip(), "requested_s": requested, "seed": None,
            "source_frame": source, "refs": tags,
        })
    llm_json = {"provider": llm.label, "prompt_version": prompt_version(), "raw": raw}
    return scenes, llm_json, dropped
```

Modify `h3_48gb/studio/ops.py` — блок импортов стал окончательным:

```python
import json
import secrets
import sqlite3

from h3_48gb.studio import db, duration, events, ids, lint
from h3_48gb.studio.director import cast as cast_card
from h3_48gb.studio.director import scenarist
from h3_48gb.studio.errors import StudioError
from h3_48gb.studio.llm import Llm
```

Append to `h3_48gb/studio/ops.py`:

```python


# --- slice S1: scenarist (task 11) ------------------------------------------------------------


def propose_scenes(conn: sqlite3.Connection, project_id: str, *, llm: Llm,
                   idea_text: str, scenes_count: int) -> dict:
    """O25. Saved as a `proposal`, not applied. The LLM call runs outside any transaction."""
    prow = _project_row(conn, project_id)
    cast = []
    for c in _cast_rows(conn, project_id):
        has_image = conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM cast_asset WHERE cast_id = ? AND role = 'image'",
                                 (c["id"],)).fetchone() is not None
        cast.append({"tag": c["tag"], "description_en": c["description_en"],
                     "portrait_phrase": c["portrait_phrase"], "has_image": has_image})
    scenes, llm_json, dropped = scenarist.propose(
        llm, idea_text=idea_text, scenes_count=scenes_count, frame_format=prow["frame_format"],
        style_block=prow["style_block"], cast=cast)
    proposal_id = ids.new_id("r")
    with db.tx(conn):
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO proposal (id, project_id, project_revision, idea_text, scenes_json, "
            "llm_json, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
            (proposal_id, project_id, prow["revision"], idea_text,
             json.dumps(scenes, ensure_ascii=False), json.dumps(llm_json, ensure_ascii=False),
             ids.now_iso()))
        events.emit(conn, "proposal_created", project_id=project_id,
                    payload={"proposal_id": proposal_id, "scenes": len(scenes)})
    # `durations`: what the grid makes of each requested length (§6.2), shown in the preview.
    durations = [{"frames": f, "duration_s": round(duration.seconds(f), 3)}
                 for f in (duration.snap_frames(s["requested_s"]) for s in scenes)]
    return {"proposal_id": proposal_id, "scenes": scenes, "durations": durations,
            "dropped_tags": dropped}


def apply_proposal(conn: sqlite3.Connection, project_id: str, *, proposal_id: str,
                   expected_revision: int) -> dict:
    """O26: the proposal's scenes replace the project's set through the O7 path."""
    row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM proposal WHERE id = ? AND project_id = ?",
                       (proposal_id, project_id)).fetchone()
    if row is None:
        raise StudioError(404, "proposal_not_found", "Предложение не найдено",
                          {"proposal_id": proposal_id})
    if row["applied_at"] is not None:
        raise StudioError(409, "proposal_already_applied", "Это предложение уже применено",
                          {"proposal_id": proposal_id, "applied_at": row["applied_at"]})

    def mark_applied(c):
        # Two clicks racing past the check above cannot both land: the second one's
        # expected_revision is stale by then and replace_scenes refuses it with 409.
        c.execute("UPDATE proposal SET applied_at = ? WHERE id = ?", (ids.now_iso(), proposal_id))

    return replace_scenes(conn, project_id, expected_revision=expected_revision,
                          scenes=json.loads(row["scenes_json"]), event_kind="proposal_applied",
                          event_payload={"proposal_id": proposal_id}, in_tx_hook=mark_applied)
```

Append to `h3_48gb/studio/schemas.py`:

```python


class ProposeRequest(_Body):
    idea_text: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=20000)
    scenes_count: int = Field(default=2, ge=1)


class ApplyRequest(_Body):
    proposal_id: str
    expected_revision: int
```

```python
    def propose(body: schemas.ProposeRequest, project_id: str = Depends(project_exists),
                conn=Depends(get_conn)):

              conn=Depends(get_conn)):
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Мутации — увидеть красными, вернуть**

1. `scenarist.propose`: `if len(got) != scenes_count:` → `if False:`. Expected: `test_wrong_number_of_scenes_is_502_and_no_proposal` — `assert 200 == 502`.
2. `scenarist.propose`: `mode = "refs" if with_image else "from_frame"` → `mode = "refs" if tags else "from_frame"`. Expected: `test_first_scene_without_a_hero_picture_waits_for_a_keyframe` красный.
3. `scenarist._with_portraits`: `if not missing:` → `if True:`. Expected: `test_propose_then_apply_gives_two_chained_scenes` — `action_text` без портретной фразы.
4. `ops.apply_proposal.mark_applied`: тело `c.execute("UPDATE proposal SET applied_at …")` → `pass`. Expected: `test_applying_twice_is_409_and_the_proposal_survives_a_restart` — `assert 200 == 409`.
5. К-6: `validate_reply(raw, STUDIO_SCENES_SCHEMA)` → `pass`. Expected: `test_a_reply_off_the_schema_is_a_named_502_not_a_500` — 4 случая, 500 вместо 502.

- Test: `src/screens/sceneDraft.test.ts` (регрессии r2–r5 на чистом состоянии), `src/screens/sceneDraft.model.test.ts` (модельный тест: случайные чередования против фейкового сервера с состоянием), `src/screens/HeroStep.test.tsx`, `src/screens/ScenarioStep.test.tsx`, `src/screens/ProjectScreen.test.tsx`, `src/mobileAction.test.tsx`, Playwright `e2e/phone.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: O3, O5, O6, O7, O8, O9, O21, O25, O26 (задачи 3, 9–11); `api`, `upload`, `ApiError`, `Button`, `Input`, `Textarea`, `Badge`, `Field`, `useEvents`, `stubFetch` (с обработчиками), `FakeEventSource` (задача 4).
- Produces: `sceneDraft` — чистое состояние черновика без React: `init`, `input`, `save`, `saveMine`, `takeServer`, `sceneSeen`, `received`, `dirtyFields`, `isDirty`, `isStale`, `conflictPairs`, `toBody`, `fromScene`, типы `DraftState`, `Request`, `Outcome`, `Field`, `FIELDS`; `FakeSceneServer` (тестовый, `src/test/sceneServer.ts`); `HeroStep({snap, onChanged})`, `ScenarioStep({snap, onChanged})`, `SceneCard({scene, castTags, findings, onSaved, onConflict, onDelete})`, `FindingList({findings})`, `CONFLICT_MESSAGE`, `STALE_NOTICE`, `toSceneIn(scene) -> SceneIn` (тело O7 из снимка: `prev_last_frame` без старого `scene_id`); `MobileActionProvider`, `useMobileAction(id, action | null)`, `useIsPhone()`; типы `Scene`, `CastMember`, `Finding`, `LintMap`, `Proposal` (`durations`, `dropped_tags`), `ScenesResult`, `SceneResult`, `Asset`.
- Поведение:
  - **черновик сцены (Н1, r5; редакция 6):** вся синхронизация — чистый модуль `sceneDraft.ts` (состояние и переходы без React); карточка его только рендерит и выполняет один запрос, который он называет. У каждого поля счётчик правок `gen`, растёт на каждый ввод; поле грязное, пока `gen > cleanGen` — значения для «тронуто» **не сравниваются**. `base` — ревизия, против которой правятся грязные поля (её шлёт «Сохранить»); `server` — новейшее увиденное состояние сцены: чистые поля всегда показывают его, снимок старше увиденного игнорируется. Новый снимок (SSE): чистые поля берут его значения, грязные и база не трогаются (`STALE_NOTICE`, если сервер ушёл вперёд); карточка без грязных полей переходит на снимок целиком вместе с базой. «Сохранить» шлёт только грязные поля с `expected_revision` базы; **в полёте не больше одного запроса на карточку** — повторное нажатие ставится в очередь и уходит после ответа с базой, которую тот поставил; ключ карточки — `scene.id`;
  - **ответ сохранения (Б-2, r4 №1, r5):** при отправке запоминается `sentGen` всех полей. 200: поле с `gen === sentGen` берёт значение сервера и становится чистым, поле с `gen > sentGen` остаётся грязным со своим текстом и уходит следующим сохранением; база = ревизия ответа (или `server.revision`, если грязных не осталось) и **никогда не уменьшается** — ответ с ревизией ниже базы игнорируется. 409: `server` = снимок из `detail.current`, чистые поля сразу берут его, грязные показываются парами «Сервер: … · Моё: …», база не трогается до выбора владельца; 409 без грязных полей (владелец уже нажал «Взять с сервера», пока запрос летел) — не конфликт. «Сохранить моё» шлёт грязные поля с `expected_revision = server.revision`; «Взять с сервера» берёт все поля из `server`, карточка чистая на его ревизии. Блок конфликта переживает перезагрузку снимка;
  - режим — сегменты «От кадра / Референсы» (R6); «Кто в кадре» — чипы тегов кастинга;
  - превью предложения — режим, кадры и секунды после снапа, действие, игра, звук; отброшенные теги названы;
  - **телефон (Н8, Б-7, R28, r5):** одна панель снизу с главным действием текущего шага: конфликт «Сохранить моё» + «Взять с сервера» (4) > правка сцены «Сохранить сцену N» (3) > заполняемый герой «Сохранить героя» (2) > «Применить» (1) > «Предложить сцены» (0); из нескольких равных — карточка, последней получившая ввод: счётчик `inputs` состояния передаётся как `touched` **в обеих ветках, конфликтной тоже**; на ширине от 768 px — обычные кнопки в шагах, панели нет;
  - при 502/503/409 `gpu_busy` от LLM шаг «Герой» открывает поля английского описания (R3);
  - находки линта — под своим полем; страница перезагружает снимок по `project_updated`, `cast_added`, `scenes_replaced`, `scene_updated`, `proposal_applied` **своего** проекта.

```tsx
import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { stubFetch } from "@/test/fetchStub";
import { snapshot } from "@/test/snapshot";
import { HeroStep } from "./HeroStep";

const ASSET = { id: "a_1", kind: "image", sha256: "h", bytes: 9, mime: "image/png", url: "/api/studio/assets/a_1/file" };

describe("HeroStep", () => {
  it("uploads the photo and saves the hero with it and the project revision", async () => {
    const calls = stubFetch({
      "POST /api/studio/assets": { status: 201, body: ASSET },
      "POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/cast": { status: 201, body: {} },
    });
    const onChanged = vi.fn();
    render(<HeroStep snap={snapshot()} onChanged={onChanged} />);

    await screen.findByAltText("Фото героя");

    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText(/Кто это/), "женщина с косой");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить героя" }));

      assets: [{ asset_id: "a_1", role: "image" }],
    });
  });

      "POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/cast": [
        { status: 503, body: { error: { code: "llm_not_configured", message: "Сценарист не настроен", detail: {} } } },
        { status: 201, body: {} },
      ],
    });

    expect((await screen.findByRole("alert")).textContent).toBe("Сценарист не настроен. Впишите английское описание сами.");
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText(/Описание по-английски/), "A woman.");
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText(/Портретная фраза/), "a woman with a braid");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить героя" }));

      expected_revision: 3, tag: "anna", description_ru: "женщина", assets: [],
      description_en: "A woman.", portrait_phrase: "a woman with a braid",
    });

});
```

```tsx
    expect(body.scenes.map((s) => [s.scene_id, s.source_frame])).toEqual([
```

Replace `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/screens/ProjectScreen.test.tsx` целиком (страница со снимком, SSE и двумя клиентами на одном фейковом сервере с состоянием; узлы карточек ищутся заново после каждого события — Н1, Н3):

```tsx
import { render, screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { Scene } from "@/api/types";
import { FakeEventSource, stubFetch } from "@/test/fetchStub";
import { scene, snapshot } from "@/test/snapshot";
import { ProjectScreen } from "./ProjectScreen";

const LINT = { "POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/lint": { body: { findings: {} } } };

beforeEach(() => {
  FakeEventSource.last = null;
  vi.stubGlobal("EventSource", FakeEventSource);
});

/** The card as it is in the document NOW — never a node remembered before an event (Н3). */
const card = (n: number) => screen.getByRole("article", { name: `Сцена ${n}` });
const field = (n: number, label: RegExp) => within(card(n)).getByLabelText<HTMLTextAreaElement>(label);

/** A server with state for scenes s_1 and s_2: GET answers the current snapshot, PATCH checks
 *  expected_revision exactly like ops.update_scene (409 with the current scene). Two clients
 *  talk to it: the page under test, and `otherWindow` — plain fetch, as a second tab would. */
function fakeStudio() {
  // `hold()` makes the NEXT PATCH from the page wait until the returned function is called —
  // the owner keeps typing while the request is in flight (review r3, blocker 2).
  let gate: Promise<void> | null = null;
  const hold = () => {
    let open = () => {};
    gate = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { open = resolve; });
    return () => open();
  };
  const scenes: Record<string, Scene> = {
    s_1: scene({ id: "s_1", idx: 0, music_text: "none" }),
    s_2: scene({ id: "s_2", idx: 1, music_text: "none" }),
  };
  let seq = 5;
  const patch = (id: string) => async (body: unknown) => {
    if (gate) {
      const waiting = gate;
      gate = null;
      await waiting;
    }
    const { expected_revision, ...fields } = body as { expected_revision: number } & Partial<Scene>;
    const current = scenes[id];
    if (expected_revision !== current.revision) {
      return { status: 409, body: { error: { code: "revision_conflict", message: "x", detail: { current } } } };
    }
    scenes[id] = { ...current, ...fields, revision: current.revision + 1 };
    seq += 1;
    return { body: { scene: scenes[id], cascade: [], lint: [] } };
  };
  const calls = stubFetch({
    ...LINT,
    "GET /api/studio/projects/p_1": () => ({ body: snapshot({ scenes: [scenes.s_1, scenes.s_2], last_seq: seq }) }),
    "PATCH /api/studio/scenes/s_1": patch("s_1"),
    "PATCH /api/studio/scenes/s_2": patch("s_2"),
  });
  const otherWindow = (id: string, body: object) =>
    fetch(`/api/studio/scenes/${id}`, { method: "PATCH", body: JSON.stringify(body) });
  const event = (id: string) => FakeEventSource.last!.emit("scene_updated", { project_id: "p_1", scene_id: id }, seq);
  return { scenes, calls, otherWindow, event, hold };
}

describe("ProjectScreen — snapshot, SSE, unsaved edits, conflicts", () => {
  it("a clean card follows the other window; a dirty card keeps its text", async () => {
    const studio = fakeStudio();
    render(<ProjectScreen id="p_1" />);
    await screen.findByRole("article", { name: "Сцена 2" });
    expect(FakeEventSource.last!.url).toBe("/api/studio/events?after=5");
    await userEvent.type(field(2, /Музыка/), " and a flute");
    await studio.otherWindow("s_1", { expected_revision: 1, action_text: "Edited elsewhere." });
    studio.event("s_1");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(field(1, /Действие/).value).toBe("Edited elsewhere."));
    expect(card(2).isConnected).toBe(true);
    expect(field(2, /Музыка/).value).toBe("none and a flute");
  });

  it("ignores events of another project", async () => {
    const studio = fakeStudio();
    render(<ProjectScreen id="p_1" />);
    await screen.findByRole("article", { name: "Сцена 2" });
    FakeEventSource.last!.emit("scene_updated", { project_id: "p_2", scene_id: "s_9" }, 6);
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 20));
    expect(studio.calls.filter((c) => c.key === "GET /api/studio/projects/p_1")).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("two clients edit the same field: no silent overwrite — a conflict «сервер / моё», then a choice", async () => {
    const studio = fakeStudio();
    render(<ProjectScreen id="p_1" />);
    await screen.findByRole("article", { name: "Сцена 1" });
    await userEvent.clear(field(1, /Музыка/));
    await userEvent.type(field(1, /Музыка/), "mine");
    // the other window saves the same field, the page learns about it over SSE
    await studio.otherWindow("s_1", { expected_revision: 1, music_text: "other" });
    studio.event("s_1");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText("Сцену изменили в другом окне — при сохранении покажу, что различается.")).toBeTruthy());
    expect(field(1, /Музыка/).value).toBe("mine");
    await userEvent.click(within(card(1)).getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить сцену" }));
    const box = await within(card(1)).findByRole("group", { name: "Конфликт правок" });
    expect(within(box).getByRole("alert").textContent).toBe("Сцену изменили в другом окне. Сравните и выберите, что оставить.");
    expect(within(box).getByTestId("conflict-music_text").textContent).toBe("МузыкаСервер: other · Моё: mine");
    expect(studio.scenes.s_1.music_text).toBe("other");                 // nothing was overwritten
    expect(studio.calls.filter((c) => c.key === "PATCH /api/studio/scenes/s_1").map((c) => c.body)).toEqual([
      { expected_revision: 1, music_text: "other" },                      // the other window
      { expected_revision: 1, music_text: "mine" },                       // ours, from our base
    ]);
    // the conflict survives the reload that the 409 triggered
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(studio.calls.filter((c) => c.key === "GET /api/studio/projects/p_1").length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3));
    expect(within(card(1)).getByRole("group", { name: "Конфликт правок" })).toBeTruthy();
    await userEvent.click(within(card(1)).getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить моё" }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(studio.scenes.s_1.music_text).toBe("mine"));
    expect(studio.scenes.s_1.revision).toBe(3);
    expect(within(card(1)).queryByRole("group", { name: "Конфликт правок" })).toBeNull();
  });

  it("«Взять с сервера» drops my edit and shows the server's text", async () => {
    const studio = fakeStudio();
    render(<ProjectScreen id="p_1" />);
    await screen.findByRole("article", { name: "Сцена 1" });
    await userEvent.clear(field(1, /Музыка/));
    await userEvent.type(field(1, /Музыка/), "mine");
    await studio.otherWindow("s_1", { expected_revision: 1, music_text: "other" });
    await userEvent.click(within(card(1)).getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить сцену" }));
    await userEvent.click(await within(card(1)).findByRole("button", { name: "Взять с сервера" }));
    expect(field(1, /Музыка/).value).toBe("other");
    expect([studio.scenes.s_1.music_text, studio.scenes.s_1.revision]).toEqual(["other", 2]);
  });

  it("a save answer applies only to what was sent: text typed meanwhile survives and goes next", async () => {
    const studio = fakeStudio();
    render(<ProjectScreen id="p_1" />);
    await screen.findByRole("article", { name: "Сцена 1" });
    await userEvent.clear(field(1, /Музыка/));
    await userEvent.type(field(1, /Музыка/), "mine-v1");
    const release = studio.hold();
    await userEvent.click(within(card(1)).getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить сцену" }));
    await userEvent.type(field(1, /Музыка/), "-v2");                 // typed while in flight
    release();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(studio.scenes.s_1.revision).toBe(2));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(studio.calls.filter((c) => c.key === "GET /api/studio/projects/p_1").length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2));
    expect(field(1, /Музыка/).value).toBe("mine-v1-v2");
    await userEvent.click(within(card(1)).getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить сцену" }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(studio.scenes.s_1.music_text).toBe("mine-v1-v2"));
    expect(studio.calls.filter((c) => c.key === "PATCH /api/studio/scenes/s_1").map((c) => c.body)).toEqual([
      { expected_revision: 1, music_text: "mine-v1" },
      { expected_revision: 2, music_text: "mine-v1-v2" },

  it("the same holds for «Сохранить моё» after a 409", async () => {

    await userEvent.type(field(1, /Музыка/), "-v2");

    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(studio.scenes.s_1.revision).toBe(3));
    expect([studio.scenes.s_1.music_text, field(1, /Музыка/).value]).toEqual(["mine", "mine-v2"]);
    await userEvent.click(within(card(1)).getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить сцену" }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(studio.scenes.s_1.music_text).toBe("mine-v2"));
    expect(studio.calls.filter((c) => c.key === "PATCH /api/studio/scenes/s_1").map((c) => c.body).slice(1)).toEqual([
      { expected_revision: 1, music_text: "mine" },
      { expected_revision: 2, music_text: "mine" },
      { expected_revision: 3, music_text: "mine-v2" },
    ]);
  });

  it("after a 409, a delayed «Сохранить моё» keeps the field the other window saved", async () => {
    // review r4 blocker: the answer must not turn the other window's sound into a local edit

    await within(card(1)).findByRole("group", { name: "Конфликт правок" });

    release();

    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(field(1, /Звук действий/).value).toBe("new remote sound"));
    await userEvent.click(within(card(1)).getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить сцену" }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(studio.scenes.s_1.revision).toBe(4));
    expect(studio.calls.filter((c) => c.key === "PATCH /api/studio/scenes/s_1").map((c) => c.body).slice(1)).toEqual([
      { expected_revision: 1, music_text: "mine" },
      { expected_revision: 2, music_text: "mine" },
      { expected_revision: 3, action_text: "She walks to the lake. Then she sits." },
    ]);
    expect([studio.scenes.s_1.sound_text, studio.scenes.s_1.music_text, studio.scenes.s_1.action_text]).toEqual([
      "new remote sound", "mine", "She walks to the lake. Then she sits."]);
  });

  it("«Сохранить моё» sends only the owner's fields: both windows' edits end up on the server", async () => {
    const studio = fakeStudio();
    render(<ProjectScreen id="p_1" />);
    await screen.findByRole("article", { name: "Сцена 1" });
    await userEvent.type(field(1, /Действие/), " Then she sits.");
    await studio.otherWindow("s_1", { expected_revision: 1, sound_text: "Wind in the reeds." });
    await userEvent.click(within(card(1)).getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить сцену" }));
    await userEvent.click(await within(card(1)).findByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить моё" }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(studio.scenes.s_1.revision).toBe(3));
    expect(studio.calls.filter((c) => c.key === "PATCH /api/studio/scenes/s_1").map((c) => c.body).at(-1)).toEqual(
      { expected_revision: 2, action_text: "She walks to the lake. Then she sits." });
    expect([studio.scenes.s_1.action_text, studio.scenes.s_1.sound_text]).toEqual([
      "She walks to the lake. Then she sits.", "Wind in the reeds."]);
  });

  it("r5: a second «Сохранить» while a request is in flight is queued — one request per card, nothing lost", async () => {
    const studio = fakeStudio();
    render(<ProjectScreen id="p_1" />);
    await screen.findByRole("article", { name: "Сцена 1" });
    await userEvent.clear(field(1, /Музыка/));
    await userEvent.type(field(1, /Музыка/), "mine");
    const release = studio.hold();
    await userEvent.click(within(card(1)).getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить сцену" }));
    await userEvent.type(field(1, /Действие/), " Then she sits.");        // typed while in flight …
    await userEvent.click(within(card(1)).getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить сцену" }));   // … and pressed again
    expect(studio.calls.filter((c) => c.key === "PATCH /api/studio/scenes/s_1")).toHaveLength(1);
    release();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(studio.scenes.s_1.revision).toBe(3));
    expect(studio.calls.filter((c) => c.key === "PATCH /api/studio/scenes/s_1").map((c) => c.body)).toEqual([
      { expected_revision: 1, music_text: "mine" },
      { expected_revision: 2, action_text: "She walks to the lake. Then she sits." },
    ]);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(within(card(1)).getByRole<HTMLButtonElement>("button", { name: "Сохранить сцену" }).disabled).toBe(true));
    expect([field(1, /Музыка/).value, field(1, /Действие/).value]).toEqual(["mine", "She walks to the lake. Then she sits."]);
  });
});
```
