Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/mobileAction.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { act, render, screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { FakeEventSource, stubFetch } from "@/test/fetchStub";
import { scene, snapshot } from "@/test/snapshot";
import { ProjectScreen } from "@/screens/ProjectScreen";

/** The viewport: phone or not; the returned function turns the page (the AB layout listens). */
function phone(matches: boolean) {
  const listeners: (() => void)[] = [];
  const query = { matches, addEventListener: (_: string, fn: () => void) => { listeners.push(fn); }, removeEventListener: () => {} };
  vi.stubGlobal("matchMedia", (q: string) => {
    if (q !== "(max-width: 767px)") throw new Error(`unexpected media query: ${q}`);
    return query;
  });
  return (now: boolean) => act(() => { query.matches = now; listeners.forEach((fn) => fn()); });
}

beforeEach(() => {
  vi.stubGlobal("EventSource", FakeEventSource);
  stubFetch({
    "GET /api/studio/projects/p_1": { body: snapshot({ scenes: [scene()] }) },
    "POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/lint": { body: { findings: {} } },
  });
});

const barLabel = async (label: string) =>
  vi.waitFor(() => expect(within(screen.getByTestId("mobile-action-bar")).getByRole("button").textContent).toBe(label));

describe("phone bar: the main action of the step in hand", () => {
  it("follows the owner from step to step by priority", async () => {
    phone(true);
    render(<ProjectScreen id="p_1" />);
    await screen.findByRole("article", { name: "Сцена 1" });
    await barLabel("Предложить сцены");
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText(/Тег/), "anna");
    await barLabel("Сохранить героя");
    await userEvent.type(within(screen.getByRole("article", { name: "Сцена 1" })).getByLabelText(/Музыка/), "!");
    await barLabel("Сохранить сцену 1");
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить сцену" })).toBeNull();   // no inline twin
  });

  it("on a wide screen there is no bar, every step has its own button", async () => {
    phone(false);
    render(<ProjectScreen id="p_1" />);
    await screen.findByRole("article", { name: "Сцена 1" });
    expect(screen.queryByTestId("mobile-action-bar")).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Предложить сцены" })).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("between two edited scenes the bar saves the one that got input last (1 → 2 → 1)", async () => {
    phone(true);
    stubFetch({
      "GET /api/studio/projects/p_1": { body: snapshot({ scenes: [scene({ id: "s_1", idx: 0 }), scene({ id: "s_2", idx: 1 })] }) },
      "POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/lint": { body: { findings: {} } },
    });
    render(<ProjectScreen id="p_1" />);
    const music = async (n: number) => within(await screen.findByRole("article", { name: `Сцена ${n}` })).getByLabelText(/Музыка/);
    await userEvent.type(await music(1), "a");
    await userEvent.type(await music(2), "b");
    await barLabel("Сохранить сцену 2");
    await userEvent.type(await music(1), "c");
    await barLabel("Сохранить сцену 1");
  });

  it("r5: between two cards in conflict the bar's «Сохранить моё» belongs to the one that got input last (1 → 2 → 1)", async () => {
    const turn = phone(false);                                          // wide: both cards reach a conflict by their own buttons
    const conflict = (id: string, idx: number) => ({ status: 409, body: { error: { code: "revision_conflict", message: "x",
      detail: { current: scene({ id, idx, revision: 2, music_text: "other" }) } } } });
    const calls = stubFetch({
      "GET /api/studio/projects/p_1": { body: snapshot({ scenes: [scene({ id: "s_1", idx: 0 }), scene({ id: "s_2", idx: 1 })] }) },
      "POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/lint": { body: { findings: {} } },
      "PATCH /api/studio/scenes/s_1": conflict("s_1", 0),
      "PATCH /api/studio/scenes/s_2": conflict("s_2", 1),
    });
    render(<ProjectScreen id="p_1" />);
    const card = async (n: number) => screen.findByRole("article", { name: `Сцена ${n}` });
    const music = async (n: number) => within(await card(n)).getByLabelText(/Музыка/);
    for (const n of [1, 2]) {
      await userEvent.type(await music(n), "x");
      await userEvent.click(within(await card(n)).getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить сцену" }));
      await within(await card(n)).findByRole("group", { name: "Конфликт правок" });
    }
    turn(true);                                                         // phone: one bar for both conflicts
    const bar = () => within(screen.getByTestId("mobile-action-bar")).getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить моё" });
    const patched = () => calls.filter((c) => c.key === "PATCH /api/studio/scenes/s_1" || c.key === "PATCH /api/studio/scenes/s_2").map((c) => c.key);
    for (const [n, expected] of [[1, "s_1"], [2, "s_2"], [1, "s_1"]] as const) {
      await userEvent.type(await music(n), "y");
      await userEvent.click(bar());
      await vi.waitFor(() => expect(patched().at(-1)).toBe(`PATCH /api/studio/scenes/${expected}`));
    }
    expect(patched()).toHaveLength(5);
  });
});
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/test/sceneServer.ts` (фейковый сервер сцены с состоянием — `ops.update_scene` в миниатюре, хранит все ревизии и их авторов; его используют оба теста модуля):

```ts
import type { Scene } from "@/api/types";
import { fromScene, type Field, type Outcome, type PatchBody } from "@/screens/sceneDraft";

export type Writer = "init" | "us" | "other";

/** ops.update_scene in miniature, for the draft tests: expected_revision must equal the current
 *  revision, else 409 with the current scene. Every revision and its writer are kept, so a
 *  snapshot of any age can be served and an overwrite can be attributed. */
export class FakeSceneServer {
  scene: Scene;
  history = new Map<number, Scene>();
  writers = new Map<number, Writer>();
  constructor(initial: Scene) {
    this.scene = initial;
    this.history.set(initial.revision, initial);
    this.writers.set(initial.revision, "init");
  }
  patch(body: PatchBody, by: Writer): Outcome {
    const { expected_revision, ...fields } = body;
    if (expected_revision !== this.scene.revision) return { kind: "conflict", current: this.scene };
    this.scene = { ...this.scene, ...(fields as Partial<Scene>), revision: this.scene.revision + 1 };
    this.history.set(this.scene.revision, this.scene);
    this.writers.set(this.scene.revision, by);
    return { kind: "ok", scene: this.scene };
  }
  /** The other window saves one field: it always reads first, so it always has the current revision. */
  other(f: Field, v: string): Scene {
    const out = this.patch({ expected_revision: this.scene.revision, ...fieldValue(f, v) } as PatchBody, "other");
    return (out as { scene: Scene }).scene;
  }
  /** Who wrote the value a field has at `revision`, and at which revision. */
  writerOf(revision: number, f: Field) {
    let rev = revision;
    const first = Math.min(...this.history.keys());
    const value = fromScene(this.history.get(revision)!)[f];
    while (rev > first && fromScene(this.history.get(rev - 1)!)[f] === value) rev -= 1;
    return { rev, by: this.writers.get(rev)! };
  }
}

export function fieldValue(f: Field, v: string): Partial<Scene> {
  if (f === "requested_s" || f === "seed") return { [f]: Number(v) };
  if (f === "refs") return { refs: v ? v.split(",") : [] };
  if (f === "mode") return { mode: v as Scene["mode"] };
  return { [f]: v };
}
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/screens/sceneDraft.test.ts` (детерминированные регрессии на каждую потерю ввода из ревью r2–r5 и на очередь, устаревшие снимки, выбор владельца — на чистом состоянии, с точными телами PATCH):

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { scene } from "@/test/snapshot";
import { FakeSceneServer } from "@/test/sceneServer";
import {
  conflictPairs, dirtyFields, init, input, isStale, received, save, saveMine, sceneSeen, takeServer,
  type DraftState, type Request,
} from "./sceneDraft";

/** Deterministic regressions for every loss of input the reviews r2–r5 found, on the pure draft
 *  state. The model test (sceneDraft.model.test.ts) explores the interleavings at random; these
 *  pin the exact scenarios and the exact bodies. */

const INITIAL = scene({ id: "s_1", revision: 1, sound_text: "old sound", music_text: "none" });

/** Drive one request to the server and back: the body sent is what `state.inflight` says. */
function roundTrip(server: FakeSceneServer, state: DraftState) {
  const request = state.inflight!;
  const outcome = server.patch(request.body, "us");
  return { request, outcome, state: received(state, request, outcome) };
}
const bodies = (...requests: Request[]) => requests.map((r) => r.body);

describe("scene draft — regressions r2–r5", () => {
  it("r2 Н1: the base stays while the other window moves on; «Сохранить» meets a 409, not a silent overwrite", () => {
    const server = new FakeSceneServer(INITIAL);
    let s = input(init(INITIAL), "music_text", "mine");
    server.other("music_text", "other");
    s = sceneSeen(s, server.scene);                                   // SSE reload with rev 2
    expect([s.base, s.draft.music_text, isStale(s)]).toEqual([1, "mine", true]);
    s = save(s);
    expect(s.inflight!.body).toEqual({ expected_revision: 1, music_text: "mine" });
    const t = roundTrip(server, s);
    expect(t.outcome.kind).toBe("conflict");
    expect(server.scene.music_text).toBe("other");                     // nothing was overwritten
    expect(conflictPairs(t.state)).toEqual([{ field: "music_text", server: "other", mine: "mine" }]);
    const u = roundTrip(server, saveMine(t.state));
    expect(u.request.body).toEqual({ expected_revision: 2, music_text: "mine" });
    expect([server.scene.music_text, server.scene.revision, u.state.base, u.state.conflict, dirtyFields(u.state)])
      .toEqual(["mine", 3, 3, false, []]);
  });

  it("r3 Н3: a clean card follows the other window; a dirty card keeps its text and takes only the clean fields", () => {
    const server = new FakeSceneServer(INITIAL);
    const clean = sceneSeen(init(INITIAL), server.other("action_text", "Edited elsewhere."));
    expect([clean.draft.action_text, clean.base]).toEqual(["Edited elsewhere.", 2]);
    let dirty = input(init(INITIAL), "music_text", "none and a flute");
    dirty = sceneSeen(dirty, server.other("sound_text", "Wind."));
    expect([dirty.draft.music_text, dirty.draft.sound_text, dirty.base]).toEqual(["none and a flute", "Wind.", 1]);
  });

  it("r3 Б-2: text typed while the request is in flight survives and goes with the next save", () => {
    const server = new FakeSceneServer(INITIAL);
    let s = save(input(init(INITIAL), "music_text", "mine-v1"));
    const request = s.inflight!;
    s = input(s, "music_text", "mine-v1-v2");                          // typed while in flight
    s = received(s, request, server.patch(request.body, "us"));
    expect([s.draft.music_text, s.base, dirtyFields(s)]).toEqual(["mine-v1-v2", 2, ["music_text"]]);
    const t = roundTrip(server, save(s));
    expect(bodies(request, t.request)).toEqual([
      { expected_revision: 1, music_text: "mine-v1" },
      { expected_revision: 2, music_text: "mine-v1-v2" },
    ]);
    expect(server.scene.music_text).toBe("mine-v1-v2");
  });

  it("r4 №1: after a 409, a delayed «Сохранить моё» keeps the field the other window saved; the next PATCH carries only the new text", () => {
    const server = new FakeSceneServer(INITIAL);
    let s = input(init(INITIAL), "music_text", "mine");
    server.other("sound_text", "new remote sound");
    const first = roundTrip(server, save(s));                          // 409
    expect(first.outcome.kind).toBe("conflict");
    expect(first.state.draft.sound_text).toBe("new remote sound");    // the clean field shows the server's at once
    s = saveMine(first.state);
    const mine = s.inflight!;
    s = input(s, "action_text", "She walks to the lake. Then she sits.");   // typed while in flight
    s = received(s, mine, server.patch(mine.body, "us"));
    expect([s.base, s.draft.sound_text, dirtyFields(s)]).toEqual([3, "new remote sound", ["action_text"]]);
    const next = roundTrip(server, save(s));
    expect(bodies(first.request, mine, next.request)).toEqual([
      { expected_revision: 1, music_text: "mine" },
      { expected_revision: 2, music_text: "mine" },
      { expected_revision: 3, action_text: "She walks to the lake. Then she sits." },
    ]);
    expect([server.scene.sound_text, server.scene.music_text, server.scene.action_text])
      .toEqual(["new remote sound", "mine", "She walks to the lake. Then she sits."]);
  });

  it("r4: «Сохранить моё» sends only the owner's fields — both windows' edits end up on the server", () => {
    const server = new FakeSceneServer(INITIAL);
    const s = input(init(INITIAL), "action_text", "She walks to the lake. Then she sits.");
    server.other("sound_text", "Wind in the reeds.");
    const t = roundTrip(server, saveMine(roundTrip(server, save(s)).state));
    expect(t.request.body).toEqual({ expected_revision: 2, action_text: "She walks to the lake. Then she sits." });
    expect([server.scene.action_text, server.scene.sound_text]).toEqual(["She walks to the lake. Then she sits.", "Wind in the reeds."]);
  });

  it("r5: a second press while a request is in flight is queued, not sent — one request per card", () => {
    const server = new FakeSceneServer(INITIAL);
    let s = input(init(INITIAL), "music_text", "mine");
    server.other("sound_text", "remote sound");
    s = saveMine(roundTrip(server, save(s)).state);                     // 409 → «Сохранить моё»
    const mine = s.inflight!;
    s = saveMine(saveMine(s));                                         // pressed twice more while in flight
    expect([s.inflight, s.queued]).toEqual([mine, true]);
    s = received(s, mine, server.patch(mine.body, "us"));
    expect([s.inflight, s.queued, s.base, dirtyFields(s)]).toEqual([null, false, 3, []]);   // nothing left to send
    expect(server.scene.revision).toBe(3);
  });

  it("r5: a late answer below the base is ignored — a field edited after the send keeps its value, the base does not go back", () => {
    const server = new FakeSceneServer(INITIAL);
    let s = input(init(INITIAL), "music_text", "mine");
    server.other("sound_text", "remote sound");
    const early = save(s).inflight!;                                   // a request from the old base (rev 1)
    s = sceneSeen(s, server.scene);
    s = saveMine(received(s, early, { kind: "conflict", current: server.scene }));
    let t = roundTrip(server, s);                                      // rev 3
    t = roundTrip(server, save(input(t.state, "seed", "22")));         // rev 4
    s = input(input(t.state, "sound_text", "old sound"), "seed", "23"); // edited after everything was sent
    const stale = received(s, early, { kind: "ok", scene: { ...server.history.get(3)!, sound_text: "remote sound" } });
    expect([stale.draft.sound_text, stale.draft.seed, stale.base, dirtyFields(stale)]).toEqual(["old sound", "23", 4, ["sound_text", "seed"]]);
    expect(save(stale).inflight!.body).toEqual({ expected_revision: 4, sound_text: "old sound", seed: 23 });
  });

  it("r5 (mobile): every input counts in the conflict branch too", () => {
    const server = new FakeSceneServer(INITIAL);
    server.other("music_text", "other");
    const s = roundTrip(server, save(input(init(INITIAL), "music_text", "mine"))).state;
    expect([s.conflict, s.inputs]).toEqual([true, 1]);
    expect(input(s, "seed", "5").inputs).toBe(2);
  });
});

describe("scene draft — queue, stale snapshots, the owner's choice", () => {
  it("a press during a request goes out once the answer is in, with the fresh base and only the new field", () => {
    const server = new FakeSceneServer(INITIAL);
    let s = save(input(init(INITIAL), "music_text", "mine"));
    const first = s.inflight!;
    s = save(input(s, "seed", "7"));                                   // typed and pressed while in flight
    expect([s.inflight, s.queued]).toEqual([first, true]);
    s = received(s, first, server.patch(first.body, "us"));
    expect(s.inflight!.body).toEqual({ expected_revision: 2, seed: 7 });
    expect(s.queued).toBe(false);
  });

  it("a snapshot older than the one shown is ignored; a newer one updates the «Сервер» side of an open conflict", () => {
    const server = new FakeSceneServer(INITIAL);
    let s = input(init(INITIAL), "music_text", "mine");
    const rev2 = server.other("music_text", "other");
    const rev3 = server.other("music_text", "newer");
    s = sceneSeen(sceneSeen(s, rev3), rev2);
    expect(s.server.revision).toBe(3);
    const t = roundTrip(server, save(s)).state;                        // 409 against rev 3
    expect(conflictPairs(t)).toEqual([{ field: "music_text", server: "newer", mine: "mine" }]);
    const rev4 = server.other("music_text", "newest");
    expect(conflictPairs(sceneSeen(t, rev4))).toEqual([{ field: "music_text", server: "newest", mine: "mine" }]);
    expect(sceneSeen(t, rev4).base).toBe(1);                           // still the owner's to decide
  });

  it("«Взять с сервера» takes the newest state seen and makes the card clean at that revision", () => {
    const server = new FakeSceneServer(INITIAL);
    let s = input(init(INITIAL), "music_text", "mine");
    server.other("music_text", "other");
    s = roundTrip(server, save(s)).state;
    s = sceneSeen(s, server.other("sound_text", "later"));
    s = takeServer(s);
    expect([s.draft.music_text, s.draft.sound_text, s.base, s.conflict, dirtyFields(s)]).toEqual(["other", "later", 3, false, []]);
  });

  it("a 409 for a request whose fields the owner has since discarded is not a conflict (found by the model)", () => {
    const server = new FakeSceneServer(INITIAL);
    let s = input(init(INITIAL), "music_text", "mine");
    server.other("music_text", "other");
    s = saveMine(roundTrip(server, save(s)).state);
    const mine = s.inflight!;
    server.other("music_text", "other again");                         // moves on while «Сохранить моё» flies
    s = sceneSeen(s, server.scene);
    s = takeServer(s);                                                 // the owner gives up on «mine»
    s = received(s, mine, server.patch(mine.body, "us"));              // 409
    expect([s.conflict, dirtyFields(s), s.base, s.draft.music_text]).toEqual([false, [], 3, "other again"]);
  });

  it("after «Взять с сервера» during a request, the 200 brings every clean field to the newest state (found by the model)", () => {
    const server = new FakeSceneServer(INITIAL);
    let s = input(init(INITIAL), "sound_text", "t");
    server.other("music_text", "other");
    s = saveMine(roundTrip(server, save(s)).state);
    const mine = s.inflight!;
    s = takeServer(input(s, "sound_text", "s"));                       // typed, then discarded, while in flight
    const ok = server.patch(mine.body, "us");                          // the server took "t" meanwhile
    s = received(s, mine, ok);
    expect([s.draft.sound_text, s.base, dirtyFields(s)]).toEqual(["t", 3, []]);
  });

  it("a failed request keeps the draft dirty and drops the queued press; a stale 409 is ignored", () => {
    const server = new FakeSceneServer(INITIAL);
    let s = save(input(init(INITIAL), "music_text", "mine"));
    const request = s.inflight!;
    s = received(save(s), request, { kind: "error", message: "Сервер не ответил" });
    expect([s.error, s.inflight, s.queued, dirtyFields(s)]).toEqual(["Сервер не ответил", null, false, ["music_text"]]);
    const t = roundTrip(server, save(s)).state;                        // rev 2, base 2
    const u = received(t, request, { kind: "conflict", current: server.history.get(2)! });
    expect([u.conflict, u.base]).toEqual([false, 2]);
  });

  it("И1: a 200 with the same revision but new content (requested_s 8 → 8.1 keeps 192 frames) is what the card shows", () => {
    let s = save(input(init(INITIAL), "requested_s", "8.1"));
    const request = s.inflight!;
    s = received(s, request, { kind: "ok", scene: { ...INITIAL, requested_s: 8.1 } });   // ops.update_scene: no fingerprint change, no bump
    expect([s.draft.requested_s, s.base, s.server.requested_s, dirtyFields(s)]).toEqual(["8.1", 1, 8.1, []]);
    expect(sceneSeen(s, { ...INITIAL, requested_s: 8.1 }).draft.requested_s).toBe("8.1");
  });
});
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/screens/sceneDraft.model.test.ts` (модельный тест: seeded PRNG, случайные чередования правок, чужих сохранений, снимков и ответов — в том числе запоздалых и переставленных, — 409 и решений владельца против фейкового сервера; инварианты проверяются после каждого шага, в конце прогон «досушивается»):

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { Scene } from "@/api/types";
import { FakeSceneServer } from "@/test/sceneServer";
import { scene as makeScene } from "@/test/snapshot";
import {
  conflictPairs, dirtyFields, FIELDS, fromScene, init, input, isDirtyField, received, save, saveMine,
  sceneSeen, takeServer, type DraftState, type Field, type Outcome, type Request,
} from "./sceneDraft";

/** Model test of the draft sync: random interleavings of the owner's inputs, another window's
 *  saves, snapshots (taken and delivered out of order), answers (processed and delivered out of
 *  order, some failing), 409s and the owner's choices — against a server with state. After EVERY
 *  step the invariants below are checked; at the end the run is drained and everything the owner
 *  typed and did not discard must have reached the server. Seeds are fixed; a failure names the
 *  seed and prints the step log. `DRAFT_MODEL_RUNS=20000 npm test` for a longer campaign. */

const RUNS = Number(import.meta.env.DRAFT_MODEL_RUNS ?? 5000);
const STEPS = 80;

// Small alphabets: equal values recur, so a merge that compares values instead of counting
// edits (review r5) is exercised, not avoided.
const ALPHABET: Record<Field, string[]> = {
  action_text: ["a", "b", "c"], performance_text: ["p", "q"], sound_text: ["s", "t", "u"], music_text: ["m", "n", ""],
  requested_s: ["4", "8"], seed: ["1", "2"], mode: ["refs", "from_frame"], refs: ["", "anna", "anna,bob"],
};

function mulberry32(seed: number) {
  let a = seed >>> 0;
  return () => {
    a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0;
    let t = a;
    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}

type Sent = { request: Request; sendStep: number; outcome?: Outcome; acceptStep?: number; delivered?: boolean };

class Run {
  rnd: ReturnType<typeof mulberry32>;
  server: FakeSceneServer;
  state: DraftState;
  log: string[] = [];
  step = 0;
  /** The last value the owner typed into a field, and when; cleared by «Взять с сервера». */
  lastOwner: Partial<Record<Field, { value: string; step: number }>> = {};
  sent = new Map<number, Sent>();
  pendingAnswers: number[] = [];
  pendingSnapshots: Scene[] = [];
  deliveredRevisions = new Set<number>([1]);
  /** Highest revision whose value of the field the owner has seen — in the field or in the conflict box. */
  displayedRev = Object.fromEntries(FIELDS.map((f) => [f, 1])) as Record<Field, number>;
  prevBase = 1;
  prevServer = 1;

  constructor(seed: number) {
    this.rnd = mulberry32(seed);
    const initial = makeScene({ id: "s_1", revision: 1 });
    this.server = new FakeSceneServer(initial);
    this.state = init(initial);
  }
  pick<T>(xs: readonly T[]): T { return xs[Math.floor(this.rnd() * xs.length)]; }
  chance(p: number) { return this.rnd() < p; }
  fail(what: string): never {
    const s = this.state;
    throw new Error(`${what}\n${this.log.join("\n")}\nstate: ${JSON.stringify({ draft: s.draft, gen: s.gen, cleanGen: s.cleanGen, base: s.base, server: s.server.revision, conflict: s.conflict })}`);
  }
  ok(cond: boolean, what: () => string) { if (!cond) this.fail(what()); }

  /** A state transition of the page; afterwards a new request may be in flight. */
  page(label: string, next: DraftState) {
    this.log.push(`${this.step}: ${label}`);
    this.state = next;
    const r = next.inflight;
    if (r && !this.sent.has(r.id)) {
      this.sent.set(r.id, { request: r, sendStep: this.step });
      this.pendingAnswers.push(r.id);
      this.log.push(`${this.step}:   → PATCH ${JSON.stringify(r.body)}`);
      // invariant 4 (client side): never a revision the server has not reached
      this.ok(r.expected <= this.server.scene.revision, () => "expected_revision ahead of the server");
      // invariant 8: the body carries exactly the fields holding an unacknowledged owner's value
      for (const f of r.fields) this.ok(this.justified(f), () => `sent a field the owner did not edit: ${f}`);
      this.ok(JSON.stringify(Object.keys(r.body).filter((k) => k !== "expected_revision").sort()) === JSON.stringify([...r.fields].sort()),
        () => "body does not match the request's fields");
    }
  }

  /** The field holds an owner's value that no delivered 200 has acknowledged. */
  justified(f: Field): boolean {
    const own = this.lastOwner[f];
    if (!own) return false;
    for (const s of this.sent.values()) {
      if (s.delivered && s.outcome?.kind === "ok" && s.request.fields.includes(f) && s.sendStep > own.step) return false;
    }
    return true;
  }
  /** The server took this value of the field from this page after the owner typed it. */
  persisted(f: Field, own: { value: string; step: number }): boolean {
    return [...this.sent.values()].some((x) => x.outcome?.kind === "ok" && x.acceptStep! > own.step
      && f in x.request.body && fromScene(x.outcome.scene)[f] === own.value);
  }

  invariants() {
    const s = this.state;
    // invariant 2: base and the shown server state never go back, base never runs ahead of it
    this.ok(s.base >= this.prevBase, () => `base decreased ${this.prevBase} → ${s.base}`);
    this.ok(s.server.revision >= this.prevServer, () => "shown server state went back");
    this.ok(s.base <= s.server.revision, () => "base ahead of the shown server state");
    this.prevBase = s.base;
    this.prevServer = s.server.revision;
    // the shown server state is one the page actually received
    this.ok(this.deliveredRevisions.has(s.server.revision), () => `server state ${s.server.revision} was never delivered`);
    this.ok(s.server.revision === Math.max(...this.deliveredRevisions), () => `a newer server state (${Math.max(...this.deliveredRevisions)}) was delivered and ignored`);
    this.ok(s.server === this.server.history.get(s.server.revision), () => "shown server state is not the real one");
    const sv = fromScene(s.server);
    for (const f of FIELDS) {
      const own = this.lastOwner[f];
      // invariant 1: the owner's last value is in the draft, or the server took it after it was typed
      if (own && s.draft[f] !== own.value) {
        this.ok(this.persisted(f, own), () => `owner's ${f}=${JSON.stringify(own.value)} lost: draft has ${JSON.stringify(s.draft[f])}`);
      }
      // invariant 5: a clean field shows the newest server state; dirty ⇔ unacknowledged owner's value
      const dirty = isDirtyField(s, f);
      if (!dirty) {
        this.ok(s.draft[f] === sv[f], () => `clean ${f} shows ${JSON.stringify(s.draft[f])}, the server state shown has ${JSON.stringify(sv[f])}`);
        this.displayedRev[f] = Math.max(this.displayedRev[f], s.server.revision);
      }
      this.ok(dirty === this.justified(f), () => `${f}: dirty=${dirty} but holds an unacknowledged owner's value=${this.justified(f)}`);
    }
    // invariant 6: a conflict is only shown while the server is really ahead of the base, and
    // there is something to choose about; its «Сервер» side is the state shown
    if (s.conflict) {
      this.ok(s.server.revision > s.base, () => "conflict shown at base");
      this.ok(dirtyFields(s).length > 0, () => "conflict without a dirty field");
      for (const p of conflictPairs(s)) {
        this.ok(p.server === sv[p.field], () => "conflict pair differs from the server state shown");
        this.displayedRev[p.field] = Math.max(this.displayedRev[p.field], s.server.revision);
      }
    }
    // invariant 7: at most one request in flight
    const inFlight = this.pendingAnswers.length + [...this.sent.values()].filter((x) => x.outcome && !x.delivered).length;
    this.ok(inFlight <= 1, () => "more than one request in flight");
  }

  // ---- steps ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  input() {
    const f = this.pick(FIELDS);
    const v = this.pick(ALPHABET[f]);
    this.lastOwner[f] = { value: v, step: this.step };
    this.page(`owner types ${f}=${JSON.stringify(v)}`, input(this.state, f, v));
  }
  otherWrites() {
    const f = this.pick(FIELDS);
    const v = this.pick(ALPHABET[f]);
    const after = this.server.other(f, v);
    this.log.push(`${this.step}: other window saves ${f}=${JSON.stringify(v)} → rev ${after.revision}`);
    if (this.chance(0.7)) this.pendingSnapshots.push(this.server.scene);
  }
  process() {
    if (this.pendingAnswers.length === 0) return;
    const id = this.pendingAnswers.splice(Math.floor(this.rnd() * this.pendingAnswers.length), 1)[0];
    const entry = this.sent.get(id)!;
    const r = entry.request;
    const before = this.server.scene;
    let outcome: Outcome;
    if (this.chance(0.08)) outcome = { kind: "error", message: "Сервер не ответил" };
    else {
      outcome = this.server.patch(r.body, "us");
      // invariant 4 (server side): accepted ⇔ the expected revision was current
      this.ok((outcome.kind === "ok") === (r.expected === before.revision), () => "server took a stale expected_revision");
      if (outcome.kind === "ok") {
        entry.acceptStep = this.step;
        // invariant 3: a value another window saved is only overwritten after the owner was
        // shown it — in the field (clean) or in the conflict box («Сервер: …»)
        for (const f of r.fields) {
          const prev = fromScene(before)[f];
          if (fromScene(outcome.scene)[f] === prev) continue;
          const writer = this.server.writerOf(before.revision, f);
          if (writer.by === "other") {
            this.ok(this.displayedRev[f] >= writer.rev,
              () => `overwrote the other window's ${f}=${JSON.stringify(prev)} (rev ${writer.rev}) the owner never saw (seen up to rev ${this.displayedRev[f]})`);
          }
        }
      }
    }
    entry.outcome = outcome;
    this.log.push(`${this.step}: server answers #${id}: ${outcome.kind}${outcome.kind === "ok" ? ` rev ${outcome.scene.revision}` : outcome.kind === "conflict" ? ` current rev ${outcome.current.revision}` : ""}`);
    if (outcome.kind === "ok" && this.chance(0.7)) this.pendingSnapshots.push(this.server.scene);
  }
  deliver() {
    const ready = [...this.sent.values()].filter((x) => x.outcome && !x.delivered);
    if (ready.length === 0) return;
    const entry = this.pick(ready);
    entry.delivered = true;
    const o = entry.outcome!;
    if (o.kind === "ok") this.deliveredRevisions.add(o.scene.revision);
    if (o.kind === "conflict") this.deliveredRevisions.add(o.current.revision);
    this.page(`page receives answer #${entry.request.id} (${o.kind})`, received(this.state, entry.request, o));
  }
  snapshot() {
    this.pendingSnapshots.push(this.server.scene);
    this.log.push(`${this.step}: snapshot taken at rev ${this.server.scene.revision}`);
  }
  deliverSnapshot() {
    if (this.pendingSnapshots.length === 0) return;
    const snap = this.pendingSnapshots.splice(Math.floor(this.rnd() * this.pendingSnapshots.length), 1)[0];
    this.deliveredRevisions.add(snap.revision);
    this.page(`page receives snapshot rev ${snap.revision}`, sceneSeen(this.state, snap));
  }
  pressSave() { this.page("owner presses «Сохранить сцену»", save(this.state)); }
  pressSaveMine() { this.page("owner presses «Сохранить моё»", saveMine(this.state)); }
  pressTakeServer() {
    for (const f of FIELDS) delete this.lastOwner[f];
    this.page("owner presses «Взять с сервера»", takeServer(this.state));
  }

  run() {
    this.invariants();
    for (this.step = 1; this.step <= STEPS; this.step++) {
      const x = this.rnd();
      if (x < 0.25) this.input();
      else if (x < 0.35) this.otherWrites();
      else if (x < 0.47) this.pressSave();
      else if (x < 0.53) { if (this.state.conflict) this.pressSaveMine(); }
      else if (x < 0.56) { if (this.state.conflict) this.pressTakeServer(); }
      else if (x < 0.70) this.process();
      else if (x < 0.84) this.deliver();
      else if (x < 0.92) this.snapshot();
      else this.deliverSnapshot();
      this.invariants();
    }
    // drain: the owner keeps choosing «Сохранить моё» / saving until the card is clean
    for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
      this.step++;
      while (this.pendingAnswers.length) this.process();
      while ([...this.sent.values()].some((x) => x.outcome && !x.delivered)) this.deliver();
      while (this.pendingSnapshots.length) this.deliverSnapshot();
      this.invariants();
      if (this.state.conflict) this.pressSaveMine();
      else if (dirtyFields(this.state).length) this.pressSave();
      else break;
      this.invariants();
    }
    this.ok(dirtyFields(this.state).length === 0 && !this.state.conflict, () => "could not settle");
    for (const f of FIELDS) {
      const own = this.lastOwner[f];
      if (own) this.ok(this.persisted(f, own), () => `owner's ${f}=${JSON.stringify(own.value)} never reached the server`);
    }
  }
}

describe("scene draft model", () => {
  it(`keeps the invariants over ${RUNS} random runs of ${STEPS} steps`, () => {
    const counts = { conflicts: 0, saves: 0, errors: 0 };
    for (let seed = 1; seed <= RUNS; seed++) {
      const run = new Run(seed);
      try {
        run.run();
      } catch (err) {
        throw new Error(`seed ${seed}: ${(err as Error).message}`);
      }
      for (const s of run.sent.values()) {
        if (s.outcome?.kind === "ok") counts.saves++;
        if (s.outcome?.kind === "conflict") counts.conflicts++;
        if (s.outcome?.kind === "error") counts.errors++;
      }
    }
    console.log(`draft model: ${RUNS} runs × ${STEPS} steps — ${JSON.stringify(counts)}`);
    // the runs really exercise saves, conflicts and failures
    expect(counts.saves).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(RUNS);
    expect(counts.conflicts).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(RUNS / 4);
    expect(counts.errors).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(Math.floor(RUNS / 40));
  }, 120_000);
});
```

```ts
import { expect, test, type Locator, type Page } from "@playwright/test";

const H = 844;
const PROJECT = { id: "p_1", revision: 3, title: "Лес", frame_format: "16:9", idea_text: "девушка у озера", style_block: "", created_at: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000+00:00" };
const scene = (id: string, idx: number, over: object = {}) => ({
  id, idx, revision: 1, state_version: 1, mode: "from_frame", action_text: "She walks to the lake.",
  performance_text: "Her gaze drops, then she smiles.", sound_text: "Bare heels on wet planks.", music_text: "none",
  state_in_text: "", state_out_text: "", requested_s: 8, frames: 192, duration_s: 8, seed: 11,
  source_frame: { kind: "none", scene_id: null, asset_id: null }, refs: [], review_state: "none",
  approved_sketch_take_id: null, approved_final_take_id: null, ...over,
});

type Reply = [number, unknown];
type Route = Reply | ((body: unknown) => Reply);

/** Exact "METHOD path?query" routes (the events stream included); anything else fails the
 *  test. Every request body is logged in order. */
async function api(page: Page, routes: Record<string, Route>) {
  const log: { key: string; body: unknown }[] = [];
  await page.route("**/api/studio/**", async (route) => {
    const request = route.request();
    const url = new URL(request.url());
    const key = `${request.method()} ${url.pathname}${url.search}`;
    const raw = request.postData();
    const body = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
    log.push({ key, body });
    const hit = routes[key];
    if (!hit) throw new Error(`unexpected request: ${key}`);
    const [status, payload] = typeof hit === "function" ? hit(body) : hit;
    if (status === 204) return route.fulfill({ status });
    await route.fulfill({ status, contentType: "application/json", body: JSON.stringify(payload) });
  });
  return log;
}

/** Pinned = its bottom edge sits 12 px (the bar's padding) above the bottom of the window, and it
 *  stays there when the page is scrolled to the very end; nothing scrolls under it unreachable. */
async function expectPinned(page: Page, button: Locator) {
  await page.mouse.move(0, 0);              // a hovered primary button lifts by 1 px (AB hover)
  await expect(button).toBeVisible();
  const atTop = await button.boundingBox();
  expect(Math.round(atTop!.y + atTop!.height)).toBe(H - 12);
  await page.evaluate(() => window.scrollTo(0, document.documentElement.scrollHeight));
  const atEnd = await button.boundingBox();
  expect(Math.round(atEnd!.y + atEnd!.height)).toBe(H - 12);
  const last = await page.locator("main input, main textarea").last().boundingBox();
  const barTop = (await page.locator("[data-testid=mobile-action-bar], form[aria-label='Новый проект'] > div.fixed").first().boundingBox())!.y;
  expect(last!.y + last!.height).toBeLessThanOrEqual(barTop);
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth)).toBe(390);
  await page.evaluate(() => window.scrollTo(0, 0));
}

test("step 1: «Создать проект» is pinned", async ({ page }) => {
  await api(page, { "GET /api/studio/projects": [200, []] });
  await page.goto("/studio/");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Новый проект" }).click();
  await expectPinned(page, page.getByRole("button", { name: "Создать проект" }));
});

test("steps 2–3: the bar carries — and performs — the main action of the step in hand", async ({ page }) => {
  // a server with state for two scenes; every snapshot keeps last_seq 5, so the stream is
  // always opened at exactly ?after=5
  const scenes: Record<string, ReturnType<typeof scene>> = { s_1: scene("s_1", 0), s_2: scene("s_2", 1) };
  let conflictOnce = false;
  const patch = (id: string) => (body: unknown): Reply => {
    const { expected_revision, ...fields } = body as { expected_revision: number };
    if (conflictOnce && id === "s_1") {
      conflictOnce = false;
      scenes.s_1 = { ...scenes.s_1, music_text: "other", revision: scenes.s_1.revision + 1 };
      return [409, { error: { code: "revision_conflict", message: "x", detail: { current: scenes.s_1 } } }];
    }
    if (expected_revision !== scenes[id].revision) return [409, { error: { code: "revision_conflict", message: "x", detail: { current: scenes[id] } } }];
    scenes[id] = { ...scenes[id], ...fields, revision: scenes[id].revision + 1 };
    return [200, { scene: scenes[id], cascade: [], lint: [] }];
  };
  const snapshot = (): Reply => [200, { project: PROJECT, scenes: [scenes.s_1, scenes.s_2], cast: [], takes: [], tasks: [], last_seq: 5 }];
  const log = await api(page, {
    "GET /api/studio/projects/p_1": snapshot,
    "GET /api/studio/events?after=5": [204, null],
    "POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/lint": [200, { findings: {} }],
    "POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/cast": [201, {}],
    "PATCH /api/studio/scenes/s_1": patch("s_1"),
    "PATCH /api/studio/scenes/s_2": patch("s_2"),
    "POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/director/propose": [200, {
      proposal_id: "r_1", dropped_tags: [], durations: [{ frames: 192, duration_s: 8 }],
      scenes: [{ ...scene("x", 0), scene_id: null, seed: null, source_frame: { kind: "none" } }] }],
    "POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/director/apply": [200, { project: PROJECT, scenes: [], diff: {}, lint: {} }],
  });
  const sent = (key: string) => log.filter((r) => r.key === key).map((r) => r.body);
  const bar = page.getByTestId("mobile-action-bar");
  const music = (n: number) => page.getByRole("article", { name: `Сцена ${n}` }).getByLabel("Музыка");
  await page.goto("/studio/#/p/p_1");
  await expectPinned(page, bar.getByRole("button", { name: "Предложить сцены" }));

  // step 2 from the bar
  await page.getByLabel("Тег").fill("anna");
  await page.getByLabel("Кто это").fill("женщина");
  await expectPinned(page, bar.getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить героя" }));
  await bar.getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить героя" }).click();
  await expect(bar.getByRole("button", { name: "Предложить сцены" })).toBeVisible();
  expect(sent("POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/cast")).toEqual([
    { expected_revision: 3, tag: "anna", description_ru: "женщина", assets: [] }]);

  // two edited cards: the bar saves the one edited last, then the other
  await music(1).fill("cello");
  await music(2).fill("flute");
  await expectPinned(page, bar.getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить сцену 2" }));
  await bar.getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить сцену 2" }).click();
  await expect(bar.getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить сцену 1" })).toBeVisible();
  await bar.getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить сцену 1" }).click();
  await expect(bar.getByRole("button", { name: "Предложить сцены" })).toBeVisible();
  expect([sent("PATCH /api/studio/scenes/s_2"), sent("PATCH /api/studio/scenes/s_1")]).toEqual([
    [{ expected_revision: 1, music_text: "flute" }], [{ expected_revision: 1, music_text: "cello" }]]);

  // a conflict: the bar offers both choices and performs «Сохранить моё»
  conflictOnce = true;
  await music(1).fill("mine");
  await bar.getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить сцену 1" }).click();
  await expectPinned(page, bar.getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить моё" }));
  await expectPinned(page, bar.getByRole("button", { name: "Взять с сервера" }));
  await bar.getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить моё" }).click();
  await expect(bar.getByRole("button", { name: "Предложить сцены" })).toBeVisible();
  expect(sent("PATCH /api/studio/scenes/s_1").slice(1)).toEqual([
    { expected_revision: 2, music_text: "mine" }, { expected_revision: 3, music_text: "mine" }]);
  expect(scenes.s_1.music_text).toBe("mine");

  // «Взять с сервера» from the bar
  conflictOnce = true;
  await music(1).fill("mine again");
  await bar.getByRole("button", { name: "Сохранить сцену 1" }).click();
  await bar.getByRole("button", { name: "Взять с сервера" }).click();
  await expect(music(1)).toHaveValue("other");

  // step 3 from the bar
  await bar.getByRole("button", { name: "Предложить сцены" }).click();
  await expectPinned(page, bar.getByRole("button", { name: "Применить" }));
  await bar.getByRole("button", { name: "Применить" }).click();
  expect(sent("POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/director/apply")).toEqual([{ proposal_id: "r_1", expected_revision: 3 }]);
});
```

```ts
// Request bodies come from the server's OpenAPI (schema.d.ts, generated by `npm run gen:api`).
// Response shapes are the dicts `h3_48gb/studio/ops.py` builds; they are written out here.
import type { components } from "./schema";

export type ProjectCreate = components["schemas"]["ProjectCreate"];
export type ProjectPatch = components["schemas"]["ProjectPatch"];
export type CastCreate = components["schemas"]["CastCreate"];
export type SceneIn = components["schemas"]["SceneIn"];
export type ScenePatch = components["schemas"]["ScenePatch"];
export type ProposeRequest = components["schemas"]["ProposeRequest"];
export type ApplyRequest = components["schemas"]["ApplyRequest"];

export type Project = {
  id: string;
  revision: number;
  title: string;
  frame_format: "16:9" | "9:16";
  idea_text: string;
  style_block: string;
  created_at: string;
};
export type ProjectListItem = Project & { scenes_total: number; scenes_done: number };

export type Asset = { id: string; kind: string; sha256: string; bytes: number; mime: string; url: string };

export type CastMember = {
  id: string;
  tag: string;
  description_ru: string;
  description_en: string;
  portrait_phrase: string;
  assets: { asset_id: string; role: "image"; ord: number; url: string }[];
};

export type SourceFrame = { kind: "none" | "prev_last_frame" | "asset"; scene_id: string | null; asset_id: string | null };

export type Scene = {
  id: string;
  idx: number;
  revision: number;
  state_version: number;
  mode: "from_frame" | "refs" | "text";
  action_text: string;
  performance_text: string;
  sound_text: string;
  music_text: string;
  state_in_text: string;
  state_out_text: string;
  requested_s: number;
  frames: number;
  duration_s: number;
  seed: number;
  source_frame: SourceFrame;
  refs: string[];
  review_state: string;
  approved_sketch_take_id: string | null;
  approved_final_take_id: string | null;
};

export type Finding = { rule: string; severity: "error" | "warning" | "info"; scene_id: string; field: string | null; message: string };
export type LintMap = Record<string, Finding[]>;

export type ProposedScene = Omit<SceneIn, "scene_id">;
export type Proposal = {
  proposal_id: string;
  scenes: ProposedScene[];
  durations: { frames: number; duration_s: number }[];
  dropped_tags: string[];
};
export type ScenesResult = { project: Project; scenes: Scene[]; diff: Record<string, string[]>; lint: LintMap };
export type SceneResult = { scene: Scene; cascade: string[]; lint: Finding[] };

export type Snapshot = {
  project: Project;
  scenes: Scene[];
  cast: CastMember[];
  takes: unknown[];
  tasks: unknown[];
  last_seq: number;
};

export type ApiErrorBody = { error: { code: string; message: string; detail: Record<string, unknown> } };
```
