Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/mobileAction.tsx`:

```tsx
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

/** Phone 390 px (spec §4.4): one main button pinned to the bottom — the main action of the step
 *  the owner is in right now. Steps register their action with a priority; the bar shows the
 *  highest: a scene in conflict (4) > an edited scene (3) > a hero being filled in (2) > a
 *  proposal to apply (1) > «Предложить сцены» (0); between equals, the card that got input
 *  last (every keystroke re-registers it through `touched`). On wider screens every step shows its own inline buttons instead. */
export type MobileAction = {
  label: string;
  onClick: () => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
  priority: number;
  /** Bumped on every input; re-registering moves the action ahead of equal priorities. */
  touched?: number;
  /** A second choice shown next to the main one (the 409 conflict: «Взять с сервера»). */
  secondary?: { label: string; onClick: () => void };
};
type Registered = MobileAction & { at: number };

const PHONE_QUERY = "(max-width: 767px)";
const Ctx = createContext<((id: string, action: MobileAction | null) => void) | null>(null);

export function useIsPhone(): boolean {
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState(() => typeof matchMedia === "function" && matchMedia(PHONE_QUERY).matches);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof matchMedia !== "function") return;
    const query = matchMedia(PHONE_QUERY);
    const onChange = () => setPhone(query.matches);
    query.addEventListener("change", onChange);
    return () => query.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, []);
  return phone;
}

export function MobileActionProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [actions, setActions] = useState<Record<string, Registered>>({});
  const [clock] = useState(() => ({ now: 0 }));
  const phone = useIsPhone();
  const register = useCallback((id: string, action: MobileAction | null) => {
    setActions((all) => {
      if (action === null) {
        if (!(id in all)) return all;
        const { [id]: _gone, ...rest } = all;
        return rest;
      }
      clock.now += 1;
      return { ...all, [id]: { ...action, at: clock.now } };
    });
  }, [clock]);
  const top = useMemo(
    () => Object.values(actions).sort((a, b) => b.priority - a.priority || b.at - a.at)[0] ?? null,
    [actions],
  );
  return (
    <Ctx.Provider value={register}>
      {children}
      {phone && top && (
        <div data-testid="mobile-action-bar" className="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-20 flex gap-2 border-t border-line bg-[var(--scrim)] p-3 backdrop-blur-md">
          <Button size="lg" className="flex-1" onClick={top.onClick} disabled={top.disabled}>
            {top.label}
          </Button>
          {top.secondary && (
            <Button size="lg" variant="outline" className="flex-1" onClick={top.secondary.onClick}>
              {top.secondary.label}
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </Ctx.Provider>
  );
}

/** Register (or, with null, withdraw) this step's main action for the phone bar. */
export function useMobileAction(id: string, action: MobileAction | null) {
  const register = useContext(Ctx);
  const key = action
    ? `${action.label}|${action.priority}|${action.disabled ? 1 : 0}|${action.secondary?.label ?? ""}|${action.touched ?? 0}`
    : "";
  const latest = useLatest(action);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!register) return;
    const now = latest.current;
    register(id, now ? {
      ...now,
      onClick: () => latest.current?.onClick(),
      secondary: now.secondary && { label: now.secondary.label, onClick: () => latest.current?.secondary?.onClick() },
    } : null);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [register, id, key]);
  useEffect(() => () => register?.(id, null), [register, id]);
}

function useLatest<T>(value: T) {
  const [ref] = useState(() => ({ current: value }));
  ref.current = value;
  return ref;
}
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/screens/HeroStep.tsx`:

```tsx
import { ImagePlus } from "lucide-react";
import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { api, ApiError, upload } from "@/api/client";
import type { Asset, CastCreate, Snapshot } from "@/api/types";
import { Field } from "@/components/Field";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { useIsPhone, useMobileAction } from "@/mobileAction";

const LLM_DOWN = new Set(["llm_failed", "llm_not_configured", "gpu_busy"]);

export function HeroStep({ snap, onChanged }: { snap: Snapshot; onChanged: () => void }) {
  const [photo, setPhoto] = useState<Asset | null>(null);
  const [tag, setTag] = useState("");
  const [ru, setRu] = useState("");
  const [manual, setManual] = useState(false);
  const [en, setEn] = useState("");
  const [phrase, setPhrase] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const form = useRef<HTMLFormElement>(null);
  const phone = useIsPhone();
  const touched = Boolean(photo || tag || ru);
  useMobileAction("hero", touched
    ? { label: "Сохранить героя", onClick: () => form.current?.requestSubmit(), disabled: busy || !tag || !ru, priority: 2 }
    : null);

  async function pick(file: File | undefined) {
    if (!file) return;
    setError(null);
    try {
      setPhoto(await upload<Asset>("/assets", file, { kind: "image" }));
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Файл не загрузился");
    }
  }

  async function save(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    const body: CastCreate = {
      expected_revision: snap.project.revision,
      tag,
      description_ru: ru,
      assets: photo ? [{ asset_id: photo.id, role: "image" }] : [],
      ...(manual ? { description_en: en, portrait_phrase: phrase } : {}),
    };
    try {
      await api("POST", `/projects/${snap.project.id}/cast`, body);
      setPhoto(null); setTag(""); setRu(""); setEn(""); setPhrase(""); setManual(false);
      onChanged();
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof ApiError && LLM_DOWN.has(err.code)) {
        setManual(true);
        setError(`${err.message}. Впишите английское описание сами.`);
      } else if (err instanceof ApiError && err.code === "revision_conflict") {
        setError("Проект изменился в другом окне — данные обновлены, сохраните ещё раз");
        onChanged();
      } else {
        setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Сервер не ответил");
      }
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="hero-h" className="mt-10">
      <div className="eyebrow">Шаг 2</div>
      <h2 id="hero-h" className="h-display mt-1 text-[24px]">Герой</h2>
      <ul className="mt-4 grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
        {snap.cast.map((c) => (
          <li key={c.id} className="flex gap-4 rounded-lg border border-line p-4">
            {c.assets[0] && <img src={c.assets[0].url} alt={`@${c.tag}`} className="h-20 w-20 rounded-md object-cover" />}
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <div className="font-mono text-[12px] text-cobalt">@{c.tag}</div>
              <p className="mt-1 text-[13px]">{c.description_en}</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-[12px] text-ink3">«{c.portrait_phrase}»</p>
            </div>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      <form ref={form} onSubmit={save} aria-label="Новый герой" className="mt-4 grid gap-4 rounded-lg border border-line p-5 md:grid-cols-[160px_1fr]">
        <label className="grid aspect-square cursor-pointer place-items-center overflow-hidden rounded-md border border-dashed border-line2 bg-surface text-ink3">
          {photo ? <img src={photo.url} alt="Фото героя" className="h-full w-full object-cover" /> : <ImagePlus className="h-6 w-6" aria-hidden />}
          <input type="file" accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp" className="sr-only" aria-label="Фото героя" onChange={(e) => pick(e.target.files?.[0])} />
        </label>
        <div className="grid gap-4">
          <Field text="Тег" hint="латиницей, для @ в сценах">
            <Input value={tag} onChange={(e) => setTag(e.target.value.toLowerCase())} pattern="[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{0,31}" required />
          </Field>
          <Field text="Кто это" hint="по-русски">
            <Textarea value={ru} onChange={(e) => setRu(e.target.value)} required />
          </Field>
          {manual && (
            <>
              <Field text="Описание по-английски">
                <Textarea value={en} onChange={(e) => setEn(e.target.value)} required />
              </Field>
              <Field text="Портретная фраза" hint="до 30 слов, вставляется в каждую сцену">
                <Input value={phrase} onChange={(e) => setPhrase(e.target.value)} required />
              </Field>
            </>
          )}
          {error && <p role="alert" className="text-[13px] text-danger">{error}</p>}
          {!phone && (
            <Button type="submit" disabled={busy || !tag || !ru} className="justify-self-start">
              Сохранить героя
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
      </form>
    </section>
  );
}
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/screens/sceneDraft.ts` (синхронизация черновика — чистые функции; карточка ниже только рендерит это состояние):

```ts
import type { Scene, ScenePatch } from "@/api/types";

/** The scene card's draft against the server (spec §7 `expected_revision`, 409 `revision_conflict`).
 *
 *  Pure state + transitions; the card only renders it and runs the one request it names.
 *  «Edited» is never decided by comparing values: every input bumps the field's `gen`, and the
 *  field is dirty while `gen > cleanGen`. The invariants the model test pins:
 *   1. an owner's value never disappears from the draft unless the owner takes the server's;
 *   2. `base` (the revision the dirty fields are edited against) never decreases, nor does
 *      `server` (the newest server state shown); an answer below them is ignored;
 *   3. another window's saved value is only ever overwritten by «Сохранить моё», which goes with
 *      the revision whose values the conflict box showed;
 *   4. clean fields always show `server`; a field is dirty exactly while it holds an owner's
 *      value the server has not acknowledged;
 *   5. at most one request per card is in flight — a second press waits for the answer and goes
 *      with the base that answer sets. */

export const FIELDS = ["action_text", "performance_text", "sound_text", "music_text", "requested_s", "seed", "mode", "refs"] as const;
export type Field = (typeof FIELDS)[number];
export type Draft = {
  action_text: string; performance_text: string; sound_text: string; music_text: string;
  requested_s: string; seed: string; mode: Scene["mode"]; refs: string;
};
export type Gen = Record<Field, number>;
export type PatchBody = Partial<ScenePatch> & { expected_revision: number };
export type Request = {
  id: number;
  expected: number;
  fields: Field[];
  body: PatchBody;
  /** `gen` of every field at the moment of sending: the answer may only touch a field whose gen is still this. */
  sentGen: Gen;
  viaConflict: boolean;
};
export type Outcome =
  | { kind: "ok"; scene: Scene }
  | { kind: "conflict"; current: Scene }
  | { kind: "error"; message: string };

export type DraftState = {
  draft: Draft;
  gen: Gen;
  cleanGen: Gen;
  /** Revision the dirty fields are edited against — what a plain «Сохранить» sends. */
  base: number;
  /** Newest server state this card has seen: clean fields show it, the conflict box compares with it. */
  server: Scene;
  inflight: Request | null;
  queued: boolean;
  conflict: boolean;
  error: string | null;
  /** Every input, any branch: the phone bar picks the card that got input last. */
  inputs: number;
  nextId: number;
};

export function fromScene(s: Scene): Draft {
  return {
    action_text: s.action_text, performance_text: s.performance_text,
    sound_text: s.sound_text, music_text: s.music_text,
    requested_s: String(s.requested_s), seed: String(s.seed), mode: s.mode, refs: s.refs.join(","),
  };
}

const zeroGen = (): Gen => Object.fromEntries(FIELDS.map((f) => [f, 0])) as Gen;

export function init(scene: Scene): DraftState {
  return {
    draft: fromScene(scene), gen: zeroGen(), cleanGen: zeroGen(), base: scene.revision, server: scene,
    inflight: null, queued: false, conflict: false, error: null, inputs: 0, nextId: 1,
  };
}

export const isDirtyField = (s: DraftState, f: Field) => s.gen[f] > s.cleanGen[f];
export const dirtyFields = (s: DraftState): Field[] => FIELDS.filter((f) => isDirtyField(s, f));
export const isDirty = (s: DraftState) => dirtyFields(s).length > 0;
/** Another window moved the scene on while this card holds unsaved text (and no conflict is shown yet). */
export const isStale = (s: DraftState) => isDirty(s) && !s.conflict && s.server.revision > s.base;
/** «Сервер: … · Моё: …» for every dirty field — the server side is the newest state seen. */
export function conflictPairs(s: DraftState): { field: Field; server: string; mine: string }[] {
  const sv = fromScene(s.server);
  return dirtyFields(s).map((field) => ({ field, server: sv[field], mine: s.draft[field] }));
}

export function input(s: DraftState, field: Field, value: string): DraftState {
  return {
    ...s,
    draft: { ...s.draft, [field]: value },
    gen: { ...s.gen, [field]: s.gen[field] + 1 },
    inputs: s.inputs + 1,
  };
}

export function toBody(draft: Draft, fields: Field[], expected: number): PatchBody {
  const out: PatchBody = { expected_revision: expected };
  for (const k of fields) {
    if (k === "requested_s" || k === "seed") out[k] = Number(draft[k]);
    else if (k === "refs") out.refs = draft.refs ? draft.refs.split(",") : [];
    else if (k === "mode") out.mode = draft.mode;
    else out[k] = draft[k];
  }
  return out;
}

function start(s: DraftState, expected: number, viaConflict: boolean): DraftState {
  if (s.inflight) return { ...s, queued: true };
  const fields = dirtyFields(s);
  if (fields.length === 0) return { ...s, queued: false };
  const request: Request = {
    id: s.nextId, expected, fields, body: toBody(s.draft, fields, expected), sentGen: { ...s.gen }, viaConflict,
  };
  return { ...s, inflight: request, queued: false, error: null, nextId: s.nextId + 1 };
}

/** «Сохранить сцену»: the dirty fields against `base`. In a conflict the owner must choose instead. */
export const save = (s: DraftState): DraftState => (s.conflict ? s : start(s, s.base, false));
/** «Сохранить моё»: the dirty fields against the revision whose values the conflict box shows. */
export const saveMine = (s: DraftState): DraftState => (s.conflict ? start(s, s.server.revision, true) : s);

/** «Взять с сервера»: the owner drops every unsaved value for the newest server state. */
export function takeServer(s: DraftState): DraftState {
  return { ...s, draft: fromScene(s.server), cleanGen: { ...s.gen }, base: s.server.revision, conflict: false, queued: false, error: null };
}

/** The later-received of two states wins on an equal revision: И1 bumps the revision only when the
 *  fingerprint changes, so the same revision can carry a new `requested_s`. */
const newer = (a: Scene, b: Scene): Scene => (b.revision >= a.revision ? b : a);

/** Clean fields show the newest server state. */
function adoptClean(s: DraftState): DraftState {
  const sv = fromScene(s.server);
  const draft = { ...s.draft };
  for (const f of FIELDS) if (!isDirtyField(s, f)) draft[f] = sv[f] as never;
  return { ...s, draft };
}

/** A queued press goes out once the answer is in and no conflict asks the owner to choose. */
const flush = (s: DraftState): DraftState => (s.queued && !s.inflight && !s.conflict ? start(s, s.base, false) : s);

/** A new snapshot of the scene (SSE reload or the reload after a save). */
export function sceneSeen(s: DraftState, scene: Scene): DraftState {
  if (scene.revision < s.server.revision) return s;                         // older than what is shown
  const next = adoptClean({ ...s, server: scene });
  return isDirty(next) ? next : { ...next, base: scene.revision };
}

/** The answer to `request`. A 200 settles every field whose gen is still what was sent: it takes
 *  the server's value and is clean. A field typed into since keeps its text and stays dirty.
 *  Answers below `base` / `server` are stale and ignored. */
export function received(s: DraftState, request: Request, outcome: Outcome): DraftState {
  const inflight = s.inflight?.id === request.id ? null : s.inflight;
  s = { ...s, inflight };
  if (outcome.kind === "error") return { ...s, error: outcome.message, queued: false };
  if (outcome.kind === "conflict") {
    if (outcome.current.revision <= s.base) return flush(s);               // stale answer to an older request
    const next = adoptClean({ ...s, server: newer(s.server, outcome.current) });
    // nothing left to choose about (the owner took the server's while this was in flight)
    if (!isDirty(next)) return flush({ ...next, base: next.server.revision, conflict: false, error: null });
    return { ...next, conflict: true, queued: false, error: null };
  }
  const { scene } = outcome;
  if (scene.revision < s.base) return flush(s);
  const server = newer(s.server, scene);
  const sv = fromScene(server);
  const draft = { ...s.draft };
  const cleanGen = { ...s.cleanGen };
  for (const f of FIELDS) {
    if (s.gen[f] === request.sentGen[f]) {
      draft[f] = sv[f] as never;
      cleanGen[f] = s.gen[f];
    }
  }
  const next = adoptClean({ ...s, draft, cleanGen, server, conflict: false, error: null });
  return flush({ ...next, base: isDirty(next) ? scene.revision : server.revision });
}
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/screens/SceneCard.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { api, ApiError } from "@/api/client";
import type { Finding, Scene, SceneResult } from "@/api/types";
import { Field } from "@/components/Field";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { useIsPhone, useMobileAction } from "@/mobileAction";
import {
  conflictPairs, init, input, isDirty, isStale, received, save, saveMine, sceneSeen, takeServer,
  type Field as DraftField,
} from "./sceneDraft";

const TEXTS = [
  ["action_text", "Действие", "что происходит в кадре"],
  ["performance_text", "Эмоция и игра", "обязательно"],
  ["sound_text", "Звук действий", "материал и контакт"],
  ["music_text", "Музыка", "необязательно"],
] as const;

// «Текст» is not offered: no ComfyUI template of part 1 renders it (spec §8.2).
const MODES = [["from_frame", "От кадра"], ["refs", "Референсы"]] as const;
const MODE_LABEL = { from_frame: "От кадра", refs: "Референсы", text: "Текст" } as const;
const VARIANT = { error: "destructive", warning: "warn", info: "outline" } as const;
export const CONFLICT_MESSAGE = "Сцену изменили в другом окне. Сравните и выберите, что оставить.";
export const STALE_NOTICE = "Сцену изменили в другом окне — при сохранении покажу, что различается.";

const LABEL: Record<DraftField, string> = {
  action_text: "Действие", performance_text: "Эмоция и игра", sound_text: "Звук действий",
  music_text: "Музыка", requested_s: "Длительность, с", seed: "Сид", mode: "Режим", refs: "Кто в кадре",
};

export function FindingList({ findings }: { findings: Finding[] }) {
  if (findings.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <ul className="mt-1.5 grid gap-1">
      {findings.map((f, i) => (
        <li key={i} className="flex items-start gap-2 text-[12px] text-ink2">
          <Badge variant={VARIANT[f.severity]}>{f.rule}</Badge>
          <span>{f.message}</span>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}

export function SceneCard({ scene, castTags, findings, onSaved, onConflict, onDelete }: {
  scene: Scene;
  castTags: string[];
  findings: Finding[];
  onSaved: (r: SceneResult) => void;
  onConflict: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
}) {
  // All sync logic lives in sceneDraft.ts (pure, model-tested); the card renders the state and
  // runs the one request the state names.
  const [state, setState] = useState(() => init(scene));
  useEffect(() => setState((s) => sceneSeen(s, scene)), [scene]);

  const callbacks = useRef({ onSaved, onConflict });
  callbacks.current = { onSaved, onConflict };
  const sent = useRef(new Set<number>());
  const request = state.inflight;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!request || sent.current.has(request.id)) return;
    sent.current.add(request.id);
    api<SceneResult>("PATCH", `/scenes/${scene.id}`, request.body).then(
      (result) => {
        setState((s) => received(s, request, { kind: "ok", scene: result.scene }));
        callbacks.current.onSaved(result);
      },
      (err) => {
        if (err instanceof ApiError && err.code === "revision_conflict") {
          setState((s) => received(s, request, { kind: "conflict", current: err.detail.current as Scene }));
          callbacks.current.onConflict();
        } else {
          setState((s) => received(s, request, { kind: "error", message: err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Сервер не ответил" }));
        }
      },
    );
  }, [request, scene.id]);

  const edit = (field: DraftField, value: string) => setState((s) => input(s, field, value));
  const { draft, conflict, error } = state;
  const dirty = isDirty(state);
  const refs = draft.refs ? draft.refs.split(",") : [];

  const phone = useIsPhone();
  useMobileAction(`scene-${scene.id}`, conflict
    ? { label: "Сохранить моё", onClick: () => setState(saveMine), priority: 4, touched: state.inputs,
        secondary: { label: "Взять с сервера", onClick: () => setState(takeServer) } }
    : dirty
      ? { label: `Сохранить сцену ${scene.idx + 1}`, onClick: () => setState(save), priority: 3, touched: state.inputs }
      : null);

  const byField = (name: string) => findings.filter((f) => f.field === name);
  return (
    <article aria-label={`Сцена ${scene.idx + 1}`} className="rounded-lg border border-line p-5">
      <header className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <h3 className="h-display text-[20px]">Сцена {scene.idx + 1}</h3>
        <Badge variant="outline">{scene.frames} кадров · {scene.duration_s.toFixed(3).replace(".", ",")} с</Badge>
        {scene.source_frame.kind === "prev_last_frame" && <Badge variant="outline">от кадра сцены {scene.idx}</Badge>}
        <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label={`Удалить сцену ${scene.idx + 1}`} className="ml-auto" onClick={onDelete}>
          <Trash2 />
        </Button>
      </header>
      <div className="mt-4 grid gap-4">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <div role="group" aria-label="Режим" className="inline-flex rounded-full border border-line2 bg-surface p-0.5">
            {MODES.map(([mode, label]) => (
              <button
                key={mode}
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={draft.mode === mode}
                onClick={() => edit("mode", mode)}
                className="h-[30px] cursor-pointer rounded-full px-3.5 text-[13px] font-medium text-ink2 aria-pressed:bg-bg aria-pressed:text-ink aria-pressed:shadow-[0_2px_10px_-3px_rgba(23,71,230,.28),0_0_0_1px_var(--line)]"
              >
                {label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          {draft.mode === "text" && <Badge variant="warn">{MODE_LABEL.text}: нет шаблона в части 1</Badge>}
        </div>
        {castTags.length > 0 && (
          <div role="group" aria-label="Кто в кадре" className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold">Кто в кадре</span>
            {castTags.map((tag) => (
              <button
                key={tag}
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={refs.includes(tag)}
                onClick={() => edit("refs", (refs.includes(tag) ? refs.filter((t) => t !== tag) : [...refs, tag]).join(","))}
                className="h-[22px] cursor-pointer rounded-full border border-line px-2 font-mono text-[11px] text-ink2 aria-pressed:border-cobalt-line aria-pressed:bg-cobalt-soft aria-pressed:text-cobalt"
              >
                @{tag}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
        {TEXTS.map(([f, label, hint]) => (
          <div key={f}>
            <Field text={label} hint={hint}>
              <Textarea value={draft[f]} onChange={(e) => edit(f, e.target.value)} />
            </Field>
            <FindingList findings={byField(f)} />
          </div>
        ))}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          <div>
            <Field text="Длительность, с">
              <Input type="number" min={3} max={15} step={0.5} value={draft.requested_s} onChange={(e) => edit("requested_s", e.target.value)} />
            </Field>
            <FindingList findings={byField("requested_s")} />
          </div>
          <Field text="Сид">
            <Input type="number" min={1} value={draft.seed} onChange={(e) => edit("seed", e.target.value)} />
          </Field>
        </div>
        <FindingList findings={findings.filter((f) => f.field === "source_frame" || f.field === null)} />
        {isStale(state) && <p className="text-[13px] text-warn">{STALE_NOTICE}</p>}
        {conflict && (
          <div role="group" aria-label="Конфликт правок" className="grid gap-2 rounded-md border border-warn p-3 text-[13px]">
            <p role="alert" className="font-medium">{CONFLICT_MESSAGE}</p>
            <dl className="grid gap-1">
              {conflictPairs(state).map((pair) => (
                <div key={pair.field} data-testid={`conflict-${pair.field}`}>
                  <dt className="text-xs font-semibold">{LABEL[pair.field]}</dt>
                  <dd>{`Сервер: ${pair.server} · Моё: ${pair.mine}`}</dd>
                </div>
              ))}
            </dl>
            {!phone && (
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                <Button onClick={() => setState(saveMine)}>Сохранить моё</Button>
                <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => setState(takeServer)}>Взять с сервера</Button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
        {error && <p role="alert" className="text-[13px] text-danger">{error}</p>}
        {!phone && (
          <Button disabled={!dirty || conflict} onClick={() => setState(save)} className="justify-self-start">
            Сохранить сцену
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/screens/ScenarioStep.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Plus, Sparkles } from "lucide-react";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { api, ApiError } from "@/api/client";
import type { LintMap, Proposal, Scene, SceneIn, ScenesResult, Snapshot } from "@/api/types";
import { Field } from "@/components/Field";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { useIsPhone, useMobileAction } from "@/mobileAction";
import { SceneCard } from "./SceneCard";

const MODE_LABEL = { from_frame: "От кадра", refs: "Референсы", text: "Текст" } as const;

/** The O7 body for the current scenes: ids kept, a prev_last_frame source sent WITHOUT its old
 *  scene id, so after a delete or a reorder the server chains it to the new previous scene. */
export function toSceneIn(s: Scene): SceneIn {
  return {
    scene_id: s.id,
    mode: s.mode,
    action_text: s.action_text,
    performance_text: s.performance_text,
    sound_text: s.sound_text,
    music_text: s.music_text,
    state_in_text: s.state_in_text,
    state_out_text: s.state_out_text,
    requested_s: s.requested_s,
    seed: s.seed,
    source_frame: s.source_frame.kind === "asset"
      ? { kind: "asset", asset_id: s.source_frame.asset_id }
      : { kind: s.source_frame.kind },
    refs: s.refs,
  };
}

const seconds = (s: number) => s.toFixed(3).replace(".", ",");

export function ScenarioStep({ snap, onChanged }: { snap: Snapshot; onChanged: () => void }) {
  const pid = snap.project.id;
  const [idea, setIdea] = useState(snap.project.idea_text);
  const [count, setCount] = useState("2");
  const [proposal, setProposal] = useState<Proposal | null>(null);
  const [lint, setLint] = useState<LintMap>({});
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const castTags = snap.cast.map((c) => c.tag);

  useEffect(() => {
    api<{ findings: LintMap }>("POST", `/projects/${pid}/lint`, {}).then((r) => setLint(r.findings), () => setLint({}));
  }, [pid, snap.last_seq]);

  async function run(action: () => Promise<void>) {
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await action();
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof ApiError && err.code === "revision_conflict") onChanged();
      setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Сервер не ответил");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  const propose = () => run(async () => {
    setProposal(await api<Proposal>("POST", `/projects/${pid}/director/propose`, { idea_text: idea, scenes_count: Number(count) }));
  });
  const apply = () => run(async () => {
    await api<ScenesResult>("POST", `/projects/${pid}/director/apply`, { proposal_id: proposal!.proposal_id, expected_revision: snap.project.revision });
    setProposal(null);
    onChanged();
  });
  const replace = (scenes: SceneIn[]) => run(async () => {
    await api<ScenesResult>("PUT", `/projects/${pid}/scenes`, { expected_revision: snap.project.revision, scenes });
    onChanged();
  });
  const addScene = () => replace([
    ...snap.scenes.map(toSceneIn),
    { mode: "from_frame", requested_s: 8, source_frame: { kind: snap.scenes.length ? "prev_last_frame" : "none" }, refs: [] },
  ]);

  const phone = useIsPhone();
  useMobileAction("scenario", proposal
    ? { label: "Применить", onClick: apply, disabled: busy, priority: 1 }
    : { label: "Предложить сцены", onClick: propose, disabled: busy || !idea.trim(), priority: 0 });

  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="scenario-h" className="mt-10">
      <div className="eyebrow">Шаг 3</div>
      <h2 id="scenario-h" className="h-display mt-1 text-[24px]">Сценарий</h2>
      <div className="mt-4 grid gap-4 rounded-lg border border-line p-5">
        <Field text="Идея">
          <Textarea value={idea} onChange={(e) => setIdea(e.target.value)} />
        </Field>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
          <Field text="Сцен">
            <Input type="number" min={1} value={count} onChange={(e) => setCount(e.target.value)} className="w-24" />
          </Field>
          {!phone && (
            <Button onClick={propose} disabled={busy || !idea.trim()} size="lg">
              <Sparkles />
              Предложить сцены
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
        {error && <p role="alert" className="text-[13px] text-danger">{error}</p>}
      </div>

      {proposal && (
        <div aria-label="Предложение сценариста" role="region" className="mt-4 rounded-lg border border-cobalt-line bg-cobalt-soft p-5">
          <ol className="grid gap-3">
            {proposal.scenes.map((s, i) => (
              <li key={i} className="rounded-md bg-bg p-3 text-[13px]" aria-label={`Предложенная сцена ${i + 1}`}>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-ink3">Сцена {i + 1}</span>
                  <Badge>{MODE_LABEL[s.mode]}</Badge>
                  <Badge variant="outline">{`${proposal.durations[i].frames} кадров · ${seconds(proposal.durations[i].duration_s)} с`}</Badge>
                </div>
                <p className="mt-1">{s.action_text}</p>
                <p className="mt-1 text-ink2">{s.performance_text}</p>
                <p className="mt-1 text-ink3">{s.sound_text}</p>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ol>
          {proposal.dropped_tags.length > 0 && (
            <p className="mt-3 text-[13px] text-ink2">Сценарист назвал героев вне кастинга: {proposal.dropped_tags.map((t) => `@${t}`).join(", ")} — они не попали в сцены.</p>
          )}
          {snap.scenes.length > 0 && <p className="mt-3 text-[13px] text-warn">Текущие сцены будут заменены.</p>}
          {!phone && <Button size="lg" onClick={apply} disabled={busy} className="mt-3">Применить</Button>}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="mt-6 grid gap-4">
        {snap.scenes.map((s) => (
          <SceneCard
            key={s.id}
            scene={s}
            castTags={castTags}
            findings={lint[s.id] ?? []}
            onSaved={onChanged}
            onConflict={onChanged}
            onDelete={() => replace(snap.scenes.filter((x) => x.id !== s.id).map(toSceneIn))}
          />
        ))}
        <Button variant="outline" onClick={addScene} disabled={busy} className="justify-self-start">
          <Plus />
          Сцена
        </Button>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

Replace `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/screens/ProjectScreen.tsx` целиком:

```tsx
import { ChevronLeft } from "lucide-react";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { api, ApiError } from "@/api/client";
import type { Snapshot } from "@/api/types";
import { MobileActionProvider } from "@/mobileAction";
import { useEvents } from "@/useEvents";
import { HeroStep } from "./HeroStep";
import { ScenarioStep } from "./ScenarioStep";

const RELOAD_ON = ["project_updated", "cast_added", "scenes_replaced", "scene_updated", "proposal_applied"];

export function ProjectScreen({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const [snap, setSnap] = useState<Snapshot | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const load = useCallback(() => {
    api<Snapshot>("GET", `/projects/${id}`).then(setSnap, (err) =>
      setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Сервер не ответил"),
    );
  }, [id]);
  useEffect(load, [load]);
  useEvents(id, snap?.last_seq ?? null, RELOAD_ON, load);
  if (error) return <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1360px] px-4 pt-8 md:px-8"><p role="alert" className="text-danger">{error}</p></main>;
  if (!snap) return <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1360px] px-4 pt-8 md:px-8"><p className="text-ink3">Загрузка…</p></main>;
  const p = snap.project;
  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1360px] px-4 pb-28 pt-6 md:px-8 md:pb-16 md:pt-8">
      <a href="#/" className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[13px] text-ink2 no-underline hover:text-ink">
        <ChevronLeft className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
        Проекты
      </a>
      <div className="mt-4">
        <div className="eyebrow">Проект · {p.frame_format}</div>
        <h1 className="h-display mt-2 text-[30px] leading-[1.05] md:text-[44px]">{p.title}</h1>
      </div>
      <MobileActionProvider>
        <HeroStep snap={snap} onChanged={load} />
        <ScenarioStep snap={snap} onChanged={load} />
      </MobileActionProvider>
    </main>
  );
}
```

## Что осталось непроверенным при написании плана

- Выкладка на alex-neuro не выполнялась: `compose.studio.yaml` на сервере, `studio_tag_prev.sh` с настоящим контейнером и `check_panel_untouched.sh` против настоящей панели — шаги задач 5 и 13. Образ, тесты в образе, проверка секретов (обе ветки) и Host/Origin на работающем контейнере проверены на Маке в Docker Desktop.
- Сценарист на настоящей CAILA (`json_object`, Sonnet 5.5) не вызывался: ответ проверяется и `provider._schema_violation`, и `llm.validate_reply`; живой маршрут — проба задачи 13, шаг 5.
- Вид мастера сверен с «AB» по токенам и классам, но не глазами владельца и не скриншотами рядом с `AB/index.html`. Раскладка 390 px проверена Playwright в Chromium; Safari на iPhone (экранная клавиатура поверх панели) — только проход владельца.
- Полный старый набор (2160 `def test_`) в новом venv не прогонялся — базис снимается в задаче 1, шаг 2.
- `set -euo pipefail` в скриптах выкладки не имеет наблюдаемой мутации (нет конвейера, чья ошибка что-то меняет); ловушку `echo "$(…)"` закрывает мутация 2 задачи 5.
- L2 и L6 сужены (R21, R21a): L2 опирается на словарь звуков — источник вне словаря даст ложный warning; L6 не видит дрейф, добавляющий слова вне карточки. Оба — warning, не блокируют; словарь и правило уточняются по живым текстам сценариста.
- Генератор графов линтера перебирает все графы до 4 сцен; на больших проектах правила те же, но перебором не проверялись.

## Execution Handoff

Plan complete and saved to `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-21-studio-s0-s1.md` (revision 4). Before the first implementer is dispatched, revision 4 goes to `/codex` side by side with the spec and the three reviews (`review-codex-s0-s1.md`, `-r2.md`, `-r3.md`) (CLAUDE.md: the plan is checked by someone who did not write it). Recommended execution: **Subagent-driven** — 13 tasks with tight interfaces between them (`ops`, `app`, `schemas` grow task by task), and a surviving mutation here costs a review round, so a fresh reviewer per task pays for itself.
